Chapter 1051: So I Was A Madman Too. (6)

He had never thought about it, nor even imagined it.

The Cult—known as the Demonic Cult in the Central Plains—regarded the people living there as nothing more than filthy unbelievers and insignificant beings. To them, these people were merely lucky to have their lives prolonged when they should have already been cut off.

Because the time had not yet come, because the time promised by the Heavenly Demon had not arrived, these lowly creatures were miserably granted worthless lives.

Therefore, he had never once thought that a day would come when he would feel fear from those lowly and insignificant beings.

Paaaaaat!

Chung Myung, his eyes dyed with madness and murderous intent, charged straight at Jeok-Il.

The only emotion allowed to those who faced such tremendous speed and momentum head-on was one.

Bang!

Jeok-Il’s foot slammed into the ground with excessive force. There was no time to think. He leapt backward and waved both hands like a madman. Fierce demonic qi poured out in streams.

It was somewhat different from before. It was not to annihilate the Cult’s enemy, but rather a desperate struggle to push back the approaching one.

Naturally, it was nothing more than a struggle to desperately protect a life believed to exist solely to be offered to the Cult.

The demonic qi was as dense as black smoke rising above fierce flames. But unlike smoke that disperses when waved away, this demonic qi was a crystallization of evil that tore flesh and shattered bones upon contact with a human body. That dreadful demonic qi began to envelop Chung Myung.

However, Chung Myung did not slow down even as he saw the demonic qi trying to cover him. Instead, he stretched the sword held in his right hand straight toward the lower left, then fiercely slashed toward the upper right.

A deafening roar that seemed to tear eardrums arose.

Paaaaaaang!

A powerful single sword strike that seemed to tear apart the very space itself.

A red line blossomed in the air, clearly etched diagonally through the center of the demonic qi. The demonic qi that Jeok-Il had emitted with all his strength was split by a single sword strike. Chung Myung threw his body through the gap and surged forward. A chilling smile brightly appeared on his face as he advanced.

Before Jeok-Il could even be surprised, a chilling sensation spread across his ankle. The Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword had sliced horizontally across his ankle.

Shreek!

The sword that cleanly cut his front ankle flew again like a sharp spear, aiming at Jeok-Il’s entire body.

Shreek! Shreek! Shreek!

Jeok-Il, who was repeatedly cut on his thigh and abdomen, soon widened his eyes as he saw the sword flying toward his neck.

“Stop!”

At that moment, another of the Enforcing Guard descended at tremendous speed toward Chung Myung’s head, as if intending to split it in one blow, holding demonic qi like the claws of a monstrous bird in both hands.

Everyone thought Chung Myung had to first block the attack falling toward his head. But Chung Myung instead surged forward at twice the speed than before.

“What?”

Swaaaash!

As the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword sliced through the air and flew toward his neck without stopping, Jeok-Il panicked and blocked it with his hand.

Kakak! Kagagak!

The sword blade, colder than the biting wind of the Frozen Hell, pierced the palm filled with demonic qi, which had become several times harder than eternal steel.

‘Ugh...’

Jeok-Il’s senses were extremely sharp in the face of mortal danger. That was the problem. The heightened senses transmitted the pain fully to Jeok-Il. The sword tearing through his skin, ripping flesh, and finally embedding into his wrist bone was vivid in every detail.

The sword, imbued with spiritual qi, struggled and unraveled the resisting demonic qi bit by bit. A terrible pain like flesh being slowly sawed by a saw arose, and the sword steadily advanced toward Jeok-Il’s neck.

“Gyaaaah!”

Jeok-Il screamed in pain and pushed the sword embedded in his hand away. But the sword pierced in exactly as much as he pushed it out.

Soon, the Ammae Sword sliced through his wrist and up to his forearm.

“Kuhat!”

Jeok-Il threw away the three swords he was holding and grabbed Chung Myung’s sword embedded in his left arm with his bare hand.

Kwaddeudeuk!

His left arm was already useless. But the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword seemed insufficient and shook as if to cut off Jeok-Il’s right hand as well. Though his right hand was also in danger, Jeok-Il desperately clung to the sword.

Because in his eyes, the demonic hand of the Enforcing Guard falling toward Chung Myung’s head was clearly visible.

‘This stupid fool! You made a mistake...’

But at that moment, Jeok-Il flinched. Though he could not be unaware of the tremendous demonic power flying above his head, there was not a trace of confusion or fear on Chung Myung’s face.

He was just staring coldly and piercingly at Jeok-Il alone.

“Dieeee!”

Just as the Enforcing Guard’s hand was about to fall on Chung Myung’s crown.

Chaeaaaang!

A huge metallic sound rang out, and the falling Enforcing Guard’s hand stopped just above Chung Myung’s head.

Between the hand filled with boiling demonic qi and the defenseless head, a white sword suddenly appeared.

“Huh, when?”

The color drained from Jeok-Il’s face right then.

A young man wielding the Hero’s Sword, who had been staying behind to guard Chung Myung, suddenly flew like an arrow and blocked the attacks pouring down on Chung Myung. As if he had known this would happen from the start.

His distorted face and flushed complexion clearly showed how much qi he had consumed to close the distance in an instant.

“Kahat!”

Of course, the Enforcing Guard would not easily give up just because one attack was blocked. But just as he tried to swing his hand again in the air, another shadow rose above Baek Cheon.

The Enforcing Guard hurriedly raised his head.

At that moment, what he saw was a female swordsman flying with her back to the night sky. Her expression was terrifyingly cold.

“Samae!”

“Yes.”

Swaaaash!

Yu Yiseol’s sword sliced through the dark air, transforming into dozens of sword shadows that pierced the Enforcing Guard’s entire body.

“Kuaaaak!”

Normally, he wouldn’t have allowed such an easy attack, but the Enforcing Guard’s attention was so focused on Chung Myung and Baek Cheon. There was no way to counter Yu Yiseol who appeared from the corner at that moment.

The Enforcing Guard soon collapsed, bleeding from his entire body. Red blood spurted from the long sword-shaped wound on his face.

‘Damn bastard!’

Baek Cheon cursed inwardly and gritted his teeth.

No need for orders or discussion. The moment this madman pushed off the ground and leapt forward, it was obvious what he was aiming for. So Baek Cheon just had to do what he always did.

But... isn’t there a limit to that? If he had been just a little slower, Chung Myung’s head would have burst like a ripe watermelon.

He didn’t know whether to call it trust or recklessness, but there was no time to debate it. What he had to do now was very clear.

“Haaap!”

Baek Cheon swiftly retrieved his sword and spun on the spot, slashing. His sword aimed at the end of the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword’s hilt held by Chung Myung.

Kagaang!

The moment his sword struck the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword, Jeok-Il fell, splattering blood. Then Chung Myung naturally began to charge forward again.

Before Baek Cheon could even push off the ground, Yoon Jong and Jo Gul brushed past him and followed right behind Chung Myung.

Any disciple of Mount Hua knows this.

When the enemy outnumbers them and the situation is unfavorable, what Chung Myung does. Everyone knows without being told.

“Gul!”

“Yes, sahyung!”

Jo Gul’s spear-like swift sword poured forward. The Demonic Cult members who charged at Chung Myung with faces mixed with confusion and hatred were swept away and pushed back in an instant.

At the same time, Yoon Jong’s sword, swung with utmost smoothness, blocked all attacks pouring from all directions toward Chung Myung.

A sword that attacks to open a path.

A sword that blocks to protect.

These two contrasting swords open Chung Myung’s path and protect him.

Baek Cheon, who quickly followed, shouted loudly.

“Open the way! We’re heading to the High priest!”

In the past, when facing the Demonic Cult, Chung Myung leapt over the enemy and cut down the Enforcing Guard. Then he cut the High priest’s neck and saved everyone’s lives.

But this time it is different. The enemy is stronger than before, and the High priest is incomparably stronger than before.

Then Chung Myung should not protect them now; they must protect Chung Myung.

The blade called Chung Myung must be sent intact to the High priest’s neck without damage. Even if it means risking everything!

“Hae Yeon, mo...”

“Excuse me.”

At that moment, a man jumped over Baek Cheon’s head. Seeing the blood-red long robe fluttering above, Baek Cheon shouted reflexively.

“Jang Ilso!”

“I roughly understand the situation.”

From Jang Ilso’s gently spoken words, bright blue flames burst from both hands. Like fireballs, they engulfed the Demonic Cult members in front of Chung Myung.

As the Demonic Cult members screamed horribly, Jang Ilso kicked off the air and lightly landed right beside Chung Myung.

“Well then...”

He smiled, baring his teeth with a sinister expression.

“Shall we go?”

Paaaaat!

Chung Myung and Jang Ilso dashed forward simultaneously.

At the same time, Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, and Jo Gul followed them as if escorting.

Their appearance was like an arrow fiercely shot from a tightly drawn bowstring.

An arrow loses its meaning the moment it stops. Once they decide to break through the enemy’s center, if they fail, only one fate awaits them.

But neither Chung Myung, Jang Ilso, nor anyone following them feared or dreaded that fate that was to come. They just broke and cut down those blocking their way and charged forward again and again.

“Ooooooh!”

Hae Yeon and Namgung Do-Wei flew over Chung Myung and Jang Ilso’s heads and opened the path, each radiating authority and sword qi. The massive qi poured like shells, violently sweeping away the unprepared Demonic Cult members.

With the front blocked, the Demonic Cult members trying to infiltrate from the sides were blocked by the sword qi of Tang Soso and the Evil qi unleashed by Im So-Byeong.

“Ugh!”

“These unbeliever bastards!”

The united group of Mount Hua deeply penetrated through the dense Demonic Cult members.

The fangs of those trying to attack from behind were blocked by the calm sword strikes of Un Geom, and the Demonic Cult members persistently following had to face the red hunting dog biting and clinging like a hound.

An unstoppable momentum. Once the Mount Hua group gained momentum, they swiftly advanced, cutting through the sea of Demonic Cult members.

“Stop themmmm!”

Jeok-Il, who was flung away in a miserable state barely clinging to life, shouted with a tearing voice. It was too urgent and desperate to be called a shout from an Enforcing Guard of the Cult.

But now, he didn’t even have the slightest margin left.

‘That guy, I must never let him reach the High priest!’

He knew well this was a blasphemous thought. No matter how great that Central Plains Taoist was, his power was nothing but a worm before the High priest who reached the heavens. To fear the man’s sword reaching the High priest was a terrible blasphemy against the High priest.

But despite knowing all this, Jeok-Il could do nothing about the fear overwhelming his chest.

No. That man must not be allowed to reach the High priest!

“Stop them! Even if you risk your life, stop them at all costs!”

The dark night sky and the dark earth. A single red arrow split the world dyed entirely in black.

An incredibly sharp arrow that would pierce the heart of the descended demon.

Chapter 1052: So I Was A Madman Too. (7)

“Block them!”

The Enforcing Guards had pondered and agonized countless times.

The Central Plains—an enemy so vast that comparison seemed impossible. How could they confront and annihilate such a force?

Yet at this very moment, they faced a situation they had never once considered in all their years.

A smaller number of Central Plains people were breaking through their lines. For those who had devoted everything to the sacred Heavenly Demon, this was unimaginable.

Therefore, they could not respond instantly. How to deal with a few strong enemies was an unknown realm to them.

Swoooosh!

Chung Myung’s sword swung like lightning, instantly crushing the advancing cultists. It was a cruel and decisive strike, unbelievable for a Taoist’s fingertip attack. Before that strike, all were equal.

Seeing a comrade torn apart before one’s eyes would make even the bravest flinch. But the cultists proved with their lives why they were called the cult.

“Kahaaak!”

A Demonic cult member behind struck away his crushed comrade’s body with both arms and charged at Chung Myung. His hands, blackened with demonic qi, scratched at Chung Myung’s face at that very moment.

Clang!

The cult member’s hand was blocked by something flying, producing a sharp metallic sound. Jang Ilso, blocking the cult member’s demonic qi claw with rings densely worn on his hand, swung his arms repeatedly.

Crack!

The cult member’s head shattered into pieces, scattering blood and brains everywhere.

“Impertinent.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes darkened as he briefly expressed his thoughts. Then his gaze turned toward the tall watchtower beyond the Demonic cult members surrounding them.

At that moment, Hae Yeon and Namgung Do-Wei brushed past him and ran forward. Jang Ilso chuckled softly.

“I never thought the day would come when I’d be guarded by Shaolin and Namgung family people. Truly a rare event.”

“If you have time to chatter, then fight!”

“...Hearing such words is rare too.”

Jang Ilso shook his head as if he couldn’t help it.

‘This is madness.’

They had broken through about half of the cultists blocking them. But that also meant they stood in the middle of the enemy camp. Things seemed to be going well now, but if they stopped even for a moment?

‘They would be attacked from all sides.’

They would be walking right into the enemy’s encirclement.

At that moment, everything would end. Numerical superiority can sometimes mean nothing, but other times it’s an insurmountable absolute difference.

If the moment comes when they stop and face the cult members surrounding them, only despair would remain.

‘Even though they clearly know that.’

Those who calmly commit this madness and those who follow without question are here. That fact chilled Jang Ilso.

What if their target now was not the High priest’s neck but Jang Ilso’s? If the one waiting across was not the High priest but Jang Ilso, how would he be feeling watching them now?

“It really is rare.”

Jang Ilso raised both arms. Bracelets rose into the air simultaneously and began spinning fiercely above his head.

“Could it be that in this Central Plains...”

Jang Ilso spread both arms wide at once.

“There are madmen even crazier than me!”

Kwaaaaaaang!

The spinning bracelets shot forward simultaneously. Over a dozen golden meteors flew toward the cult members in an instant.

“Yah!”

The cult members gathered all their demonic qi to strike down the bracelets flying straight at them. Or rather, tried to strike them down. But at that moment.

Whoooosh!

The bracelets flying straight wriggled as if alive. Dodging the cult members’ swinging hands, they carved strange curves and pierced their bodies.

Creak!

The tough skin resisted momentarily, but the fiercely spinning bracelets literally ground the flesh, digging deeper inside.

“Gyaaah!”

The bracelet that pierced the chest tore apart the cult member’s insides and pierced through the back. Still undeterred, it pierced the bodies of the members charging from behind one after another.

The fiercely spinning bracelets moved freely, carrying enough force to shatter the pierced cult members’ bodies into pieces.

“Kraaaah!”

“Aaaah!”

Desperate screams erupted everywhere. It looked like ten golden snakes rampaging.

A Demonic cult member tried to grab the bracelet flying toward his chest with both hands, but the golden bracelet arced upward and pierced his face. It looked like a huge star shooting into the night sky.

“I have to keep pace too. As long as I don’t stop, it’ll work, right?”

The momentum of those charging fiercely collapsed instantly. Even the Mount Hua disciples running in sync were speechless, staring blankly at the scene before them.

Only one person did not lose sight of his task at that moment.

Swoooosh!

Chung Myung’s sword swiftly severed the throats of Demonic Cult members trembling in pain.

“Oh-ho?”

Just as Jang Ilso was about to express admiration, two shadows rushed in from Chung Myung’s left and right.

Swoooosh!

Swoooosh!

Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol, perfectly filling left and right, unleashed sword strikes clearly resembling Chung Myung’s. They were extremely concise but deadly swift swords, merciless killing blades allowing no waste.

The Cult members’ necks rose into the air one after another.

Then the two swords began to change as if by an agreement. The concise swings secured space and transformed into a terrifying phantom sword scattering countless sword qi.

Thousands of petals bloomed simultaneously at the sword tips. Red plum blossoms fiercely swept through the cult members still unprepared on what was happening, piercing their bodies. There was no way to block the sword qi filling every visible space.

And then.

“Amitabha! Taaah!”

Hae Yeon, uncharacteristically shouting fiercely, drew up all his internal qi. The formidable internal qi painstakingly cultivated in Shaolin Sect and perfected by Mount Hua began to fill his fist.

Bang!

A powerful true strike!

Wheeeeee!

Following was a roar like a huge building crumbling, as formidable power burst from Hae Yeon’s fist.

A Buddha’s fist that destroys all evil and purifies all wickedness of the world!

Hae Yeon’s Arhat Divine Fist unleashed fiercely, sweeping through the cult members.

Into the space thus created, Mount Hua disciples including Yoon Jong and Jo Gul swiftly penetrated.

“Break through!”

“Open the path!”

Their sword strikes poured forward countless times, piercing the thick cult formation instantly like sharp awls.

They didn’t even aim for heads. Their task was not to kill or topple enemies, but simply to carve a single straight path toward the watchtower!

“You damn bastards!”

The Enforcing Guards gritted their teeth and surged toward Chung Myung. It was clear who they must kill first.

Bang!

But the Mount Hua disciples and Hae Yeon, as if by agreement, scattered in all directions to block the charging Enforcing Guards.

Kaaang!

The Enforcing Guard’s hand and Mount Hua disciple’s sword clashed in midair, creating a huge metallic sound.

“Get out of the way, you filthy bastards!”

“No way!”

Jo Gul smirked mockingly at the Enforcing Guards.

“We’re desperate too!”

Jo Gul’s eyes glanced at Chung Myung running ahead.

“That step won’t stop before I die, you stupid cult bastard!”

Mount Hua’s swordsmen fiercely pushed back the impatient Enforcing Guards.

“Ugh!”

“These bastards!”

The Enforcing Guards were clearly strong. But since their hearts were elsewhere and they couldn’t give their all to the opponents before them, they couldn’t show their true strength.

While Mount Hua disciples briefly dealt with the Enforcing Guards, new faces stepped forward to fill the gaps.

“This seems a bit tough alone.”

“Ugh. Now it’s the front again, gotta handle it properly!”

Un Geom and Im So-Byeong, leaving the rear to the Red Dogs, rushed forward.

“Hoo! Hoo!”

Seeing that scene, sparks flew in Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes. Panting as if about to collapse, he gritted his teeth.

“The vanguard!”

A loud lion’s roar burst out.

“That’s my spot!”

Namgung Do-Wei, having drawn up his nearly depleted internal qi, dashed forward raising his sword.

Goooooo!

White sword qi at the tip grew endlessly. Namgung Do-Wei unleashed sword qi larger than most great swords, glaring fiercely as he swung his sword in one stroke.

“Ooooooh!”

Emperor’s Manifestation.

The Emperor Sword Form, containing the essence of Namgung, was unleashed instantly.

Kwaaaaaaang!

The white sword qi that fell in the enemy’s midst caused a massive explosion.

“Go, Taoist!”

“Go, Chung Myung!”

Hearing all the shouts, Chung Myung stomped the ground. He didn’t turn his head. No need to check those behind him; that would insult those who opened the path.

He had only one task: to run along that path and reach the end!

As Un Geom and Im So-Byeong pushed away the urgently charging cult members, Chung Myung and Jang Ilso stomped the ground simultaneously as if by agreement. The two, glowing red and black, pierced the thinning encirclement at terrifying speed.

Finally!

Bang!

Chung Myung grabbed the last blocking cultist’s head with one hand and slammed it into the ground, using the recoil to leap up. His raised Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword gleamed white.

“Tahahaaap!”

The red sword qi burst forth and instantly struck the lower floor of the watchtower.

Kwaaaaaaang!

With a deafening explosion, the already half-destroyed watchtower began to collapse instantly.

Rumble! Rumble rumble rumble!

“Ah...”

Jeok-Il’s eyes were filled with despair and fear.

Dust rose thickly from the collapsed watchtower. It was proof that Chung Myung’s sword had finally reached its destination.

Kurrurrung! Kurung!

The watchtower completely collapsed in an instant. The thick dust was slowly blown away by the wind.

And... in the midst of the ruins of the watchtower, now too ruined to recognize, a man slowly appeared.

The world was enveloped in silence.

“H-high priest...!”

Only Jeok-Il’s scream echoed like a wail.

The man who heard the cry slowly turned his head. Even through the still settling dust, his cold gaze was clearly felt.

Chung Myung bared his teeth like a wolf meeting those eyes.

“Should I say it’s good to see you?”

The Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword emitted a chilling sword cry.

Chapter 1053: So I Was A Madman Too. (8)

The two groups, who had stained the utterly devastated land of Hangzhou with blood, came to a halt in different ways.

“...High priest...”

Jeok-Il’s jaw trembled uncontrollably. Anyone unfamiliar with him might have thought he looked pitiful witnessing this scene.

His mission was to ensure that these filthy unbelievers dared not disturb the High priest’s rest. As the enforcing guard serving the High priest, it was a duty he should have fulfilled properly.

But he failed to complete that mission. Ultimately, their blades were pointed at the High priest. That fact filled Jeok-Il’s entire body with indescribable terror.

The Demonic cult members under Jeok-Il’s command were no different. They all stared dazedly at Da Ja-Kang, standing alone amid the ruins.

The disciples of Mount Hua, who had caused Jeok-Il and the cult members to commit such a grave mistake, looked at Da Ja-Kang with a different kind of gaze.

“What is this?”

Confusion flashed in Baek Cheon’s eyes.

‘That man is the High priest?’

The man standing alone was very different from the High priest Baek Cheon had imagined. The High priest he had seen in the North Sea was a being that could only be described as a monster—wearing a human mask but utterly inhuman in every sense, a massive embodiment of malice. That was the High priest Baek Cheon remembered.

But the one he saw now...

‘Different.’

Though tall and abnormally thin, giving off a strangely eerie feeling, he was not as inhuman as the High priest Baek Cheon remembered. Rather, he seemed like a wanderer one might see anywhere.

But...

Chilling.

It was only in his mind. Baek Cheon’s body was definitely sensing it.

Something immense hidden beneath that unremarkable appearance—gloomy and dark, a deep abyss so dense that just facing it felt crushing.

Baek Cheon bit his lip tightly and glanced around. The faces of those beside him were also pale and stiff. They too felt the same as Baek Cheon.

Baek Cheon’s gaze stopped on Chung Myung’s back. Even facing the High priest, his posture did not waver even slightly.

Da Ja-Kang slowly looked down at the cup in his hand.

The red-colored liquor in the cup had become murky, covered with dust. No matter how much they had trampled the Central Plains and occupied this place, those who worshipped the Heavenly Demon were not worthy to drink the sacred wine.

Da Ja-Kang silently stared at the cup for a long time, then slowly brought it to his lips and swallowed the liquor.

The original fragrant sensation that tickled his nose was gone. The scent and taste were lost, leaving only the taste of dirt and dust—a nauseating filth.

But Da Ja-Kang did not avoid the disgusting taste; he fully accepted it. After all, what they had been drinking was not much different from such filth.

Savoring the disgusting taste and scent lingering in his mouth, Da Ja-Kang slowly opened the hand holding the cup.

Clang.

The fallen cup shattered into pieces and scattered.

“How sad.”

Da Ja-Kang briefly looked at the scattered white porcelain, then shifted his gaze to the two people standing before him.

One was flamboyant like a clown, and the other a cold-eyed swordsman dressed in black martial attire.

He took in everyone behind them and the cult members looking on with terrified faces. Da Ja-Kang’s gaze remained so dark that no one could guess his intentions.

“You mean you won’t allow me even a small fragment of what you enjoy?”

Da Ja-Kang smiled softly. Indeed, from their perspective, he was merely an invader coming to take everything they enjoyed.

“H-High priest!”

At that moment, Jeok-Il screamed like a shriek and bowed deeply on the spot.

“This is not something the High priest should handle personally. I will deal with them!”

Da Ja-Kang glanced at Jeok-Il. His condition was a complete mess. The corner of Da Ja-Kang’s lips twitched slightly.

“Please forgive my incompetence and grant me a chance to make up for my mistake...”

“Stand down.”

“H-High priest.”

Da Ja-Kang’s gaze turned to Chung Myung and Jang Ilso, who stood at the front.

Different qi’s emanated from them. One was so refreshing it was almost repulsive; the other so sinister it was nauseating. Like water and oil that never mix, their qi’s were starkly opposed.

But what was reflected in both their eyes was no different.

Clear hostility.

Feeling the blatant hostility, Da Ja-Kang slowly spoke.

“Here...”

But he soon stopped. He realized he was trying to ‘converse’ with them.

Of course, he had exchanged many words with the people of the Central Plains before. But that was not ‘dialogue.’ It was only one-sided notification.

But now Da Ja-Kang was not trying to notify them. He was trying to ask and listen.

Da Ja-Kang closed his eyes slightly.

“I have become clouded.”

Now he could not deny it even if he tried. No matter what happens, he could never return to the High priest Da Ja-Kang who left the sect.

But... even so...

It was at that moment.

“A young brat.”

The black-clad swordsman at the front spoke with a crooked smile. His glaring eyes were fierce.

“How laughable.”

“...”

“You’ve never seen the Heavenly Demon, yet you lick the non-existent soles of its feet.”

If it were the Da Ja-Kang of a while ago, he would have felt indescribable rage at those words. Like the cult members who dared not unleash their boiling anger before him and forced themselves to suppress it.

But ironically, the current Da Ja-Kang did not feel much anger from hearing that. He also thought those words were not entirely wrong, even knowing it proved his broken faith.

So he became curious anew.

“I will ask one thing, swordsman.”

“I don’t think we’re close enough to exchange words like that.”

Da Ja-Kang smiled softly.

“You can call it a request.”

At those words, a slight surprise flickered in Chung Myung’s eyes.

“A hundred years...”

He suddenly realized how long that time was. He never thought he would see a High priest say such words with his own eyes.

“Speak.”

A moment of silence passed. Da Ja-Kang, silently looking at Chung Myung and Jang Ilso, slowly opened his withered lips.

“...Why do you not flee?”

“What?”

Da Ja-Kang’s indifferent gaze pierced Chung Myung.

“Someone like you must have already felt it. Probably you understand it better.”

Da Ja-Kang’s tone was definitive, as if stating a fact.

“The difference in power is clear. You cannot stop me.”

This was not a threat. At least from Da Ja-Kang’s perspective.

“Then why do you not flee? Why do you resist? Why fight a battle with a predetermined outcome?”

This is truly foolish.

Of course, he acknowledged their strength. At that level, even within the sect, it would be hard to find a match unless one was a High priest.

But precisely because of that, the moment they face him, they would clearly understand that they have no chance of winning.

“Still, why do they show such unwavering eyes?”

Why can they hold their path so firmly even before an overwhelming existence?

Though a meaningless question, Da Ja-Kang could not help but ask.

But the answer was somewhat different from what Da Ja-Kang expected. What returned was clear mockery.

“What stupid questions you ask. Cult bastards living off dirt in the borderlands, now you think you’re philosophers?”

Such rude and uncivil emotions to show the High priest. Chung Myung laughed aloud and instead asked Da Ja-Kang.

“So? Because the enemy is strong, should we bow our heads and submit to them?”

“...”

“Listen well, you stupid bastard. Cowards like you tremble before a non-existent Heavenly Demon out of fear of death, but I’d rather die than do that.”

“You mean you’re not afraid of death?”

“Of course I’m scared, you stupid bastard.”

Chung Myung laughed as if exasperated.

“But surrendering is worse than dying. Especially for bastards like you.”

Just as Da Ja-Kang was about to speak, a faint nasal sound pierced his ear.

“Hmmm.”

Da Ja-Kang’s gaze turned to Jang Ilso beside Chung Myung.

His entirely white face and blood-red lips curved chillingly.

“...You kept saying High priest, High priest. I wondered how great a man he was.”

Jang Ilso paused briefly and exaggeratedly sighed.

“This is a bit disappointing.”

Da Ja-Kang’s eyebrows twitched uncomfortably.

“Let me tell you one thing.”

Jang Ilso spoke in an extremely sinister voice. The malice on his face made even the mighty Da Ja-Kang frown.

“The moment you admit that someone exists above you, you are finished.”

“...”

“That’s the difference between the ruler and the ruled. And I am not afraid of the ruled. No matter how strong, they are the same in the end—humans who choose to submit to others.”

Clack!

Jang Ilso’s rings rubbed harshly.

“So, what kind of man are you?”

Listening to Jang Ilso, Da Ja-Kang quietly smiled.

“A man who does not submit...”

He slowly shook his head.

“You don’t know.”

“...”

“You don’t know either. The answer I want is not with you. No... perhaps no one in the world can give it.”

A hollow voice escaped Da Ja-Kang’s lips.

“I do not submit. I overcome. I resist.”

The longer he muttered, the more his face twisted.

“Those are words only spoken by those who have never encountered true terror, true greatness, true divinity.”

Black demonic qi bubbled out from beneath Da Ja-Kang’s feet. So dense it seemed otherworldly, darkness began to coil around his feet.

“If you still intend to spout such words...”

Whoooooosh!

The demonic qi Da Ja-Kang released spun faster and faster, forming a massive swirling vortex that writhed fiercely.

“I will show you.”

Da Ja-Kang’s voice grew rougher. His eyes emitted a fiercely deep crimson glow.

“What true despair is!”

Kraaaaash!

The black demonic qi soared into the sky like a black dragon.

Finally, Da Ja-Kang, throwing himself into madness, let out a howl like a demon from hell. It was an overwhelming and shocking sight.

Before the terrifying feast of demonic qi, the world began to be dyed with fear.

Chapter 1054: So I Was A Madman Too. (9)

Kwaaaaaaah!

A storm of black demonic qi raged violently. The rough ground was torn and lifted by the fierce wind, trees uprooted and caught in the tempest. The storm, swallowing debris from broken buildings and choking dust, grew larger, sweeping everything around.

Facing that astonishing sight, even Baek Cheon’s eyes filled with uncontrollable fear.

“Th-this is...”

It was literally a storm—something no human could create. No, something humans must never create!

Kwaaaaaaaah!

The ominous black currents swept everything away, crushing and tearing the world apart.

The demonic qi pressed down on his entire body, choking his breath. It seemed to forbid even a single breath or the twitch of a finger.

Before that overwhelming power, Baek Cheon’s existence was utterly insignificant.

“This is the true presence of a High priest...”

He understood. He had no choice but to understand.

Why the martial world still despised the name High priest, why even mentioning it was taboo. Why the sects deeply buried the cursed name of the Demonic cult and refused even to think about it.

And he also understood why Chung Myung said that the High priest of the Northern Sea was only half the story.

The true High priest tore the ground with his demonic qi and made the heavens wail. Unlike anything the martial arts realm had imagined, he could only be described as purely destructive.

...His spirit felt like it would break.

Even Baek Cheon, who had overcome countless crises with Chung Myung’s sword, felt the urge to give up everything the moment he faced this High priest.

No matter the situation, he had always believed his spirit would not break. But now he painfully realized how arrogant that thought was.

Before that overwhelming power that seemed to engulf the entire world, how powerless was human will?

“Ugh...”

“Uh... ugh...”

Even the Red Dogs were retreating. The hunting dogs who would accept being torn apart laughingly at Jang Ilso’s command were stepping back before their master. No, it was more accurate to say they were fleeing, only lacking the courage to turn their backs on the High priest.

They did not fear death. But surely, there are things in this world more terrifying than death?

Though the Red Dogs’ notorious reputation seemed meaningless in this weak state, Baek Cheon could not mock them for their thoughts. No, he couldn’t. He too was exhausting all his mental strength to keep his faltering feet planted firmly.

Gnash.

His tightly bitten lower lip split, bleeding. The swirling wind scattered the blood droplets into the air.

“Ugh...”

Nevertheless, Baek Cheon endured.

Because he had faced it once before. He knew to some extent how inhuman these High priests were.

But for those encountering a High priest for the first time in their lives, the scene before their eyes was even more shocking. It was a disaster that could not be fully described by the word despair.

“Ah...”

Namgung Do-Wei involuntarily stepped back. His eyes trembled as if struck by lightning, lost and uncertain.

“How...”

How could something like that exist in this world?

He was the eldest son of the Namgung Family and destined to become its head. Thus, he was very familiar with the existence of powerful rulers of the world. But this High priest was on a different level from any strong person he had known.

“How...”

Different. From the heads of the Five Great Families ruling the world. From the great sect leaders of the Nine Great Sects One Union governing the world.

Even the previous absolute rulers who had turned their backs on the world with even greater power did not possess such immense force. It was that despairing.

Naturally, one thought arose.

That is not something a human can handle. That is...

“Ugh...”

Someone grabbed Namgung Do-Wei’s shoulders from both sides as he was unknowingly stepping back. Startled, he turned and muttered.

“T-taoist, Yoon Jong... Taoist Jo Gul...”

Jo Geol and Yoon Jong were gripping his shoulders painfully, preventing him from retreating any further.

“...Do not retreat, young lord.”

Yoon Jong bit his lip until it bled and spoke.

“Endure this.”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes wavered at those firm words.

Endure? How? How could they say that after seeing all this? If they truly saw the same sight, how could they utter such words?

‘Aren’t you all afraid?’

Even after seeing that? Even now, Namgung Do-Wei felt his mind breaking before his body. His soul screamed beyond his will. How was he supposed to endure this?

Or was he being told to silently stand firm here and accept the approaching death as inevitable?

‘I can’t!’

Just as Namgung Do-Wei was about to shout with bloodshot eyes, he suddenly closed his mouth. What calmed him was the slight trembling he felt from Yoon Jong’s hand gripping his shoulder.

Namgung Do-Wei flinched and looked at Yoon Jong.

‘Trembling?’

His gaze slowly dropped. Yoon Jong’s legs were trembling as if he was about to buckle.

A suppressed groan escaped Namgung Do-Wei’s lips.

‘How...’

They were not unafraid of this. Nor were they stubbornly defiant.

They were trembling.

It was natural when you think about it. They were human too; how could they not be afraid of that thing? Like Namgung Do-Wei, or perhaps even more terrified.

Yet they did not step back even a single step, enduring the presence of the High priest.

“Don’t step back...”

Grit.

Yoon Jong bit his lip hard as he spoke.

“...Do not leave behind the memory of fleeing, young lord... For your own sake!”

Namgung Do-Wei’s jaw trembled violently at those words.

Forcing his gaze away, he saw Chung Myung standing at the front.

Even Namgung Do-Wei standing behind felt his will twisting. Then how immense was the burden Chung Myung bore facing the High priest head-on?

‘Taoist...’

Clenching his fist tightly, Namgung Do-Wei’s nails dug into his palm.

Crunch.

Grinding his teeth as if they might break, he firmly planted his feet on the ground.

Courage to fight back? Still none. If that demon charged at him, Namgung Do-Wei’s will might collapse instantly.

But...

‘No matter how weak I am.’

With someone holding the line ahead like that, how could he possibly turn and flee?

With bloodshot eyes, he gripped his sword as if to break it.

“Even if I can’t help...”

A voice filled with blood and sorrow escaped Namgung Do-Wei’s throat.

“...At least, I will die together in here.”

Only then did the hands holding his shoulders lose their grip. But Namgung Do-Wei didn’t notice, staring intently at Chung Myung’s back.

‘Taoist, please!’

That gaze was filled with utmost desperation. It resembled the look someone from the distant past had sent to his back. That look pushed Chung Myung forward.

Kwaaaaaaaah!

The fierce demonic qi swept around at an incredible speed, tearing skin and shaking the insides just by contact.

Facing the explosively spreading demonic qi head-on, Chung Myung bared his teeth.

“So...”

A pointless chuckle escaped.

‘I actually dealt with things like this?’

Every time his fingertips ached as if crushed, a hollow laugh came out naturally.

“Damn it...”

A chuckling voice slipped through Chung Myung’s crooked, mocking lips.

“I was a madman too.”

It was demonic qi he had faced countless times. But they say perspective changes from what you see. The High priest he had looked down on from a lofty position in the past and the High priest he now looked up at from a much lower place were worlds apart.

He felt overwhelmingly despairing power. At the same time, determination began to boil in Chung Myung’s eyes.

‘The difference in power...’

He bared his teeth like a snarling beast.

“A mere High priest bastard talking arrogantly.”

A thick killing intent sharpened Chung Myung’s momentum. Then, a voice muttered beside him.

“...Creepy.”

Jang Ilso’s long cloak fluttered wildly in the blowing wind. But Jang Ilso stood unmoved, facing the surging demonic qi head-on.

He quietly raised his hand and wiped his face.

“The world is indeed vast. To think such guys obviously existed.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“Why? Are you scared?”

“Scared?”

Jang Ilso turned his head to Chung Myung standing beside him. His long eyes curved as if amused.

“You naughty kid. You should stop saying things you don’t mean.”

“That’s not something you should say, madman.”

“Hehehehe.”

Jang Ilso laughed cheerfully. Even so, his eyes flashed a deep blue light. Despite facing an absolute enemy right before him, his mischievousness did not fade at all.

“This is definitely outside my calculations. And I don’t like variables.”

His bright smile was filled with a sinister aura.

“If a variable that can’t be used arises, it must be removed. No matter what it is.”

“...I don’t particularly want to agree with you, but.”

Chung Myung twisted his sword to hold it in reverse grip and chuckled.

“I agree with that opinion.”

Jang Ilso’s mischievous glowing eyes and Chung Myung’s cold, boiling gaze crossed in the air.

“Then let’s find out.”

Jang Ilso slowly lowered his hand and spoke. Then, spreading both hands, he calmly stepped toward the terrifyingly raging demonic energy.

“Let’s see how sharp the fine sword I prepared really is.”

“Don’t get in my way, you idiot bastard.”

Chung Myung, sword lowered, also stepped forward.

The two, one black and one red, keeping a distance, advanced toward the black storm that seemed to devour the world.

Though they were opposites who could never walk the same path, at this moment their gazes were fixed on the same place.

Kagagak!

Kalararak!

At the moment Chung Myung’s sword scraped the ground and Jang Ilso’s rings clashed, the sounds echoed simultaneously.

Kwaaaang!

As if by agreement, the two pushed off the ground and plunged at once into the raging storm of demonic qi.

Chung Myung’s sword, emitting a fierce Purple Cloud Divine Skill, radiated a red sunset glow. Meanwhile, Jang Ilso’s hands, drawing up the full power of Slaughtering Blue Flame, blazed blue.

The two streams of red and blue light surged toward the darkest place in the world.

Chapter 1055: So I Was A Madman Too. (10)

Kwaaah!

The trembling rock, unable to withstand the tremendous wind pressure, shot up into the sky. It’s rare to see a rock larger than a person soaring through the air, but an even more spectacular sight followed.

Kwa-ga-ga-gak!

The moment the black demonic qi storm swallowed the rock, the solid stone shattered instantly into pieces and crumbled.

The swirling black demonic qi—no, it should rightly be called demonic enhance—not only broke the unyieldingly hard rock but crushed it into dust in an instant.

If the rock suffered that fate, what chance would a human body have? To leap into that storm was nothing less than an act of suicide. No sane person would ever choose that.

Yet, here in this place, there were two such insane humans.

Paaaah!

With his red long robe fluttering, Jang Il-so charged forward, a chilling murderous aura blazing from his eyes. At that moment, thousands of black demonic enhanced blades poured toward him like countless knives.

Kkiaaaaaak!

The sound of the demonic enhance tearing through the air echoed like a haunting wail.

“Hmph!”

Jang Il-so’s unique enhanced qi technique, Slaughtering Blue Flame Enhanced, flared fiercely as blue flames erupted from both his hands.

The blazing blue flames collided head-on with the incoming black demonic enhance.

Kwaaaaang!

Demonic Enhance and Evil Enhance!

At the moment the ultimate qi of Demon and Evil clashed, terrifying killing intent and qi explosively spread in all directions.

Ka-ga-ga-gak! Ka-ga-ga-gak!

As the pain of hundreds of daggers seemed to carve his hands, a chilling killing intent appeared on Jang Il-so’s face.

Kwang!

He stomped the ground again and charged forward even more fiercely, thrusting both hands ahead. The blue flames blossoming from his body licked like a demon’s tongue, wrapping around the flying demonic enhance.

Ka-ga-ga-gak!

It was a scene like legendary swords clashing fiercely in midair, unmatched anywhere in the world. Jang Il-so’s qi, forged by refining his innate killing intent to the extreme, even twisted and broke Dan Ja-Kang’s demonic qi.

The twisted demonic enhance that flew in instantly shredded Jang Il-so’s fluttering splendid robe to tatters. Yet Jang Il-so didn’t even glance aside and pressed forward.

His face, smiling pure white, evoked not just eeriness but even a sense of ruthlessness.

Kwa-ga-ga-ga-gak!

Jang Il-so thrust his hand into the demonic qi surging and suddenly spread both arms wide, tearing apart the demonic steel that swept him and creating a large space straight ahead.

It was truly just a brief gap. Tearing the demonic qi was indeed a great feat, but the swirling qi would fill that gap in an instant.

But behind Jang Il-so, there was someone who did not miss even that fleeting moment.

Paaaah!

Chung Myung threw his body like a flash of light through the gap Jang Il-so created and raised his sword high into the sky.

Kwaaaaaah!

The fiercely flying demonic steel strands were harder than steel and sharper than a fine sword. The fragile body of Chung Myung seemed like it wouldn’t leave a trace in an instant.

Yet even within that storm, Chung Myung’s sword traced an endlessly smooth circle.

From top to bottom.

What the sword tip drew was just a single semicircle movement. Nothing special, nothing extraordinary. That semicircle was as natural as a flowing river.

The red line blossomed like the sun hanging at the red-tinted horizon, smoothly slicing through the black swirling demonic qi storm.

Chwaaaaaak!

The demonic enhance, which should never be cut, split like a silk curtain sliced by a single sword.

Through the space torn open, Chung Myung’s and Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes met briefly. Seeing Chung Myung’s smile, a fierce anger instantly filled Dan Ja-Kang’s face.

Dan Ja-Kang had fully expected they would break through the demonic enhance. They too were warriors difficult to match in this murim world.

But even if it was possible, the process should not be so simple. His demonic enhance was a blessing bestowed by the Heavenly Demon to the Cult. It was a blade to make the unbelievers who learned martial arts pay the price for their disbelief.

Yet those unbelievers dared to tarnish the blessing given by the Heavenly Demon?

Kwang!

Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes emitted streams of blood-red light.

Even if forbidden doubts existed within him, he was still one who devoted everything to the Heavenly Demon. He was not so treacherous as to stand by while the sacredness of the Heavenly Demon was defiled by filthy unbelievers!

“How dare you!”

He stretched his hand forward.

The repulsive force created by his fingertips momentarily pushed back Chung Myung and Jang Il-so, who tried to leap into the torn space.

“Tear apart!”

At the same time, the spinning demonic enhance emitted a chilling wail and began to surge violently.

Kwa-ga-ga-ga-gak!

The speeding demonic enhance scraped the land and tore the air. The black storm instantly engulfed the momentarily hesitating Chung Myung and Jang Il-so.

Dan Ja-Kang clenched his fist tightly. He felt a definite sensation at his fingertips.

How dare these mere mortals open their mouths against him?

But at that very moment.

Hwaaaaak!

Something fiercely blossomed.

At first, it looked just like red blood. If blood gushed from a shattered body, it would take exactly that form.

But soon it became clear it was not human blood. It did not scatter.

The sword qi, dyed red beyond sunset hues like blood itself, bloomed like a phantom within the black demonic qi storm. The scattering red sword qi looked as if a giant brush had splattered it across the void.

Even Dan Ja-Kang was momentarily stunned watching that scene.

‘Flowers...?’

Like plum blossoms dyed red across the whole mountain, scattered by a wind from somewhere, they dyed the entire world red.

The plum blossom petals fiercely blocked the flying demonic enhance.

That sight looked like a futile resistance. Fragile petals would just be torn apart and swept away by that fierce storm.

Kwa-ga-ga-ga-gak!

As if to prove that prediction right, the demonic enhance crushed the blossoming petals in an instant. Thousands of petals shattered and flickered out.

But they bloomed again.

Each one was so fragile, utterly insignificant compared to the malevolent demonic enhance.

Yet the petals gathered. As if fully aware of their weakness, they united in one place to resist the overwhelming pressure.

Originally, martial arts existed so the weak could resist those born strong. In other words, filling what is lacking is the essence of martial arts, isn’t it?

Hundreds, thousands of petals bloomed again simultaneously, blocking the demonic enhance. A huge wall made of petals and the rushing black storm crashing against it. It was a sight that seemed impossible in this world.

Wuuuuuuuuuuung!

The fierce storm struck the wall of petals. Though the petals seemed about to be pushed back, they staggered and ultimately surged forward again, twisting the demonic qi storm.

As the demonic qi began to shatter, Dan Ja-Kang’s face hardened.

‘Being pushed back?’

It was incomprehensible.

Of course, he knew how great that swordsmanship was. It was the pinnacle of phantom swordsmanship that forced a complete reassessment of that swordsman. Rather than flashy, it was more fitting to call it ruthless.

But no matter what, it made no sense that his demonic qi couldn’t trample such a mere amount of qi.

‘Scattered? My demonic qi?’

Dan Ja-Kang’s face twisted fiercely.

The moment his demonic qi touched those red petals, it withered like fire doused with water. It wasn’t losing in strength; it felt like a fight he could never win from the start.

“Opposition...?”

That qi seemed to suppress the demonic qi. But that too made no sense.

It was none other than Dan Ja-Kang’s demonic qi.

Of course, there is always compatibility in the world. But before overwhelming power differences, even compatibility becomes meaningless.

Water opposes fire by extinguishing it. But a great blaze can evaporate water instead.

But... his demonic qi can’t function against such mere qi?

‘What on earth?’

Just as Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes filled with doubt.

In the center of the fierce clash between red and black qi, a streak of blue flame rose. Though it was fire, it was colder than the northern sea’s chill wind and sharper than a blade. The blue flame mercilessly engulfed Dan Ja-Kang’s demonic qi that the petals had been blocking.

Kwaaaaaaaang!

A deafening sound of heaven’s collapse erupted.

The tremendous aftermath of the clash of immense qi forced even the mighty Dan Ja-Kang to take a step back.

His entire body’s demonic qi began to churn wildly. A pain he had never felt since becoming a High priest came upon him.

That familiar yet strange sensation momentarily distracted Dan Ja-Kang. He lost track of the two presences.

“Ugh!”

Realizing his mistake immediately, Dan Ja-Kang quickly summoned his demonic qi. But before it could manifest, a white sword suddenly appeared before his face.

Dan Ja-Kang widened his eyes and reached out like lightning. As he blocked the flying sword, a loud metallic clang echoed.

Ka-gaaaang!

The white sword pierced Dan Ja-Kang’s palm.

Chung Myung’s blazing eyes and Dan Ja-Kang’s furious gaze clashed fiercely in the air as Chung Myung struck down with his sword.

“Th-this...!”

Just as Dan Ja-Kang was about to strike Chung Myung with raging fury, a golden meteor flew toward his face.

Kwang!

Dan Ja-Kang’s body was pushed far back, leaving a long mark on the ground. Without even glancing at the two, he looked down at his hand.

Splat.

Blood trickled down his deeply cut palm and fell to the floor. A faint scent of blood brushed his nose. A burning pain spread through his palm.

After a moment of staring at his hand, Dan Ja-Kang slowly raised his head and stared at the two standing before him.

“Well, well. It seems the great High priest is flustered.”

Jang Ilso wiped his mouth with his sleeve. His pale skin was stained with red blood.

A streak of blood also flowed from Chung Myung’s mouth.

“Why? Seems like no one in the Demonic Cult fought properly today?”

Chung Myung sneered, baring his teeth fiercely.

“But don’t be too disheartened. You’ll soon find out.”

Chung Myung raised his sword, aiming at the High priest’s neck.

“What it really means to fight with your life on the line.”

Dan Ja-Kang clenched his fist as if gripping the wound. A fierce light began to explode from his eyes again.

“You damn mayflies!”

Roaring, Dan Ja-Kang looked no different from a wounded beast.

“I will tear you all to pieces!”

“Oh?”

Seeing that, a strange madness flashed in Chung Myung’s eyes.

“Now... this is the High priest I know! Hahaha!”

Chung Myung gripped his sword backward, burst into a loud mad laugh, and shot toward Dan Ja-Kang.

Chapter 1056: Your Voice Can’t Reach Me. (1)

He wanted to ask. To those who always spout the obvious phrase that you must do your best against any enemy, he wanted to show them this scene and ask: do you really have the confidence that your fighting spirit won’t break even after witnessing this sight?

Namgung Do-Wei stared, mesmerized by the swirling demonic qi, the red plum blossom petals, and the soaring blue flames.

‘Is this really a fight between humans?’

Rather than a clash of warriors, it looked more like a scene from a myth.

As the heir of the Namgung Family, he could understand all this—he had no choice but to grasp it intellectually. Since childhood, his father had told him countless times that martial arts are similar from beginning to end, but once a certain level is surpassed, they transform into a completely different form.

However... Namgung Do-Wei was now certain that even Namgung Hwang, who had told him those words, would not have reacted much differently upon seeing this scene.

Kwaaahhh!

The sound of qi colliding and exploding repeatedly struck his eardrums. Without internal qi protecting his body, his eardrums would surely have been torn.

He used the Namgung Family’s sword style, so in terms of power, he was confident he was not inferior to any martial artist in the world. Yet, the pride he had carried all his life shattered instantly before this sight.

Above all, what troubled Namgung Do-Wei was...

‘How can they charge right into that?’

It was the actions of Chung Myung and Jang Ilso who plunged without hesitation into that terrible and dreadful storm of demonic qi. This torment had followed him since arriving at Hangzhou.

Is that something anyone can do just by being strong? Absolutely not. The desire to survive is something everyone has, strong or weak. It’s an instinct born with us. So to run into that hellish place, which makes your spine chill and knees buckle just by watching, requires something beyond mere strength.

It was at that very moment.

Whoooosh!

The demonic qi that had been endlessly growing and swirling began to shatter, covered by red flowers and blue flames. Then, in an instant, it vanished.

“Ah!”

Namgung Do-Wei saw it.

Chung Myung rushing at Dan Ja-Kang with terrifying speed.

Kaaahhh!

The sword swung upward collided head-on with Dan Ja-Kang’s hand.

Kagak! Kagagak!

A harsh, grating sound rang continuously, and fragments of demonic qi and sword qi scattered like sparks everywhere.

Dan Ja-Kang’s face twisted. Just as he tried to grasp the sword that touched his palm, Chung Myung’s sword suddenly withdrew as if it were an illusion. Then it transformed into dozens of sword shadows and poured over Dan Ja-Kang’s entire body.

Dan Ja-Kang’s hand also split into dozens in response.

Shaking only the sword tip to create changes versus swinging the hand directly—there’s no need to ponder which is easier. Still, Dan Ja-Kang’s hand was following Chung Myung’s created changes without much difficulty.

But that was only for a moment.

Paaaah!

The split sword tips of Chung Myung trembled simultaneously, then divided into thousands of petals and attacked Dan Ja-Kang.

Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes filled with shock.

‘Here?’

He couldn’t block it. No one could stop such a torrent, especially not with just their hands.

Dan Ja-Kang withdrew his outstretched hand and let out a loud shout. At the same time, the demonic qi emanating from his body suppressed and knocked down the fiercely spinning petals around him.

‘Each one is not strong!’

Dan Ja-Kang poured more internal qi into his demonic qi.

Paaaah!

But then, through the spinning demonic qi, a pure white sword blade suddenly appeared.

‘What?’

Even the mighty Dan Ja-Kang was startled by this unexpected attack. He hastily raised his hand and barely blocked it. But that was all.

“Ugh!”

Dan Ja-Kang was pushed back little by little in that state.

Chung Myung gave him no chance to catch his breath. When Dan Ja-Kang reflexively shot out his energy, Chung Myung lowered his body flat to dodge and flew just above the ground like a swallow. Then he clung tightly to Dan Ja-Kang.

“You!”

Kwaaahhh!

The demonic qi Dan Ja-Kang had just unleashed was fired powerfully again toward Chung Myung. It was an attack filled with certainty that no one charging at that speed could evade it.

But then Chung Myung threw the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword to the ground.

The sword, which had bent as if about to break, snapped back resiliently, and using that rebound, Chung Myung leapt high into the air.

Kwaaahhh!

Dan Ja-Kang’s demonic qi shattered only the innocent ground miserably. Before the echo of that roar ended, dozens of sword auras burst forth from Chung Myung’s sword tip.

Blood-red sword auras flew fiercely toward Dan Ja-Kang, as if intending to pierce his body instantly.

‘Careless.’

A bleak light flashed in Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes.

That such a predictable attack would work...

But then, behind him, a huge cracking sound arose.

‘What?’

He hurriedly turned and clearly saw ten golden bracelets flying toward him at terrifying speed.

Kwaaahhhhh!

The ground overturned and soared high, and the forcibly lifted earth soon poured down like an avalanche.

Whoooosh.

The bracelets returned to Jang Ilso with a clear rustling sound, settling back on his wrists.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso snorted as if intrigued. But his eyes were dark and sunken.

“At this point, I’m curious what exactly that body is made of.”

Dan Ja-Kang appeared amidst the fallen earth. Despite being caught in such a massive explosion, there wasn’t a single visible wound.

But his gaze was clearly different from when the battle began.

‘Is this the Central Plains?’

Dan Ja-Kang’s gaze fixed on one side.

‘Especially that swordsman.’

Chung Myung was catching his rough breath, watching this way. The sword techniques Chung Myung had just displayed still vividly flashed in Dan Ja-Kang’s mind.

To those with little understanding of martial arts, it might have seemed like just a rapid chain attack. But within it was a hidden, incredible secret of advancement.

From Strength to Change. From Change to Illusion. Then to Speed.

In barely the time of a single breath, four different secrets were unleashed.

It’s not hard for a swift sword user to strike dozens of swift sword attacks. But for that user to momentarily unfold a sword technique with strength and then create numerous changes again is nearly impossible.

No, most swordsmen can’t even reach that peak by honing just one style their whole life. But that swordsman was doing that impossible feat effortlessly.

The frustration felt when the opponent’s sword style changes instantly is unimaginable to anyone who hasn’t experienced it.

And... Dan Ja-Kang’s gaze now turned to Jang Ilso.

The venomous snake-like gaze and the extremely sinister aura kept irritating Dan Ja-Kang’s mind. Just facing him felt like a poisoned dagger was aimed at his back.

A fierce killing intent, rarely seen even in the Demonic Cult, transcended martial skill and hardened a person.

Considering the meaning of ‘threatening,’ that person’s presence irritated Dan Ja-Kang even more than the swordsman. If even a slight gap appeared, that venomous snake’s fangs would surely pierce it.

The only commonality between those two, who were like opposites, was one thing.

‘They are skilled.’

By watching their sword techniques and their sense of targeting the opponent’s gaps, one can immediately tell. Both are extremely experienced fighters.

Bang!

At that moment, Chung Myung literally became a ray of light and rushed toward Dan Ja-Kang. He didn’t miss even the brief moment Dan Ja-Kang’s gaze shifted to Jang Ilso.

Paaaah!

Chung Myung’s sword, filled with sunset-like sword aura, fell toward Dan Ja-Kang’s head.

‘A predictable move!’

Dan Ja-Kang reached out his hand covered in demonic qi to snatch the flying sword. But at that moment.

Fwaaak!

With a sound like roaring flames, Chung Myung’s sword blurred and pierced through Dan Ja-Kang’s hand.

‘What...’

Swaaaak!

Chung Myung’s sword regained its form above Dan Ja-Kang’s head. Then it twisted again like a venomous snake and flew toward Dan Ja-Kang’s neck.

Dan Ja-Kang urgently twisted his neck to dodge the sword.

“Ugh!”

It wasn’t some magic. When Dan Ja-Kang tried to block the sword, it momentarily bent its path and slipped past his hand. The process was so fast and natural that it seemed as if the sword disappeared.

It’s easy to say but nearly impossible to perform in reality. This is not just a technique, but close to divine technique.

Paaaah!

The sword that narrowly grazed Dan Ja-Kang’s neck stopped abruptly in midair like a lie, then moved horizontally as if to slice his neck.

Thrust, stop, slash.

Though these should be three separate moves, the entire process was fast and natural as if it were one from the start. Is there a better textbook to explain how aiming processes combine into a technique?

Kagagagak!

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang grabbed the sword flying toward his neck. He clearly understood it wouldn’t be good to let Chung Myung run wild anymore. His dark hand began to squeeze the sword tightly, preventing any movement.

‘Damn!’

Gripping the sword with his black hand, Dan Ja-Kang pulled hard, intending to strike Chung Myung’s face with a punch.

But Chung Myung simply released the sword in midair.

Dan Ja-Kang, who had exerted all his strength aware of the opponent’s resistance, staggered for a moment.

“No way!”

Then a chilling voice came from behind.

Jang Ilso’s red long robe fluttered like a flag.

Bang!

Jang Ilso, having stepped into his true form, unleashed a barrage of chained strikes like torrential rain on Dan Ja-Kang’s back, while Chung Myung flipped in midair and rained down chained kicks like a waterfall on Dan Ja-Kang’s head.

“Ugh!”

Realizing he couldn’t handle it with one hand, Dan Ja-Kang threw the sword he was holding upward and spread both hands. The demonic qi rising like thick smoke blocked Jang Ilso’s fists and Chung Myung’s kicks.

Kwakwakwakwang!

Chung Myung’s foot, having delivered over ten chained kicks in one breath, descended fiercely. Seeing that, Dan Ja-Kang momentarily drew up his internal qi to counter!

Tak!

Chung Myung suddenly relaxed his descending leg, stepped on Dan Ja-Kang’s demonic energy like a stepping stone, jumped up, and snatched the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword floating in midair.

Kwaang!

Turning his body and kicking the air, Chung Myung shot toward Dan Ja-Kang like a falcon diving after prey. His eyes flashed with killing intent.

Finally, the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword fiercely slashed down from above.

Thhuuuuddddddd!

The sword tip struck the ground.

A suffocating silence flowed.

And Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes trembled greatly for a moment.

Chwaaak!

One of Dan Ja-Kang’s shoulders split open with a crack, blood spraying out like a fountain.

Chapter 1057: Your Voice Can’t Reach Me. (2)

A distance where hands could reach each other—no exaggeration for a martial artist to say it’s the line between life and death.

Yet, even with the enemy right there, Dan Ja-Kang’s gaze wasn’t on him but fixed on his own shoulder. Through the torn clothing, the split and bleeding wound was clearly visible.

With every heartbeat, the throbbing pain spread around the injury. But more intense than the pain was the stark reality that his shoulder had been cleanly sliced.

Flesh is cut when touched by a sword—that’s an obvious fact. But for Dan Ja-Kang, it had never been so.

“So easily...?”

This wound carried great meaning for him. His body, harder than steel and tougher than heavenly silkworm’s silk, was no different from an ordinary body before that sword.

The split shoulder began to close on its own. The wound that had exposed red flesh quickly knit together, erasing itself. It was an unreal, abnormally fast recovery.

Seeing this, Chung Myung muttered softly.

“Is that the Skeleton Demonic Technique?”

Though the voice was small, Dan Ja-Kang clearly understood and flinched. He glared at Chung Myung.

“How do you know that...?”

“A body that doesn’t die even when wounded—that’s certainly convenient in fights...”

Chung Myung bared his teeth.

“Do you know how the one who learned that demonic technique before you died?”

Before Dan Ja-Kang could answer, Chung Myung’s sword sliced through the air.

Pwaaah!

The red sword aura flying straight toward his neck seemed to answer for him.

Kagakang!

Dan Ja-Kang raised his hand to block the sword aura, and Chung Myung rushed right in front of him. The moment he faced that grotesque smile, a chill ran down Dan Ja-Kang’s back.

Chwaaaak!

A sword strike tearing through the air descended. But having faced this move before, Dan Ja-Kang deployed his demonic qi without a hint of carelessness. Surely, it was a trick to disrupt his defense with scattering sword strikes again!

But at that moment, Chung Myung’s sword accelerated again in the air and struck Dan Ja-Kang with overwhelming force.

Kwaaaaang!

Dan Ja-Kang’s knee twitched as he concentrated on the sword’s change.

Continuing!

Kwang! Kwaang! Kwaang!

Sword strikes like giant hammers fell repeatedly onto his demonic qi.

“Ugh!”

A groan escaped his lips. Every time the demonic qi flowing from his hands collided with the sword, an unknown qi penetrated inward through his hands. It was a pain like pouring ice-cold water directly inside his body.

‘My demonic qi... is dispersing?’

What kind of martial skill did this man have to so easily disrupt demonic qi?

Bang!

A disturbed mind eventually affects the body. Dan Ja-Kang, whose posture collapsed, was pushed back by Chung Myung’s sword. At the same time, Chung Myung stomped the ground and thunderously pursued the retreating Dan Ja-Kang.

“No chance!”

Demonic qi rose like clouds, beginning to surround Chung Myung. Unable to predict his opponent’s moves, he intended to overwhelm him with sheer force.

But at that moment, Chung Myung pulled his sword almost behind his back and fiercely slashed from left to right in one swift motion.

Pwaaaaaaang!

A cracking sound like a whip echoed. At the same time, everything around where Chung Myung’s sword passed began to distort, like crumpling a painted sheet of paper.

Then, a single red line bloomed.

A vivid line as if drawn by a painter’s brush. The demonic qi of Dan Ja-Kang touched by that line split in half as if by illusion.

Chung Myung, having cut the demonic qi with a single strike, immediately charged straight at Dan Ja-Kang with madness blazing in his eyes.

This was a battlefield where life and death hung in the balance, and the enemy was overwhelmingly strong.

But at this moment, what Chung Myung felt was not burden but an indescribable pleasure in the fight.

Pwaaah!

The sword extended twice as fast as usual flew straight toward Dan Ja-Kang’s face. The moment Dan Ja-Kang hastily raised his hand, Chung Myung’s sword seemed to know in advance and changed direction to evade the blocking hand.

Chwaaaak!

The tip of the sword that pierced Dan Ja-Kang’s wrist sliced lengthwise from wrist to elbow.

Blood sprinkled from the shallow cut. To Chung Myung’s eyes, this scene unfolded slowly without missing a detail.

‘Not yet.’

Not yet! Still not enough! Not yet!

‘I...’

The Plum Blossom Sword Saint was twice as fast and twice as strong. This level could not satisfy his thirst at all.

The internal qi, gathered drop by drop, surged from his dantian. With each step and sword swing, forgotten sensations revived at his fingertips.

‘More!’

The demonic qi pressing on his throat, breath rising to the top of his head. Though unhit, his whole body ached as if crushed, and blood vessels burst in his eyes.

His hands, constantly swinging the sword resisting the demonic qi, were already ragged, with finger bones about to show.

But...

Kwang!

The moment sword met the hand, Chung Myung clearly saw the distinct confusion in Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes. That expression fueled Chung Myung’s pleasure even more than before.

‘You don’t understand.’

How many countless battlefields had he crossed, how many Demonic Cult members he had killed, and how many High priests he had faced?

A High priest who hadn’t experienced the time of a hundred years ago could never understand.

‘More!’

Pwaaaaaah!

Still too slow. The power at the sword’s tip was too weak to compare with his former self. But...

Kagagagak!

Chung Myung’s sword pierced through the demonic qi again, carving a long wound on Dan Ja-Kang’s cheek.

‘That’s not all!’

Chung Myung now was still not at the level of the Plum Blossom Sword Saint, but he wielded a different weapon. A clearer qi than anything else gathered at the sword’s tip.

‘It pierces through.’

Gathering and refining the purest qi in the world, it could not be compared to ordinary righteous qi. It was sharp enough to tear apart demonic qi.

The wielder may have weakened, but the sword itself was an incomparable famous blade. Its sharpness served as Chung Myung’s other weapon.

“Haah!”

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang shouted a battle cry. The swirling demonic qi enveloped Chung Myung like a floating ghost.

The fierce demonic qi that would crush flesh and rot it just by touch was instead sharpening Chung Myung’s senses to the extreme. His whole body tingled.

“More!”

Pwaaah!

The sword tip painted illusions.

Kwagak! Kwagak!

Chung Myung, having struck away all the demonic qi, threw his body toward Dan Ja-Kang’s approaching hand. When his face was almost touching the hand, he kicked the air again with his extended toe. His body had gained even more speed.

Udeudeudeuk!

Dan Ja-Kang’s hand brushed over his shoulder. Though just a brush, flesh from shoulder to back was torn off as if ripped away.

A dizzying pain arose. But before it fully registered in his mind, Chung Myung plunged into Dan Ja-Kang’s embrace.

If weaker than before, there was only one way to overcome it: to rampage more violently and dangerously than before.

Pwaaah!

At extremely close range, the sword swung along an optimal trajectory deeply cut Dan Ja-Kang’s thigh. Because of the close distance and Dan Ja-Kang’s tough body, the sensation was more like a heavy skewer tearing up the flesh than a clean cut.

The moment the sword tip touched Dan Ja-Kang’s bone, Chung Myung quickly slashed. Using the recoil, he slipped his body backward.

Kwaaaaaang!

The space where his head had just been was slashed at tremendous speed by Dan Ja-Kang’s hand. Had Chung Myung greedily swung his sword fully, his head would have been split by this move.

But on Chung Myung’s face, who narrowly escaped death by a hair’s breadth, there was no trace of fear.

Standing on the edge of a blade where life and death hinge on a single moment’s choice, what ruled Chung Myung’s mind was not fear but ecstasy. The intense pleasure felt only when death approaches momentarily then swiftly recedes. Fully surrendering to it, Chung Myung twisted his body and charged at Dan Ja-Kang again.

Udeudeuk!

His sword-gripping hand tensed violently. Yet his body felt lighter than ever. Here, there was no one he had to protect or watch over. No reason to care about battles or sudden changes behind him.

He had only one task.

To cut the neck of the one before him.

Chung Myung’s face, freed from the burden pressing on his shoulders, was soaked with killing intent and ecstasy.

Seeing that excited face, Jang Il-so matched steps with Chung Myung and charged at Dan Ja-Kang. If there had been any room to spare, he might have been laughing wildly by now.

‘Crazy bastard.’

Jang Il-so always knew he wasn’t sane. But that guy, Chung Myung, was crazy in a completely different way.

The gap between life and death—the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint crosses the razor’s edge line between death and life several times even in a moment. No sane person could do that.

Of course, those who aim for the top must be willing to bet their lives, but what that crazy Taoist does far surpasses that level.

The sword Jang Il-so grabbed to swing was out of control, rampaging wildly. Or should it even be called a sword? It was more like an arrow released from a bowstring—an extreme weapon that becomes a meaningless stick if it can’t pierce the enemy.

‘I never thought the day would come when I would support someone else.’

His noble pride twisted, but even that ache was almost enjoyable. With his eyes shining mischievously, Jang Il-so raised his Spear of Flaming Death to match Chung Myung’s frenzied attacks.

‘Not yet!’

Both hands were dyed deep blue.

At that moment, Chung Myung’s sword transformed brilliantly, showering sword qi toward Dan Ja-Kang. All of Chung Myung’s focus was on Dan Ja-Kang. Anyone who considered Chung Myung an enemy would want to reach out and attack him.

But Jang Il-so suppressed his desires with utmost patience.

‘Not yet!’

Kwaaaaah!

The demonic qi, shooting out like a rain of arrows, instantly blew away the pouring sword qi. Then, like an enraged black dragon, the blazing sword’s demonic qi rushed toward Chung Myung—or rather, tried to.

‘Now!’

Bang!

Jang Il-so closed the distance in an instant and drove his spear qi into Dan Ja-Kang’s exposed side. The scene resembled a beast leaping out to seize its prey.

Kwaaaaang!

Dan Ja-Kang staggered and retreated further back. Chung Myung shouted sharply.

“Too late!”

“...You are such a greedy child.”

The two exchanged glances briefly and charged at Dan Ja-Kang like a wolf pack hunting a tiger.

Chapter 1058: Your Voice Can’t Reach Me. (3)

Baek Cheon unconsciously clenched his teeth tightly, his wide-open eyes fixed on Chung Myung and Jang Ilso as they fiercely pressed Dan Ja-Kang.

“That...”

The sight was truly astonishing. Even to Baek Cheon’s trained eyes—honed through intense practice with Chung Myung—the rapid exchange of attacks was so swift that he momentarily lost track.

Though he couldn’t catch every detail, he could clearly feel the intensity of the fierce battle—not through sight, but through his skin. The sensation, not his mind, was the first to shiver.

“Those crazy bastards...”

They risked everything to seize even a single opening revealed by the high priest, blocking the enemy’s seemingly impossible attacks as if they had foreknowledge.

The ultra-fast exchange of blows, turning like gears, resembled a well-choreographed opera more than a battle.

“Sasuk...”

“Yes.”

Baek Cheon nodded heavily at the voice behind him.

“They’re pressing hard.”

With every strike of Chung Myung’s sword—swift and precise like a viper—the enemy, the high priest of the Demonic sect, was forced to retreat.

Paaaah!

Each time Chung Myung sliced the air like a sharp spear, a fierce cracking sound rang out. Baek Cheon clenched his fists so tightly his knuckles turned white.

“That bastard...”

Looking at Chung Myung now, it was clear that neither the form he showed in sparring with them nor in the fight with the Black Dragon King represented his full strength.

No, that wasn’t it.

Chung Myung wasn’t deliberately hiding his true power. It was just that neither they nor the Black Dragon King could draw out all of Chung Myung’s strength. That meant the high priest was strong enough to pull out all of Chung Myung’s power and still remain a formidable opponent.

But what caught Baek Cheon’s attention most wasn’t Chung Myung’s astonishing swordplay or Dan Ja-Kang.

It was Jang Ilso.

It wasn’t about who was more impressive.

Baek Cheon was quite taken aback by this man. Jang Ilso was perfectly synchronizing with Chung Myung, who charged recklessly without concern for his back.

Is this even possible?

Baek Cheon knew Jang Ilso was never the type to yield initiative to others and merely support from the sidelines. Wasn’t Jang Ilso the one who manipulated everything in the world as he wished, using others as tools?

Anyone who knew Jang Ilso would agree. Even those from the Evil Sect would say the same.

Yet here, Jang Ilso gave the first attack to Chung Myung without complaint. He focused on blocking Dan Ja-Kang’s sudden counterattacks and searching for openings.

It was hard to believe this was a choice made by the notorious Jang Ilso.

Even more astonishing was how incredibly well their movements matched. The longer the fight went on, the more synchronized they seemed.

How could that be?

Baek Cheon knew best, having supported that wild man the most. He understood better than anyone how difficult it was to cover Chung Myung’s back.

Chung Myung’s sword, to put it elegantly, was like a mysterious divine dragon; crudely put, like a slippery loach sprinkled with salt. You never knew what it would do or where it would suddenly leap.

Even Baek Cheon, familiar enough with Chung Myung’s sword, had to sharpen his senses like a blade just to barely keep up, exhausting his internal qi terribly.

Yet now, Jang Ilso, synchronizing with Chung Myung for the first time, was accomplishing that difficult task as if they had practiced countless times and mastered professional combined strikes.

Damned bastard!

Putting aside feelings about Jang Ilso, there was no denying his greatness.

Kwahaaang!

As Chung Myung lunged forward, bracelets on Jang Ilso’s hands unleashed a terrifying force. Ten golden beams preemptively marked the bishop’s movement range, blocking any escape from Chung Myung’s sword.

It was a movement perfectly matched to Chung Myung. At the same time, Jang Ilso followed closely behind, ready for any sudden counterattack.

Neither of them was sane.

Jang Ilso, leader of the Evil Sects controlling half of the Central Plains, chose to cover Chung Myung’s back at this moment, and Chung Myung entrusted his back to the hated villain without hesitation. Both were madmen doing unimaginable things with ease.

But the insane harmony of those madmen was now driving the terrifying bishop back relentlessly.

Grit.

Baek Cheon bit his lip unconsciously.

The situation was so impressive that only admiration came to mind. Yet Baek Cheon could not watch with mere goodwill.

Because the position Jang Ilso took was originally meant for disciples of Mount Hua.

Still too lacking.

Baek Cheon glared fiercely at Jang Ilso.

Anyway... now we must win!

No matter the method, the important thing was to defeat the bishop and stop the demonic sect’s rampage.

He suddenly turned around.

At the moment he faintly gained confidence in the outcome, he finally had the leisure to look behind. It was uncertain how the Demonic Cult followers would react when the high priest died by Chung Myung’s sword, so he needed to check their movements in advance.

But Baek Cheon froze as he looked back at the Demonic Cult followers.

What is this?

It wasn’t because there was any noticeable reaction.
