Rather, the lack of any reaction confused Baek Cheon. The Demonic Cult followers remained prostrated as when the high priest first appeared, chanting incantations.

Come to think of it, the incantations, ringing like a song, had never stopped even once until now.

Since the battle began, even after Dan Ja-Kang clearly started to be pushed back.

Perhaps it was simply fanaticism. Faith inherently disregards circumstances. Since they lived steeped in fanaticism, it wouldn’t be strange for them to show blind faith in their leader.

But...

No. Something’s different.

They definitely seemed to know something—that this battle would not end like this.

“Chung Myung!”

Baek Cheon’s gaze snapped fiercely toward the front.

Chung Myung was rushing at Dan Ja-Kang, scattering lightning-like sword qi.

Kwakak!

Chung Myung’s sword sliced right beside Dan Ja-Kang’s neck. Blood gushed from the chillingly split wound, staining the robe black in places.

Paaaah!

Chung Myung’s sword relentlessly pressed Dan Ja-Kang, not giving him a single moment to breathe. It transformed bizarrely, sometimes relentlessly chasing, sometimes making insane gambles.

Beside, behind, and above Chung Myung, Jang Ilso continuously unleashed his power. When attacks seemed too much for Chung Myung’s internal strength, he risked himself to block them.

This was...

A chilling aura flashed in Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes.

Boom!

Dan Ja-Kang stomped firmly as he stared intently at Chung Myung’s sword flying overhead. At the same time, a massive demonic qi burst forth, spreading in all directions from his body.

Kwahaaaaaang!

Caught in the aftermath, Chung Myung’s body was flung backward like a leaf in a typhoon. After flying far back, he spun once in the air and landed low.

“Huff! Huff! Huff!”

As soon as he touched the ground, rough breaths like coughing up lungs poured from Chung Myung’s mouth. Sweat drenched not only his entire body but even his hair.

He continuously unleashed consecutive strikes while restricting his breathing, inevitably consuming his internal qi and stamina severely.

But Chung Myung’s eyes, as blue and fierce as ever, stared at Dan Ja-Kang, who was hiding behind his demonic qi.

After a moment, the demonic qi dissipated and Dan Ja-Kang revealed himself.

“Perhaps I... have underestimated you too much.”

Covered in wounds, anyone seeing Dan Ja-Kang now would first think of those words.

But Chung Myung knew that among the wounds on that body, few were truly fatal—especially considering Dan Ja-Kang had mastered the Skeleton Demonic Technique, which granted abnormal recovery.

“But... I still cannot understand,” Dan Ja-Kang said softly. “Is this all there is to you?”

He stomped the ground hard.

“If the level of the Central Plains is only this much... then why was the sect driven to that barren land after being defeated by you?”

Countless questions churned in Dan Ja-Kang’s mind.

How could the Heavenly Demon have been caught off guard by such people?

Of course, even a tiger can be bitten to death by a viper sometimes. But is there a tiger in the world that dies from an ant’s bite? Are these people even ants or more?

“It can’t be just this much, right?”

As anger rose, blood-red light burst again from Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes.

“Speak, warrior of the Central Plains. This can’t be all you have. Then show me everything you possess. Otherwise...”

Black demonic qi swirled around Dan Ja-Kang’s body like a floating evil spirit. A tremendous pressure began to weigh down the surroundings.

“You will die here!”

Kwahaaang!

Dan Ja-Kang charged at incredible speed.

It was closer to spatial contraction than movement—as if forcibly twisting and piercing space, Dan Ja-Kang’s body instantly appeared right before Chung Myung. Perhaps “appeared” was a better term.

Chung Myung’s eyes widened.

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang’s hand ruthlessly pierced Chung Myung’s side.

Chung Myung instantly raised his sword to block the flying hand, but the force Dan Ja-Kang wielded crashed into Chung Myung’s side along with the sword.

Kwahaaaaaang!

Crack!

The explosion of qi colliding and the sound of bones crushing mixed. Chung Myung’s body was flung away like a pebble kicked with full force.

Kwang!

Dan Ja-Kang pushed off the ground and pursued Chung Myung at terrifying speed. Having caught up instantly, he raised one hand toward the sky.

Black qi emitted from his entire body swirled and climbed along him, soon gathering at his fingertips.

The demonic qi, initially the size of a human head, quickly grew larger than a house. The darkness was so dense it seemed to suck in everything, resembling a blazing black sun.

Just as Dan Ja-Kang was about to strike down, numerous bright blue flame-like forces poured urgently before his face. Each had enough power to break through his qi.

But Dan Ja-Kang lowered his hand without hesitation.

“Ooooooh!”

The blazing black sun swallowed and crushed the blue flames.

The demonic qi that swept away all obstacles swallowed Chung Myung’s flying body whole.

Kwahaaaaaang!

The black sun blazed on the ground, shaking the entire world. The prostrated Demonic Cult followers also couldn’t withstand the massive explosion’s aftermath and were thrown about.

“Chy...”

Baek Cheon’s eyes widened as if they would tear.

“Chung Myungggg!”

The unbearably desperate scream was completely drowned out by the successive explosions.

Chapter 1059: Your Voice Can’t Reach Me. (4)

The earth screamed loudly, and the sky trembled.

From the successive impacts flying through the air, not only the cultists but even the disciples of Mount Hua were thrown back and pushed backward. Considering the distance between the fierce battle site and them, it was an absurd situation.

“Ch-Chung Myung...”

Baek Cheon’s hand trembled.

No matter how strong Chung Myung was, could he survive taking such an attack head-on? If so...

Faced with a conclusion he dared not even imagine, Baek Cheon’s reason wavered faintly.

“Sasuk! Over there!”

At the voice that came then, Baek Cheon’s head turned sharply.

The one who reached out was Jo Gul. And what he pointed at was... Chung Myung, half-sitting, supporting himself on one hand on the ground.

“Ah...”

For a moment, it felt as if all the strength drained from his body.

But it was not the time to be relieved.

“What kind of internal qi...”

Cold sweat poured down Baek Cheon’s forehead like rain.

There are usually two factors to discuss a martial artist’s strength. One is how perfectly they have mastered their martial arts. The other is how strong their internal qi is.

According to Chung Myung’s theory, elements like technique, improvisation, and mental strength would be added, but basically these two are the measures that determine a martial artist’s strength.

It is not good if one side clearly outshines the other. The most important thing is the harmony between martial arts and inner strength. At least Baek Cheon had believed so.

But after seeing this scene, he could not bring himself to say that.

The destructive power emanating from the overwhelmingly strong internal qi shattered Baek Cheon’s common sense about martial arts.

‘Th-this is why...’

The Demonic cult members knew how strong their high priest was, what kind of strength he was hiding.

That is why even when their high priest was being overwhelmed unilaterally, they could remain unshaken.

“Sa, Sasuk...”

Tang Soso’s frightened voice reached him.

“Can we really win this? Against that monster?”

Baek Cheon could not answer. He could not recklessly say he believed in Chung Myung. That would be recklessness, not faith.

Instead, Baek Cheon clenched his teeth and said.

“...Listen carefully, Soso.”

“...”

“That guy must not be allowed to live, absolutely not.”

“Sa-Sasuk.”

“If... if even Chung Myung or Jang Ilso cannot handle that guy... then everyone here must rush and somehow kill him.”

It was truly a ruthless statement to say. But on reflection, it was not wrong at all. Once those two lose, those left here have no future. The cult members would never let them go peacefully.

Then at least they must find value in that death.

But...

‘When will that time come?’

Baek Cheon clenched his fist tightly and pondered.

Is it really right to delay more time here? Are we putting too much burden on Chung Myung? Maybe we should join him right now...

Just as Baek Cheon nervously stepped forward unknowingly, someone grabbed his sleeve tightly.

“...Samae?”

Yu Yiseol shook her head with a stern expression.

“Wait, elder brother.”

“...”

“Sajil is still fighting.”

At those words, Baek Cheon’s gaze turned back to Chung Myung.

Even from this distance, he could clearly see the bright light flowing from Chung Myung’s lowered eyes.

“...Alright.”

Baek Cheon finally nodded heavily.

“But I can’t wait long. I don’t plan to take revenge after he dies.”

“Then I will be the first to rush out.”

Yu Yiseol’s voice was firm. Baek Cheon nodded with difficulty and stared at Chung Myung with bloodshot eyes.

‘Chung Myung...’

“Hoo... hoo...”

Blood-mixed sweat dripped down Chung Myung’s chin. His whole body ached as if it would shatter and felt unbearably heavy. It was as if all the moisture in his body had drained away.

Chung Myung glanced down at his feet. His right foot was half crushed.

At the moment the demonic qi flew in, he threw his sword into the air and used the recoil to escape successfully. But by a hair’s breadth, his right foot was swept by the demonic qi.

‘...Is it fortunate that it’s the right foot?’

For a user of the right-hand sword, the left foot is where weight is placed for sword strikes. If the left foot is injured, the sword cannot exert its full power. The right foot also affects, but at least it’s better than the left.

But... the important thing was not the power applied to the sword. Being hit in the foot means a problem with mobility. He lost one of his greatest weapons that allowed him to press Dan Ja-Kang until now.

Why? Why did this result happen?

Crack!

He stabbed his sword into the ground and struggled to stand, then spat arrogantly toward the approaching Dan Ja-Kang.

“...You, have you also mastered the Absorbing Demonic Arts?”

At those words, Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes flickered.

“It seems you are quite knowledgeable about the cult.”

“...Looks like you’re practically a cult member. To give a rookie like you the Absorbing Demonic Arts.”

Chung Myung bit his lip.

It was not a mistake. Just something he hadn’t thought of.

The current cult is different from the past. In the past, the cult would not have given such a rookie young high priest the Absorbing Demonic Arts.

Among demonic skills, the most incomplete and dangerous is the Absorbing Demonic Arts, which absorbs others’ internal qi to increase one’s own, but drives the user’s mind into chaos.

Those who enter the Heart Demon through Absorbing Demonic Art do not become cult fanatics or believers, but truly madmen who rampage indiscriminately like beasts.

So even the cult, which pursues only power and fanaticism, does not easily allow learning this skill. But...

‘I was stupid.’

He should have thought that the current cult is different from the past.

A hundred years ago, wars never ceased, so some who absorbed inner strength recklessly could not handle the Absorbing Demonic Arts.

But in the current cult, where cult members live only among themselves, there is only one target for absorption.

“...That bastard like a viper has now even devoured his own cultists.”

If the target of absorption was a cult member who had learned the same demonic skill, the side effects would have been minimized. Of course, it wouldn’t be completely free from side effects, but it could have been minimized.

Because of missing that, even Chung Myung did not realize that the High priest’s internal qi far exceeded imagination.

All predictions are based on one’s common sense and experience. Therefore, things beyond one’s common sense are not predicted. What those opposing Chung Myung often did, Chung Myung now did against that high priest.

“The cult madmen seem to have gotten their bodies right? But in the past, they didn’t touch their own cult members. Why? Are they afraid they will rot and decay after the Heavenly Demon died?”

“...”

“Answer me. How did it feel to devour your own cult members? When you absorbed the qi of theirs, when they began to scream, did you properly look at their faces?”

Dan Ja-Kang closed his eyes tightly.

‘How did it feel?’

That guy doesn’t know at all. He doesn’t understand at all. He seems to have knowledge about the cult, but no understanding.

‘He can’t understand.’

The faces of those who happily offered their internal qi to him as they died. Those who died in ecstasy believing they would be fertilizer when the Heavenly Demon descended.

The one who couldn’t endure was rather...

Dan Ja-Kang’s gaze darkened.

“Pitiful, Central Plains people.”

“...What?”

“Beasts do not show weakness until the moment of death. No matter how painful, they endure until the moment they can bear no more.”

“...”

“If not, they are abandoned and become targets again. What on earth have you experienced that you don’t show weakness even for a moment despite your condition?”

Grit.

Chung Myung ground his teeth.

“You talk nonsense as you please.”

Chung Myung’s voice sounded like a groan from someone stabbed in the lungs.

After a few short breaths, the true Chung Myung walked toward Dan Ja-Kang, dragging his wounded foot.

“This is why rookies like you won’t do.”

“...”

“What, do you already think you’ve won?”

Chung Myung smiled with a sinister aura.

“Let me tell you something, you fool. There is no victory or defeat on the battlefield. There is only kill or be killed.”

Such a trivial wound is nothing. He had fought with wounds many times worse and killed his opponents. It was nothing new now. There was no reason to complain just because one foot was crushed.

But Dan Ja-Kang just calmly looked at Chung Myung.

“We waited for the descent.”

“...”

“For all those long years. Just waited... and waited again. Never giving up the faint hope that one day he would answer our faith, we just waited.”

Dan Ja-Kang paused and looked up at the sky.

“That waiting is not the hard part. The truly hard part is the lives of those dying without even a single answer. That weight.”

“Ugh...”

Chung Myung’s shoulders twitched suddenly as he listened quietly.

Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes flashed darkly for a moment. He instinctively felt that the laughter was not made to provoke him.

“What’s so funny?”

He asked, but Chung Myung, unable to calm down, laughed for a long time and then choked and covered his mouth.

“Ah, sorry.”

Still laughing, his blood-stained teeth were fully exposed.

“It’s like watching a person fishing for fish above the clouds.”

“...What do you mean?”

“It means everything you say is wrong.”

Chung Myung gripped his sword.

“You will never be rewarded.”

“...”

“Even if the Heavenly Demon descends, your voice won’t reach there. At least not to him.”

Dan Ja-Kang’s face twisted.

Chung Myung returned Dan Ja-Kang’s words exactly.

“Pitiful.”

“...You...”

“A fool who doesn’t even know what he believes in or whom he serves. Your god does not care for you. Your screams mean nothing to your god.”

Gnash!

With the sound of grinding teeth, Dan Ja-Kang’s face twisted like a demon.

He knew. There was no need to listen to that guy’s words. No reason to be shaken by words picked up from someone who knows nothing about the cult or the Heavenly Demon.

Yet the only reason he could not contain his anger now was one.

Because what Chung Myung said was exactly what he least wanted to hear.

Like a dragon whose reverse scale was struck, the qi flowing inside him began to boil uncontrollably.

“You... you filthy unbeliever...”

“Yes. In your eyes, I’m just an unbeliever. Dirty and ugly. But you should know one thing.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“The unbeliever you despise so much and you who risk your lives licking the Heavenly Demon’s feet... to that madman called the Heavenly Demon, there’s no difference at all.”

“You...”

Bloody gaze exploded from Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes.

“How dare you! How dare you say that! How dare you!”

“At this point, I’m curious too.”

Chung Myung shrugged and laughed, even wiping tears.

“I wonder what expression a fool like you who doesn’t even know what the Heavenly Demon is would make when you see the Heavenly Demon with your own eyes. You’re just an idiot who doesn’t even know what god you believe in.”

“BRATTTT!”

Responding to Dan Ja-Kang’s rage, demonic qi explosively surged like a huge waterfall shooting into the sky. Overwhelmed by uncontrollable anger, Dan Ja-Kang rolled his eyes back and rampaged.

“I will kill! I will kill! I will crush that cursed mouth leaving not a single piece of flesh! How dare you insult the cult and blaspheme the Heavenly Demon, I will not leave even your soul in peace!”

“Come and try, you idiot...!”

Just as Chung Myung shouted fiercely and tried to rush forward, someone blocked his way.

The man wearing a torn red robe was Jang Ilso.

“Sorry to interrupt your friendly gathering, but you still need to rest more.”

“...What?”

“I’m tired of listening to that nonsense now. I’ll create a momentary opening, so don’t miss it, okay?”

Jang Ilso smiled faintly, clenched his fist tightly, and with the clinking sound of his ring, he charged headlong at the rushing Dan Ja-Kang, radiating tremendous killing intent.

Chapter 1060: Your Voice Can’t Reach Me. (5)

The dark red blood light pouring from his eyes was chilling. On top of that, the black demonic qi swirling around his entire body resembled an evil spirit.

The High Priest charged forward with a face half-lost to reason, mercilessly digging into the primal fear inherent in humans.

From his mouth, stretched as if to tear apart, came a beastly, no, even more feral howl. Hearing that scream piercing the eardrums, Jang Il-so raised the corner of his mouth.

Truly fascinating creatures.

Those called the strong inevitably become aware of their position, whether they like it or not. Put positively, they gain dignity; put negatively, they become hypocrites with double standards.

But to these demonic cult members, no such ‘class’ was felt at all. What could be seen in the High Priest was raw savagery itself. It seemed to prove that humans are nothing more than beasts.

No, perhaps that is the true nature of fanaticism. True believers are all equal under the deity, so there is no need to claim any class for oneself.

“But no matter how much...”

His lips, looking redder against his pale face, drew a chilling curve.

“Isn’t this just too disgraceful?”

Emitting a chilling killing intent, Jang Il-so accelerated and surged forward. The torn hem of his robe fluttered wildly in the gust.

“Kyaaaaaa!”

Demonic qi gathered in the High Priest’s hands as if even his last shred of reason had been blown away. Each strand of that smoky qi was stronger and more destructive than the steel qi wielded by absolute warriors. Even a graze would crush bones and tear flesh.

Dan Ja-Kang charged at a terrifying speed, swinging his hand toward Jang Il-so’s head.

It was a reckless attack without any technique, but the fierce demonic qi surrounding his hand transformed the madman’s desperate strike into an unparalleled martial move.

At that moment, Jang Il-so’s hand shot out like lightning.

‘I’ve seen enough, what now!’

Before Dan Ja-Kang’s raised hand could reach his head, Jang Il-so’s palm struck and broke Dan Ja-Kang’s elbow first. The swirling demonic qi barely grazed Jang Il-so’s head before crashing to the ground.

Jang Il-so seized the fleeting opening he found and leapt forward again.

But Dan Ja-Kang, as if anticipating his actions, swung his other hand to attack the advancing Jang Il-so.

‘That’s right!’

Jang Il-so twisted his body with the momentum of his charge.

His robe fluttered toward the incoming hand. He spun as if the High Priest’s arm was an icy surface, sliding smoothly. The hem of his robe fluttered as he moved toward the High Priest’s side, not his chest.

‘Still there, right?’

No matter how much he denies it, the image surely lingered in the High Priest’s mind like an afterimage: the swordsman Chung Myung, wielding a sword as a mid-range weapon, relentlessly piercing into the opponent’s embrace!

Humans instinctively avoid such situations after experiencing danger. So naturally, the bishop would try to block his own embrace when Jang Il-so charged forward!

This reaction was exactly what Jang Il-so aimed for from the start.

Kwaaahhh!

The demonic qi that crashed into the ground exploded, delivering a delayed shock to Jang Il-so’s back. The impact was so great that blood momentarily surged backward, but Jang Il-so did not dodge; he accepted it.

Then he swiftly penetrated the High Priest’s completely exposed side.

“So this is how it’s done.”

Jang Il-so’s hand, tinged blue, struck the High Priest’s side without hesitation.

Bang!

Before the High Priest’s body could even be pushed back by the impact, Jang Il-so’s fists struck the same spot repeatedly like lightning bolts.

Having driven a dozen punches into the High Priest’s side at once, Jang Il-so hesitated briefly before stomping the ground. Then he pursued the rebounding High Priest.

‘No! Not yet!’

Landing hits was not everything. The important thing was to give the enemy no chance to catch their breath!

Kwaaah!

The High Priest, rebounding, swung his arm in midair. Rough, black demonic qi flew horizontally toward Jang Il-so, as if a giant brush had splattered ink in the air.

Jang Il-so lowered his stance close to the ground right then and narrowly dodged. The eerie qi brushing just above his head sent a chill down his spine.

But even more so, Jang Il-so’s face began to be dyed with an even more chilling delight.

“This guy’s crazier than I thought! Hahaha!”

Jang Il-so burst into a wild laugh and surged forward to a close distance from the High Priest.

Having experienced it firsthand, he began to understand why that mad Taoist fought like that.

Jang Il-so had already fought alongside Chung Myung once during the Black Dragon incident. At that time, he was fed up with Chung Myung’s combat style that seemed to risk his life recklessly.

Back then, he only thought a rare madman had appeared in the Justice faction. But watching this scene against the High Priest and fighting in that style himself, he began to understand.

‘That’s not how you fight!’

Swoosh!

The wide sleeve of his robe instantly hardened like steel. Charged with internal qi and sharpened like a blade, the sleeve ruthlessly tore at the bishop’s hand.

‘He has no choice but to fight like that!’

This guy was strong. Unbelievably strong.

Especially his internal qi was vast to an extent even Jang Il-so, the best in the world, had never experienced before. Before seeing him, he never thought a person could wield such internal qi.

Even the Shaolin abbot, unmatched in internal qi worldwide, would seem ordinary next to this guy.

But compared to that vast internal qi, their techniques were extremely simple. Their martial art was simply overwhelming the opponent with sheer internal qi.

Then how should someone without such internal qi face them?

All the answers were with Chung Myung.

To draw up internal qi from the dantian and convert it into qi requires a minimum amount of time. The larger the amount of internal qi to be drawn, the longer the time needed.

The key was to not give that time.

If they fought at a distance where they could exchange qi blasts, defeat was certain. The destructive power of their martial art was so great it ignored the finesse of techniques.

Therefore, the only way was to give no chance to gather internal qi, and bombard with continuous attacks at close range, dragging the fight into a brutal brawl.

But!

Kwaaahhh!

Jang Il-so sharply bent his waist backward. The High Priest’s black hand flew past just above his nose by a hair.

‘Easier said than done!’

This was just a methodology.

The enemy’s internal qi was vast. Even the hastily drawn internal qi, without enough time to gather, was enough to reduce a human body to a handful of blood and flesh.

So that method was no different than saying to survive a spreading fire on a mountain, you must break through in the direction the fire spreads. This was a method only a madman who didn’t value his life would take.

But there was someone. Surely existed. Someone who could do that madness without flinching.

So!

Kwaaah!

With terrifying madness pouring from his eyes, Jang Il-so threw his body toward Dan Ja-Kang’s arm aimed at him.

Inside! Inside! Faster!

‘If I can’t do it!’

Jang Il-so’s elbow drove into Dan Ja-Kang’s forearm.

Crack!

But the sound of bones crushing came from Jang Il-so’s shoulder, not Dan Ja-Kang’s arm. The demonic qi brushing past easily crushed his shoulder.

His face twisted involuntarily in pain. But Jang Il-so gritted his teeth and moved again toward a spot very close to Dan Ja-Kang.

‘Won’t I be ashamed!’

Spinning quickly like a top right in front of Dan Ja-Kang’s nose, Jang Il-so rammed his shoulder into his chest. The shoulder strike, powered by rotational force, exploded against Dan Ja-Kang’s chest.

Bang!

Using the rebound, he slightly lifted his body and struck Dan Ja-Kang’s jaw with his knee.

Smash!

As he spun his body in the air, he unleashed dozens of continuous strikes toward Dan Ja-Kang’s head. The blue qi from his feet shot toward Dan Ja-Kang like a waterfall.

“Ugh!”

The continuous strikes rained down like a torrential downpour in the blink of an eye, finally drawing a groan from the now insane Dan Ja-Kang.

Bang!

Like Chung Myung, Jang Il-so kicked off the air repeatedly to accelerate his approach, charging at Dan Ja-Kang with a face full of raging qi.

“Kyaaaaaa!”

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang let out a roar and threw a lightning-fast punch to hit back. It was incomparably faster than any punch he had thrown so far.

Though he had surrendered to demonic qi and chosen to be a beast in life, it seemed a trace of reason remained. He reduced internal qi to increase speed, delivering the most perfect strike possible at this moment. For Jang Il-so accelerating in midair, it was an unavoidable and unblockable blow.

‘He still has some part of brain working, huh!’

But Jang Il-so did not slow down or change direction at all, even as Dan Ja-Kang’s fist flew straight to pierce his face.

It was suicide. Nothing else could describe such madness.

Without a moment’s hesitation, Jang Il-so charged forward, drawing qi into both hands as if Dan Ja-Kang’s fist did not exist.

At that moment.

Kwaaahhh!

Chung Myung leapt like a ray of light above Jang Il-so’s head and swung his sword. Not the blade but the flat side, a strike that hits rather than cuts!

Dan Ja-Kang’s fist, aimed to pierce Jang Il-so’s face, was deflected. Its trajectory twisted, grazing Jang Il-so’s cheek.

Crack!

In an instant, skin was torn and bone crushed.

But Jang Il-so did not blink at that and immediately thrust both hands with swift speed toward Dan Ja-Kang. The qi flowing through his hands left irregular blue trails in the air. The blue lines, etched at the fiercest points, were strangely beautiful, unbefitting this brutal battle.

Kwaaahhh!

Jang Il-so’s twin palm strikes buried into Dan Ja-Kang’s abdomen. His hands, surrounded by blue flames, tore through Dan Ja-Kang’s clothing and imprinted two bright blue palm seals on his stomach.

Blood spurted from Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth, covering Jang Il-so’s face. Stained red with both his own and the enemy’s blood, Jang Il-so grinned, revealing white teeth.

“In the end, it is humans who catch and kill beasts.”

Bang!

Jang Il-so’s palm strike lifted Dan Ja-Kang’s jaw. As Dan Ja-Kang was bounced back halfway airborne, Jang Il-so wildly whipped his robe’s hem and pursued.

His face, mixed with killing intent, pleasure, and even fear, now bore an intense expression unlike any before.

‘This!’

His eyes flashed incessantly at the intense sensation that seemed to pierce him from head to toe.

‘What an amazing feeling?’

Right now, he was on the edge of a blade.

Chapter 1061: Is This How You Do It? (1)

Kwaaaaaaang!

The disciples of Mount Hua staggered, their eyes wide as they took in the aftermath of the raging explosion.

“Th-That...”

Every time Dan Ja-Kang unleashed his powerful qi, the earth cracked and the terrain twisted. Yet, amidst the terrifying explosion of demonic enhanced qi, Jang Ilso did not retreat an inch; he stood firm against Dan Ja-Kang.

Even in the face of an attack that could topple Mount Tai, Jang Ilso tenaciously clung to Dan Ja-Kang.

It was truly a perilous tightrope walk.

No, even that phrase couldn’t fully capture the deadly stunt.

Even the disciples of Mount Hua, who harbored nothing but malice toward Jang Ilso, felt their hearts drop every time the dark demonic qi seemed to graze his body.

In the blink of an eye, dozens of exchanges occurred. If he made even a single mistake, that dreadful demonic qi would strike him directly at close range, and at that moment, half of Jang Ilso’s body would be blown away.

And Jang Ilso himself knew this far more clearly than the disciples of Mount Hua. Yet, he pressed Dan Ja-Kang relentlessly as if he felt no fear toward the man.

As if...

“Th-That... Chung Myung is...”

At Jo Gul’s groan, Baek Cheon nodded unconsciously. The fighting style Jang Ilso was showing now resembled exactly the figure they knew well.

‘To think there’s another person in the world who can do that.’

Even Baek Cheon, who had endlessly observed and learned from Chung Myung, dared not attempt such an absurd feat. The reason Baek Cheon couldn’t try that method wasn’t because he was weak.

It was because knowing it didn’t mean he could do it.

In a situation where unseen attacks are being exchanged, standing that close and not allowing a single attack from the opponent is a matter of sense before learning. And no matter how much one refines their senses, human senses cannot escape their innate limits.

In terms of boldly unfolding the knowledge of learning in accordance with the right means, Baek Cheon was actually a lot better than Chung Myung. However, no matter how hard Baek Cheon tried, it was impossible to imitate Chung Myung’s animalistic nature of sense.

Yet now, Jang Ilso is facing the high priest just like Chung Myung.

At that moment, Yoon Jong, who had been watching dazedly, spoke with a strained voice.

“Isn’t that... too dangerous, he...?”

That tightrope act, which squeezed even the hearts of the onlookers, finally made the disciples of Mount Hua voice their concern for Jang Ilso.

They knew.

That if someone could do the same, Jang Ilso might even be better than Chung Myung now.

But strangely, watching that scene didn’t calm their hearts. It felt as if Jang Ilso’s head might explode and scatter his brains at any moment.

‘Is that really possible?’

Cold sweat broke out and their whole bodies felt sticky.

“If Jang Ilso gets hit like that...”

At that moment, a chilling voice whispered in their ear.

“You blabber as soon as you appear, you foolish pups of the Justice sect.”

Baek Cheon reflexively turned his head to look at the speaker.

Ho Ga-Myung, a soldier of the Evil sect, continued speaking without taking his eyes off the fierce battle between Jang Ilso and Dan Ja-Kang.

“If you don’t know, shut your mouths and watch. You’re not worthy to judge that man with your petty mouths.”

Baek Cheon felt a surge of anger at those words but it quickly faded—not because of Ho Ga-Myung’s presence, but because of the expression he wore.

As far as Baek Cheon knew, Ho Ga-Myung was like an extension of Jang Ilso’s limbs.

He had heard that even before the Ten Thousand People Clan existed, the names of Jang Ilso and Ho Ga-Myung spread together.

Then to Ho Ga-Myung, Jang Ilso must be an irreplaceable existence. Yet how could he wear such a calm expression while his lord risked his life on that tightrope?

Then Ho Ga-Myung shifted his gaze and coldly looked at the disciples of Mount Hua.

He had no desire to speak with them, but...

“You seem to trust Mount Hua’s Sword Saint more.”

Baek Cheon didn’t answer, but Ho Ga-Myung continued as if no answer was needed.

“That’s because you’ve witnessed with your own eyes the miraculous victories the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint has achieved in his time.”

Baek Cheon opened his lips to retort but then closed them firmly.

That wasn’t the only reason they trusted Chung Myung. But if Chung Myung hadn’t repeatedly won battles that seemed impossible to them, they wouldn’t blindly trust him like they do now.

“How many years have you watched that? Five? Ten?”

“...”

“Listen carefully, you pups of the Justice faction.”

Ho Ga-Myung spat out the words.

“The Hegemony Lord Jang Ilso has crushed those stronger than himself for over twenty years. If there had been even a single defeat, the name Jang Ilso would have been forgotten as just another wandering warrior.”

“...”

“He survived because he never lost, and he conquered because he never lost. Whether the opponent is strong or not, it doesn’t matter.”

For a moment, Baek Cheon felt a chill down his spine.

Of course, they also trusted Chung Myung excessively. But Ho Ga-Myung’s trust in Jang Ilso was on a different level.

‘Isn’t that closer to a cult belief than trust?’

It was more like faith than trust. Without questioning reasons or circumstances, he simply believed.

But... when you think about it, wouldn’t it be like that?

If they continue to watch Chung Myung win repeatedly like this for over ten years, wouldn’t they become fanatical about him in terms of victory?

Just like Ho Ga-Myung, who has watched everything and blindly trusts Jang Ilso.

Then Ho Ga-Myung spoke decisively.

“At least I cannot even imagine the Hegemony Lord losing.”

Shaken by that firm and blind faith, Baek Cheon involuntarily looked back at Jang Ilso.

The giant who had risen to the pinnacle of the Evil Sect with those two hands.

A few strands of torn hair fluttered in all directions.

Disheveled, Jang Ilso spat blood and charged forward relentlessly. Both his shoulders had long since darkened from the pressure of the demonic qi.

His body was a wreck, obvious to anyone.

But the glimmers in his eyes through the messy hair were far more vivid than at the start of the battle.

Kwaduk!

Dan Ja-Kang’s hand scratched Jang Ilso’s wrist, literally tearing off a handful of flesh. Even a mere graze left a deep wound exposing bone.

Tearing flesh from bare skin surely caused pain, but Jang Ilso showed no reaction and persistently targeted Dan Ja-Kang’s head.

‘Crazy bastard.’

From behind Jang Ilso, Chung Myung suddenly let out a hollow laugh.

It wasn’t a provocation on the battlefield; such genuine disbelief laughter was rare.

That much, Jang Ilso’s current display was unbearably intense.

‘Is that how I look when you watch me from behind?’

One doesn’t know when doing it oneself. People cannot objectively see themselves in action. It’s an experience no one else can have. Chung Myung’s sole impression from watching himself fight from a third-person perspective was one thing.

‘...You survived well until the end.’

While doing such things. Now he seemed to understand why those who saw him fight all felt distant from him. And he clearly understood how his opponents felt.

Of course, Jang Ilso was not perfect.

He was a swordsman, but Jang Ilso was a warrior who used fists. No matter how much he tried to be alike, fundamental differences existed between them. Those differences pushed Jang Ilso to the brink of death repeatedly.

But that didn’t matter. Because Chung Myung was supporting him from behind.

It also didn’t matter that Chung Myung had never supported anyone before. He could understand. He sensed how the one always behind had protected Chung Myung—not with eyes, but with feeling.

Yes, the scene he was witnessing now...

Was the very scene Tang Bo saw a hundred years ago.

Breathing and harmonizing. Every breath, every movement, even the slightest muscle twitch must be felt as if touched. One must perfectly unify with the one before them and predict movements before they happen.

‘You pulled off this dreadful stunt well, damn bastard!’

Though it was displeasing that the one needing such protection was given by that damn Jang Ilso, somehow it didn’t feel as bad as expected.

Thanks to that, he could engrave in his eyes a scene he could never have known alone.

Pwaaah!

Chung Myung’s sword shot forward. Immediately after, Dan Ja-Kang and Jang Ilso fiercely reached toward each other. Chung Myung’s sword, moving just a moment earlier, pre-empted Dan Ja-Kang’s attack direction, blocking it and slashing flesh with a precise strike.

An extreme thrust putting everything into a single sword strike. This was closer to secret sword techniques than swordsmanship.

Other secret techniques? Well, I use them if needed. I just like this one. Why? Got a problem?

‘Stupid bastard!’

Only after standing behind did he understand.

For killing an opponent, subtle secret techniques are much more useful in action. If combined with Dan Ja-Kang’s poison, hitting the opponent’s body with secret techniques is the priority.

But that alone can’t protect the one in front. To block an enemy, qi must be applied, and to threaten an enemy, it must be visible to their eyes.

The paradox that the secret sword technique, which should be more secretive and swift than any weapon, must be thrown visibly to the enemy’s eyes. Within that paradox, the twelve swords, the Twelve Secret Swords, were perfected.

Grit.

Chung Myung bit his lip until it bled.

‘Why do I realize everything so late, this idiot...’

Even on the battlefield, where he was painfully lonely and sometimes utterly isolated, countless people stood behind him. Without them, Chung Myung would have long since become a cold corpse on the battlefield, never having cut the Heavenly Demon’s neck.

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang let out a terrible roar and raised his arm. Jang Ilso did not just watch. He instantly penetrated inwards the qi and, in the blink of an eye, delivered twelve fist attacks to Dan Ja-Kang’s upper body.

“Kkeuruk...”

Blood gushed again from Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth. Yet, even as he spat blood, his eyes gleamed with even darker bloodlust.

“Heaven... Heavenly Demon’s Second Coming.”

At that moment, Jang Ilso’s gaze sharply rose upward.

Unlike a swordsman’s attack, a fist master’s strike can be endured with defensive enhanced qi. That difference was dragging Jang Ilso to the depths of hell at this moment.

“Demons-Bow-And-Pray!!”

A massive demonic qi, the size of a house, burst from Dan Ja-Kang’s fingertips and fell simultaneously over Jang Ilso and Chung Myung’s heads.

At that moment!

Taoist hyung!

‘Yes!’

Chung Myung dashed forward as if he already knew.

‘Is this how you do it?’

Chung Myung’s sword shot out like a flash, transforming into a secret sword technique and flying toward Dan Ja-Kang’s neck.

Chapter 1062: Is This How You Do It? (2)

Kwaaaaaaah!

The sky seemed to collapse overhead. The demonic qi darkening everything around rushed in like hell descending upon the world.

A proclamation of destruction and an incarnation of the end. Within that deep despair, a sword dyed in sunset hues moved—like a ray of light or a phantom, just like a moment from the past.

A sword is just a sword, merely a cold metal blade.

But to a swordsman, a sword is not just an emotionless blade. At its tip lies everything of the wielder.

Perhaps nobility. Perhaps confidence.

And perhaps... what rested at the tip of this sword, or rather the flying sword, was...

‘Reach out.’

The vital qi rising from the toes gathered beyond the fingertips into the sword.

A sense of unity as if the sword and body became one in an instant. The clarity born from an excessively sharpened perception. And the great satisfaction that follows.

The sword, containing all of that, simply advanced.

“Just once!”

Chung Myung’s eyes holding the sword emitted a fierce killing intent. The sword could be swung again. Even if it cut through the air, it could be swung again or stabbed once more.

But the flying dagger was a single, fatal strike.

Once the flying dagger left the fingertips, it was over. No second chance was given to change it. Amidst an overwhelming pressure that words cannot describe, Chung Myung’s sword drew a single line. It was as if everything stopped except for the sword flying through the air.

A line is originally a connection. It draws between things that should not touch, linking them as one.

The line drawn by Chung Myung’s sword connected the present where he stood and the faded past long ago.

A gap in time split and split again in an instant. An unprecedented acceleration of thought. In a world that felt painfully slow by comparison, Chung Myung felt a burning thirst.

‘Not enough!’

This wasn’t it.

‘It’s not just about this much!’

The flying dagger of Tang Bo that he remembered was never such a mediocre strike. A flying dagger that seemed to carry even the soul in a single release. Like a meteor at dawn, it cut through a fleeting moment and disappeared, but because of that, Tang Bo’s flying dagger was more brilliant than anything else.

So it had to be faster. More precise, and more powerful! More! Even more!

At that moment, an almost phantom-like voice brushed past Chung Myung’s ear.

Taoist hyung, you’re always too impatient, that’s your flaw.

Words Tang Bo once playfully said to him in the past.

There are countless things you want to hold within your grasp, but if you hold them all, it only becomes heavy. Like your two shoulders, Taoist hyung. Can you properly wield a sword with shoulders weighed down so heavily?

The voice sounded so clear, as if whispered right beside him.

If you want to throw the flying dagger properly, you must empty, not fill it. The tip of the flying dagger must be light. The more you want to hold, the more you empty. Isn’t that the way of Tao that it teaches? Though, I don’t know if a blockhead like Taoist hyung can understand that...

Even the laughter mixed with that voice tickled his ears. Truly, at this moment, it felt as if a moment from the past continued.

If someday that becomes possible...

Everything that bound Chung Myung was severed. What remained was only the sensation of the sword at his fingertips. That sensation spread throughout Chung Myung’s entire body.

The flying sword and Chung Myung became one.

The sword and body united.

Who knows? Taoist hyung’s sword might really reach that Heavenly Demon.

Chung Myung cut through the darkness-drenched world. The tip of the flying weapon pointed to the source of that darkness. That strike was the sword of Mount Hua that Chung Myung wielded and the flying dagger Tang Bo launched long ago.

Chung Myung, who connected what should not be connected, transformed into the dagger that destroys demons and shot toward the demon’s heart.

‘I am here!’

That was an undeniable fact. But all of him was clearly connected to the past.

The sword, having achieved unity, transcended selflessness, and finally reached nature, cut through the world as it was meant to.

And then, in a moment, Jang Ilso and Chung Myung’s gazes met as they turned their heads.

Even in the flow of time almost frozen, it was clearly visible—the sly smile Jang Ilso wore.

Jang Ilso raised his arm. It was a gesture too weak and powerless to oppose the massive darkness descending overhead. Yet at that moment, an icy cold flame burst forth from his entire body.

Hwaaaaaaak!

A final, all-out strike drawing out the last ounce of strength soared toward the pouring demonic qi.

Blue flames and black demonic qi clashed in the air. The flames were too faint to block the overwhelming demonic qi pouring down.

But even if only for a moment, those flames clearly pushed back the collapsing mountain-like demonic qi. In the seemingly unbreakable iron wall, a tiny but distinct gap opened. No, Jang Ilso pried it open.

Their locked gazes broke as Chung Myung’s body brushed right past Jang Ilso. At that very moment, Jang Ilso’s hand touched Chung Myung’s completely defenseless back.

A voice that could never be heard by Chung Myung seemed to brush past his ear.

“Go.”

Paaaah!

The sword shot beyond limits into a miracle, aided by Jang Ilso’s force pushing Chung Myung’s back.

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes, clouded and blurred as he surrendered to the rising demonic qi, regained a clear light.

‘Sunset?’

It was a vision like a dream. So wondrous it even restored Dan Ja-Kang’s reason, which had been completely tainted by demonic qi and the fight.

In the world Dan Ja-Kang created, where not a speck of light seemed visible, a faint red glow blossomed. That faint red light pierced the deep, deep darkness and gradually spread.

Like the sunset spreading from the eastern sky at the end of a long night.

That was dawn. The end of night and another beginning.

In that scene where reality and illusion mixed, a white sword flew cutting through the red sunset.

“What is this?”

It was a chilling sense of death Dan Ja-Kang had never experienced before. The sword, like dawn burning through darkness, shattered everything and deeply pierced toward his neck.

Kwaduk!

At that moment, as if the world completely stopped, a deep silence fell around. Everyone present saw clearly.

The scene of the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword piercing through the neck of the despairingly strong High priest Dan Ja-Kang, who seemed never to fall, protruding from the nape of his neck.

Everyone held their breath.

It felt as if all the sounds of the world vanished for a moment.

Though the qi waves still raged around them like a storm, everyone distinctly felt that silence. A silence all the more intense because it was so brief.

Drip.

A drop of blood fell from the tip of the white sword that pierced through Dan Ja-Kang’s neck.

And at that moment.

Chung Myung’s darkened indifferent gaze and Dan Ja-Kang’s raging eyes intertwined closely.

Vomiting the refluxed blood, Dan Ja-Kang muttered something silently. Though others couldn’t understand, Chung Myung clearly understood the incantation.

“...Pray”

Dan Ja-Kang forcefully slammed down the arm he had raised as if shaking it off.

The sun of demonic qi, not yet fully dispersed, fell above their heads. It was like a mountain collapsing.

“Ah...!”

Everyone’s pupils dilated widely.

“Noooooooo!”

With a massive scream whose source no one could tell, overwhelming demonic qi collided with the earth.

Kwaaaaaaaaaaang!

The demonic qi embedded in the land surged as if to destroy everything. Soon, it covered the world in a massive storm of demonic qi.

“Aaaaaaaah!”

The disciples of Mount Hua caught in the storm were blown away helplessly like leaves in a typhoon.

Not only Mount Hua’s disciples but also the cult members watching the fierce battle with dazed faces were swept away like fallen leaves by that irresistible force.

The disciples of Mount Hua, repeatedly slammed into the ground after being thrown back, groaned in pain.

Kwaaaaang!

The successive thunderous booms assaulted everyone as if to burst their eardrums. It was a shock as if the world was collapsing all at once.

Kwang! Kwaaaang! Kwang!

After that massive shock violently swept the earth.

Finally, silence arrived.

Twitch.

Baek Cheon, covered in the dirt and debris violently thrown by the earth’s upheaval, trembled. His fingertips twitched in a spasm.

“Ugh...”

He groaned and, regaining consciousness, lifted his head fiercely.

“Ch-Chung Myung...”

Bloodshot veins began to appear in his eyes.

Even Chung Myung... could he really survive at the center of such an absurd explosion? Even he, caught in the aftermath, was so turned inside out that moving was difficult.

“No, no... no...”

Crunch!

Baek Cheon crushed the rock in his hand, gathering strength. He almost dug into the ground and scratched himself up as he got up, urgently looking around.

“Ch-Chung Myung! Chung Myung!”

Chung Myung was nowhere to be seen. Baek Cheon, who imagined the outcome he never wanted to think of, shouted at the top of his lungs.

“Chung Myung! You bastard!”

“Sahyung!”

At that moment, Yu Yiseol’s urgent voice pierced his ear.

“There!”

Baek Cheon hurriedly turned to where she pointed. The earth was massively gouged as if torn by a heavenly god. In the middle, two red and black dots were visible.

“Ch-Chung Myung!”

“Alliance lord!”

Shouts burst simultaneously from Baek Cheon and Ho Ga-Myung. Both began running at full speed toward the fallen Chung Myung and Jang Ilso.

At that moment, Chung Myung’s lips, half-buried in dirt and utterly battered, twitched faintly.

“Ugh...”

As his cracked lips parted, the wounds that had barely stuck together tore open again, and red blood flowed down.

“Ugh...”

He barely lifted his heavy eyelids and saw Jang Ilso, also half-buried in the ground, his head tilted.

Silently watching that figure, Chung Myung strained his exhausted voice after a long struggle.

“...Hey.”

No answer came.

“Hey.”

At that moment, a voice without any strength leaked from Jang Ilso’s mouth, who was tilted as if dead.

“...What?”

Hearing that voice, Chung Myung frowned.

“Are you alive?”

Then a weak voice came from Jang Ilso’s mouth. A feeble voice that seemed impossible to come from him.

“...Maybe... I’m dead...?”

“Is that so...?”

Chung Myung painfully turned his body. Forcing his motionless body to lie down, he saw the sky. It was blue enough to hurt his eyes.

“That...”

The long night ended, and finally morning arrived.

“...That’s a welcome sound to hear.”

Chung Myung’s chuckling laughter slowly spread through the quietly settled morning world.

Chapter 1063: Is This How You Do It? (3)

“Chung Myung, ahhhhh!”

“Damn it! You bastard!”

The disciples of Mount Hua rushed toward Chung Myung like a gale.

“C-calm down a bit when you run... my body is trembling...”

The vibrations from their footsteps were so intense it felt as if his body was splitting, but the Mount Hua disciples didn’t seem to hear a word of it. Jo Gul, who ran the fastest, aggressively pounced on Chung Myung.

“Hey! Are you okay? Huh?”

Jo Gul grabbed Chung Myung’s neck and shook him vigorously.

“Are you not cut anywhere? Are you alright?”

“G-Gul!”

“You, brat! Why aren’t you answering?”

“You crazy bastard! You’re going to kill the kid!”

“Huh?”

Only then did Jo Gul regain his senses and look down at Chung Myung. His eyes were rolled back and he was nearly out of breath. Jo Gul quietly let go and cleared his throat.

“No... I was just worried...”

Chung Myung, who had almost lost his life not to the Heavenly Demon or the high priest but at the hands of a fellow Mount Hua disciple, looked at Jo Gul with dazed eyes and said weakly.

“...Please go and die. Please...”

Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol sighed as they supported Chung Myung by his shoulders on both sides.

“Are you okay?”

“...Do I look okay?”

“No.”

“...I don’t even have the strength to twitch a finger.”

This was no exaggeration. It felt as if all the vital energy had completely drained from his body. At this moment, even if the opponent was Jo Gul, he would have to meekly offer his neck and ask to be killed.

If there was any consolation...

“Alliance Lord! Are you alright?”

“...I’m dead, I said.”

Even the most threatening person here looked no different from him.

“cough.”

Every time Jang Ilso coughed, a gush of red blood spilled from his mouth. He was already pale white, bleeding so much it wouldn’t be surprising if he suddenly broke his neck and died.

“...What a ruthless bastard.”

Chung Myung recalled the recent situation and cursed involuntarily. Attacking even after having his throat pierced. He knew all the High priests were insane, but experiencing it again after a long time made his blood boil.

If they hadn’t pierced his throat to weaken his power, neither Jang Ilso nor Chung Myung would have remained alive; they would have turned to dust without a single piece of flesh left.

“...This madman actually pulled it off.”

And the Mount Hua disciples looked at the slumped Chung Myung with eyes mixed with many emotions.

One emotion was awe. Another was pity.

It was nothing but admiration that they finally killed the high priest who hardly looked human, but the cost was too great. To exaggerate slightly, Chung Myung’s body looked like a half-sliced chunk of meat. It was amazing he was still breathing.

Baek Cheon immediately placed his hand on Chung Myung’s lower abdomen and pushed innate qi into him.

“cough!”

Then a bucketful of dark, dead blood poured out of Chung Myung’s mouth.

“...I’m really going to die.”

“Damn bastard.”

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth as he infused his innate qi. He knew there was no other way, but still, watching Chung Myung so badly broken was painful for him.

At that moment, Un Geom, who had been silent until now, spoke.

“It’s not over yet.”

At those words, the Mount Hua disciples suddenly lifted their heads.

“Beware the remnants of the Demonic cult! We don’t know what they might do.”

The Mount Hua disciples flinched for a moment, then instantly changed their momentum and blocked the space between Chung Myung and the cult members like enraged beasts. But unlike their reaction, the cult members stood motionless as if they were statues.

“H-high priest...“

“The high priest...“

The cult members stared blankly at the blackened, scarred earth as if they had lost the world.

The defeat of Dan Ja-Kang.

It was a result they had never even considered. Having never imagined it, they had no way to respond. They just stared blankly at the traces of the fierce battle as if their souls had left them.

“High priest... the High priest.”

Jeok-Il collapsed to the ground as if his legs gave out. Then he clenched his fists as if tearing the earth.

“This...“

Grrr...

His tightly bitten lips split and bled. His gaze filled with intense hatred and anger fixed on the enemies not far away, specifically Chung Myung and Jang Ilso.

“These... damn unbelievers who won’t even be quenched by grinding and drinking them!”

His eyes reddened with bloodshot fury.

“I will kill... I will kill to avenge the high priest! Absolutely!”

As Jeok-Il gritted his teeth and stood up, the vacant-eyed cult members’ eyes suddenly filled with fierce energy. Watching this transformation, the Mount Hua disciples drew their swords simultaneously.

The Red Dogs that had run up also growled like hunting dogs protecting their master in front of a beast and stood before Jang Ilso.

“Ugh...“

Chung Myung, supported by Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol, grimaced and opened his mouth.

“First, that...“

But at that moment, Baek Cheon felt Chung Myung’s body, which had been limp like wet cotton, suddenly stiffen.

“Huh?”

Chung Myung’s gaze hurriedly turned backward. His eyes held disbelief, shock, and a twisted emotion.

“This, this bastard...“

“Chung Myung?”

Kraaaaaaang!

Before that question could even be answered, a huge explosion erupted. The Mount Hua disciples guarding against the cult members turned around in alarm.

And they too saw it. Black demonic qi erupting explosively, scattering the piled earth like the ruins of a collapsed mountain.

Cold sweat trickled down Baek Cheon’s spine.

“N-no way...“

“Kraaaaaaaaah!”

It sounded like a demon trapped in the heavenly realm’s prison howling. Amid the swirling black demonic qi, someone who should never have been there appeared.

“H-high priest...“

The color drained from Baek Cheon’s face.

Blood-red light burst from his eyes and a desperate scream escaped. Covered in black as if soaked in ink, Dan Ja-Kang revealed his withered upper body and screamed like a beast.

Everyone who saw that showed a flicker of terror on their faces.

“Ooooooooooooo!”

The Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword that Chung Myung had embedded was still diagonally stuck in his neck. Dan Ja-Kang, who had been screaming, grabbed the sword in his neck.

Crunch. Crunch.

Even amidst the roaring noise created by the swirling demonic qi, the sound of the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword being pulled from his neck was clearly heard.

Crack!

Finally, he completely pulled out the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword from his neck and threw it to the ground.

“Ugh...“

Yoon Jong grabbed his right hand holding the sword with his left hand. His hand trembled uncontrollably.

At that moment, Chung Myung muttered a small curse and struggled to lift his limp body.

“...Did I miss it?”

It seemed he hadn’t completely severed the neck bone by a hair’s breadth. Dan Ja-Kang, who had mastered Skeleton Demonic Arts, could survive even with such a severe wound.

“Sasuk. My sword...“

“Don’t talk nonsense! You crazy bastard!”

Baek Cheon and the other Mount Hua disciples overcame the intense fear rising from deep within and blocked Chung Myung even more firmly.

‘No more!’

Chung Myung could no longer fight. He must not fight. Then they had to protect this damn guy. Without a word, everyone gripped their swords tightly with the same thought.

Dan Ja-Kang, who had become a form no longer resembling a human, raised his head and roared.

“Whyyyyyyyyyyyy!”

It sounded so desperate it was almost sorrowful. Like a child who lost its mother wailing.

“Why! Why do you not look back at us! Whyyyyyyyy!”

The demonic qi Dan Ja-Kang emitted swirled violently.

“Heavenly Demon Lordddd!”

His voice was now as rough as scraping metal.

“You! Even though there are those who wait for you so desperately, why! Why do you ignore us! Heavenly Demon! Why! Heavenly Demon!”

‘He’s gone mad...’

Tang Soso was so shocked she instinctively covered her mouth.

Even using all known languages, it seemed impossible to fully describe that madness. It was hardly an emotion a human could possess.

“If even this is not enough! If this is insufficient! What more shall we do! Does this scream not reach you!”

Dan Ja-Kang’s gaze, emitting streams of blood-red light, turned toward Chung Myung.

“Can you understand? Our anguish! Our pain! Can scum like you understand!”

Chung Myung grabbed Baek Cheon’s shoulder and stepped forward.

“Chung Myung!”

His legs trembled so much it was hard to take a step. But Chung Myung pushed them aside and stepped forward.

And as if it were natural, Jang Ilso was already stepping forward alongside him.

As if it had to be done. No matter the condition, when facing the enemy, there was only one thing to do.

“That was a bit lacking.”

“Seems so.”

“Can’t be helped.”

Chung Myung and Jang Ilso bared their teeth simultaneously.

“If he comes back to life... we’ll just have to kill him again and again!”

“Heh heh heh heh.”

The Mount Hua disciples and the Red Dogs didn’t back down this time and lined up on both sides of the two. It was as if they were ready to fight together.

Then Dan Ja-Kang’s demonic qi grew darker.

Dan Ja-Kang instinctively understood. Having pushed his demonic qi beyond the limit, he would never return to his former self.

He would probably become a madman forever engulfed in this madness, massacring everything he saw.

But if his desperate voice could reach the Heavenly Demon through this, he would accept even that fate without resistance.

Dan Ja-Kang drew all his remaining qi. The terrifying demonic qi wrapped around his body and soared into the sky.

“Ugh...“

Before that overwhelming might, suppressed groans escaped from the Mount Hua disciples’ mouths.

‘He still has that much power...’

Just as Baek Cheon bit his lip to calm his trembling jaw.

‘Huh?’

At first, he thought he had seen it wrong.

Behind the swirling black demonic qi, something whitish flickered faintly. He thought he might have hallucinated due to fear.

But the next moment, Baek Cheon realized he hadn’t been mistaken.

Right behind Dan Ja-Kang, in the storm of demonic qi swirling fiercely enough to crumple even eternal cold iron like paper, a man stood like a ghost. Dressed in a white robe, no one knew where he suddenly appeared from or when he had been there.

It was truly an alien sight.

Baek Cheon, unable to accept the situation, stared blankly at the scene, when the man in the white robe behind Dan Ja-Kang gave a faint smile.

“This is why I...“

Only then did Dan Ja-Kang realize someone was behind him and instinctively turned his head. Or tried to.

But before his head could move, the man in the white robe pierced Dan Ja-Kang’s back with his hand.

Kwadadadadak!

“Kraaaaaaah!”

A scream full of pain burst from Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth.

Then he lowered his eyes filled with horror and looked at his chest. A hand, pure white, pierced through his blackened chest.

“Ugh... ugh...?”

A sound like air escaping came from his mouth.

Dan Ja-Kang, unable to believe the situation, looked down at his hand and finally trembled as he turned around. When his eyes met the man who had pierced his back, a huge terror began to spread across his face.

“This...“

Dan Ja-Kang’s voice trembled unbelievably. His appearance was unimaginable from what he had shown so far. But even the Mount Hua disciples could clearly hear the tremble in his voice.

“Y-you, Second High Priest...“

The man called the Second High Priest clicked his tongue lightly and continued.

“This is why I don’t like you young punks like you.”

Kwadadadak!

The man’s arm dug deeper into Dan Ja-Kang’s chest. Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth opened wide as if it would tear.

Despite his severe injury, Dan Ja-Kang, who was emitting powerful demonic qi, was now being easily crushed by the man who suddenly appeared from nowhere.

Baek Cheon, not fully understanding the situation, glanced at Chung Myung and was met with an even more astonishing sight.

Chung Myung, who had never shown confusion in front of an enemy, looked pale white.

“Heaven...“

A voice as if possessed escaped from Chung Myung’s mouth.

“Heaven Killer...”

Chapter 1064: Is This How You Do It? (4)

“Gurgle... gurgle...”

Thick blood foam sputtered from Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth as his body convulsed relentlessly, as if unbearable pain coursed through him.

The scene was inevitably shocking to everyone present. It was neither understandable nor acceptable.

No matter how abnormal his state was after being attacked by Chung Myung, Dan Ja-Kang was still Dan Ja-Kang. How could anyone comprehend or accept a man who had subdued the ruthless High Priest—who had driven both Chung Myung and Jang Ilso to the brink of death with a single strike?

“Uh... how...”

Every time Dan Ja-Kang moved his lips, the sound of boiling blood bubbles escaped. It was so faint that without careful listening, one could hardly realize he was trying to speak.

However, the man dressed in a white robe, the Heaven Killing Demon Emperor, smiled gently as if he understood what Dan Ja-Kang was attempting to say. His image, casually laughing with his hand plunged into a human body, etched chillingly into the minds of the onlookers.

“Are you asking how I am here?”

“Gurgle, gurgle...”

Heavenly Kill lightly shook his head and sighed, as if feeling pity.

“Child.”

“...”

“Why don’t you understand? You doubted the doctrine and the Heavenly Demon, so why didn’t you doubt that obvious question?”

“...”

“The Heavenly Demon never told us not to head to the Central Plains. He is not the kind of person to say such things. People may give food to ants, but they do not command the ants. Isn’t that so?”

Dan Ja-Kang’s body began to tremble more violently at these words. His eyes, already devoid of bloodlust, involuntarily turned toward Chung Myung.

After staring intently at Chung Myung for a long moment, Dan Ja-Kang’s lips emitted a trembling voice again.

“Th-then... then why...”

“Tsk tsk. This is why you don’t appoint an unqualified young brat to the High Priest’s position. I wonder what the Great Priest is so urgent about to do such rushed things.”

“...”

Heavenly Kill smiled faintly.

“Logic is unnecessary in serving him. What we want is obedience itself, not the reward he might give us. Do you understand?”

“Th-that...”

Bloodied, Dan Ja-Kang grabbed the Heavenly Demon’s hand that had pierced through his chest.

“Guh... guuuuh!”

Then, tearing at his own body rather than Heavenly Kill’s hand, he pulled out Heaven Killer’s hand. Having barely freed himself from Heaven Killer’s grip in a horrific and shocking way, Dan Ja-Kang collapsed mercilessly to the ground.

“Oho?”

Seeing that, Heaven Killer laughed heartily.

“Indeed. So you still have the right to bear the name of High Priest?”

Dan Ja-Kang, groaning on the ground, forced himself to lift his head. His eyes, filled with murderous hatred toward the man, glared at Heaven Killer.

“Then...”

Dan Ja-Kang shouted desperately with bloodshot eyes.

“Then what exactly did we endure all that time for? Answer me, High Priest! If that was not the Heavenly Demon’s command, why did we waste our lives bound by doctrines we didn’t even need to uphold? Answer me!”

“Hmm.”

“Answer meeeee!”

Even hearing that agonized roar, Heaven Killer’s eyes did not move. Instead, they grew colder.

“Why should I explain that to you?”

“...”

“To a filthy apostate like you.”

Dan Ja-Kang looked dazed, as if struck on the back of the head.

“Apostate?”

He spoke in a daze, as if hearing words he should never have associated with himself.

“...I’m an apostate? I... am I...?”

“Didn’t you know?”

Heaven Killer smiled brightly.

“You doubted the Heavenly Demon’s teachings, doubted his divinity, broke the doctrine which spoke of him, and led followers to the Central Plains. If you are not an apostate, then who in the world can be called one?”

“Th-that teaching is all false!”

“And who told you that?”

Dan Ja-Kang was momentarily at a loss for words and could not answer.

“Who told you all that was false? Have you ever seen the Heavenly Demon yourself?”

“You... you...”

Hearing such nonsense unworthy of a reply, Dan Ja-Kang trembled all over. Heaven Killer took a leisurely step toward him.

“Child. You will never understand.”

Another step.

“You have lost that chance to understand forever. Those who have seen the God with their own eyes cannot converse with those who have not. You will now lose the chance to see the God with those eyes forever.”

“I...”

Dan Ja-Kang’s body collapsed weakly. The pain from the word ‘apostate’ seemed greater than the physical agony piercing his flesh.

“I! I did not apostatize! I did not abandon my faith. You are not the Heavenly Demon’s agent! My faith is only...!”

“Shut up.”

Heaven Killer cut him off as if tired of listening to the mumbling. At the same time, a tiny black flame blossomed at his fingertips and flew like scattered embers, landing on Dan Ja-Kang’s forehead.

Kwahhhhh!

At that moment, an intense black flame engulfed Dan Ja-Kang’s entire body and soared.

“Guaaaahhhhh!”

A desperate scream, unbearable to hear calmly, echoed across the desolate land where almost nothing alive remained.

“There is only one death given to apostates.”

An emotionless voice flowed from the Heavenly Demon’s mouth.

“Die feeling the utmost pain you can endure. Your soul will never be saved even after death. Forever, eternally, wander the Nine Nethers regretting and regretting the sins you committed.”

The karmic fire punishment that can only be inflicted on those who have mastered martial arts.

“Guaaaahhhhh!”

The demonic qi backfired, burning everything about him to ashes.

Even the strong Dan Ja-Kang writhed and screamed on the ground in agony. He screamed, sobbed like a child, and twisted his whole body.

No one dared to speak rashly. Even the disciples of Mount Hua, who only felt hatred toward Dan Ja-Kang, could not bear to watch and averted their eyes.

However, the only emotion in Heaven Killer’s eyes was cold malice. In the cult, unbelievers must be killed, but apostates are treated as even filthier than unbelievers.

“Guaaaah! I, I... I am...!”

A voice filled with pain and anguish flowed like a wail from Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth, covered in black flames.

He did not die easily despite such terrible pain. Because he could not die, it was cruel; because it was cruel, it was hell. That is why the cult uses this as punishment for apostates.

“I...”

Dan Ja-Kang’s body slowly crumbled.

“I... am not an ap... apostate... I...”

His faint voice flowed out like a groan.

“Heavenly Demon... Oh Heavenly Demon... why... why...”

“Tsk.”

“Why... my desperate cry...”

Crack!

Heaven Killer mercilessly stomped and crushed Dan Ja-Kang’s nearly burnt head. Then, frowning as if stepping on something filthy, he lifted his foot.

“It’s disgusting to listen to this any longer.”

Dan Ja-Kang.

It was an unbelievably miserable death for the Cult’s High Priest who had turned Hangzhou into hell and driven the great Chung Myung and Jang Ilso to the brink of their deaths.

Having delivered that chilling death, Heaven Killer averted his gaze from Dan Ja-Kang as if he had no more business here. Then he looked elsewhere.

Flinch.

The cult followers before his cold gaze immediately bowed their heads to the ground. They all trembled like aspens, as if they had met the King of the Underworld.

At the moment Heaven Killer frowned in displeasure, Jeok-Il, who was bowing, crawled forward on his belly.

“I, Jeok-Il, the Enforcing Guard, dares to greet the second High Priest.”

His voice was filled with earnestness. It felt more intense than the pain of losing Dan Ja-Kang or the fear of facing Heaven Killer.

“All of this is my fault for failing to serve the High Priest properly. Please punish me by taking my life and forgive these others with a heart as vast as the ocean! This humble one dares, dares to plead.”

Thud! Thud! Thud!

Jeok-Il repeatedly banged his head on the floor. But Heaven Killer watched the scene with the same indifferent eyes.

“...Responsibility.”

Heaven Killer murmured softly and chuckled. Even that faint, low laugh made Jeok-Il tremble as if struck by lightning.

“Is it because we entrusted the Cult to someone unqualified? The Cult is a mess. How dare a mere Enforcing Guard talk about responsibility.”

Jeok-Il, not knowing what to say, just held his breath like a corpse.

“No fool punishes the hand for making a mistake. Moreover, punishing an Enforcing Guard who is not even worth the hand is meaningless.”

“...”

“You and the followers will not be held accountable for the High Priest’s sins. A mere Enforcing Guard could never go against the High Priest’s will.”

“Th-thank you...”

“However.”

Heaven Killer spoke in an emotionless voice.

“Shouldn’t the Enforcing Guard pay the price for foolishly discussing responsibility without knowing his place?”

Jeok-Il’s pupils trembled. When he barely raised his head and saw Heavenly Demon’s faint smile, he realized what he must do.

“Hea... Heavenly Demon...”

Biting his lips tightly, he shouted at the top of his lungs.

“Heavenly Demon’s Second Coming! Ten thousand demon blessings!”

Crack!

Then Jeok-Il smashed his own head with his hands in one blow.

Thud.

Jeok-Il, now a headless corpse, collapsed to the ground instantly.

Heaven Killer, watching the scene with displeased eyes, turned his gaze to the bowing followers.

“Return to the cult.”

“Hea, Heavenly Demon’s reincarnation...”

“Shut up.”

Everyone held their breath.

“Though you bear no doctrinal responsibility, the sin of following an apostate cannot be judged by doctrine alone. For thirty-three sunrises and sunsets, remain silent and reflect on your own faults.”

Thud! Thud! Thud!

Unable to defy those words, the followers smashed their heads on the ground without reply. Then, they rose simultaneously and began moving in one direction as if hypnotized.

Heaven Killer’s cold gaze subtly shifted toward his feet as he watched the scene.

“Hmm.”

Dan Ja-Kang was now nothing but black ashes, leaving only traces. A strange expression flickered across Heaven Killer’s face. He cast his gaze toward the tense disciples of Mount Hua.

“Indeed...”

A voice filled with admiration flowed out.

“The Central Plains is an interesting place. No matter how much of a foolish brat he is... I never thought the High Priest would end up like this.”

Smiling as if pleased at what he witnessed, he looked at Chung Myung and Jang Il-so.

His gaze, which had shown no particular hostility, changed instantly at that moment. When Heavenly Killer’s eyes, which could have been described as gentle, landed on Chung Myung, they turned into a completely different hue.

“You...?”

Heaven Killer’s eyes filled with clear hostility and killing intent as he confirmed Chung Myung and the group standing protectively around him. Even a sinister aura, never before shown on his face, began to emerge.

Gnash.

The sound of grinding teeth echoed chillingly.

“Mount Hua... that cursed sect?”

Heavenly Demon’s growling voice silenced even the sky.

Chapter 1065: Is This How You Do It? (5)

Thoughts gradually faded and disappeared.

It wasn’t about fear or trembling. The moment they faced the killing intent radiating from Heaven Killer, all thoughts vanished from their minds.

This killing intent made everything they had endured and overcome until now seem like child’s play. Before that overwhelming pressure, all living beings held their breath.

Intent-Form Killing.

It was the moment they were forced to understand why the phrase “killing with will alone” existed.

Heaven Killer’s gaze, reminiscent of the biting north wind from the North Sea, slowly swept over everyone remaining there.

It felt as if a person filled with intense malice was pressing an incredibly sharp blade against their neck. The pressure was such that the slightest finger movement would cause the blade to pierce their throat instantly. To those who didn’t know what to do, Heaven Killer’s voice murmured into their ears.

“Mount Hua... yes. It is Mount Hua. You have still managed to maintain your lineage well.”

From that reaction alone, it was clear that this newly appeared high priest harbored a deep grudge against the Mount Hua Sect.

And the result of that grudge... anyone could predict.

Because Chung Myung and Jang Ilso, already worn out from fighting Dan Ja-Kang, could never handle that high priest. If it was impossible for those two, it was impossible for anyone here.

There was only one path left: escape.

It was better to survive by turning their backs than to resist meaninglessly against an unbeatable enemy. Wasn’t that what Mount Hua had repeatedly emphasized to its disciples?

But...

‘Is it really possible?’

To run away from that high priest?

They couldn’t even continue thinking, much less attempt anything. All they could do was barely avoid collapsing on the spot. But how could they possibly escape from someone like that?

But just then, amidst the intense pressure, someone began to move. It wasn’t Chung Myung, Baek Cheon, or even Jang Il-so, but someone else.

Step. Step.

All eyes poured onto the back of the man walking forward through the stiffened crowd.

A back not very broad.

One sleeve awkwardly empty and fluttering in the wind.

“Sa-Sasuk...”

A strained voice escaped from Baek Cheon’s mouth. His sasuk, Un Geom, had moved forward alone.

Baek Cheon swallowed dryly without realizing it.

‘Un Geom Sasuk!’

How could that be? It was already hard enough to endure without losing their minds in front of such deadly killing intent, so how could he move like that?

Baek Cheon looked at Un Geom with near horror.

Un Geom took his final step and stood facing Heaven Killer. Trembling as if it was difficult to straighten his body, he opened his mouth with a suppressed voice.

“Great... Mount Hua sect.”

Those standing behind couldn’t see Un Geom’s expression. All they could see were his trembling shoulders and hear his voice. It was hardly a confident appearance.

But who would dare belittle that back? Un Geom standing alone facing Heaven Killer looked even more impressive than Heaven Killer himself who had intimidated them, at least to the eyes of Mount Hua disciples.

“...I am called Un Geom, the First class disciple.”

Heaven Killer silently glared at Un Geom with cold eyes. At that moment, the intense pressure that weighed on the others lessened. But that meant even greater pressure was now placed on Un Geom.

But Un Geom continued speaking to the end.

“...Do you have business with Mount Hua?”

“Ha...?”

Heaven Killer let out a hollow laugh as if incredulous.

Of course, they might not know what happened between Mount Hua and the Demonic Cult in the past. But even so, wasn’t this question utterly unreasonable?

They had just been at war with the Demonic Cult, pushing the High priest Dan Ja-Kang to the brink of death. And now, they were asking Heaven Killer about business?

“Hahahahaha!”

Heaven Killer finally burst into loud laughter.

“Business...”

Then suddenly wiping away his laughter, he coldly glared at Un Geom and asked.

“Then what do you intend to do if I do?”

“...”

“If I decided to kill you all right here and now, would you try to stop me, descendant of Mount Hua?”

“...”

“With that feeble abilities?”

Heaven Killer slowly shook his head as if emphasizing the point.

“It’s absurd. You may bear the name of Mount Hua, but you possess none of its power. Compared to those I had to respect even as enemies, you are less than a worm. Yet you dare stand before me?”

At that moment, the overwhelming killing intent from Heaven Killer was fully directed at Un Geom.

“You who have inherited nothing but the two words ‘Mount Hua’?”

“Gulp!”

Black blood gushed from Un Geom’s mouth. The killing intent, too intense to describe, completely shook his qi.

Un Geom, wounded internally by the pressure alone, coughed up black blood several times before wiping his mouth with his remaining arm.

“...It seems there has been a misunderstanding.”

Though his face was pale from internal injuries, Un Geom’s expression looked somewhat more at ease than before.

“I also know my place. I don’t dream such high dreams.”

“Then why did you dare stand before me?”

“...You are also someone in a higher position, so you would understand.”

“Hmm?”

Un Geom smiled faintly with much difficulty.

“Even if my ability is lacking, if I have those who follow me... at least I must die first.”

Un Geom gripped the sword at his waist. His hand no longer trembled.

“As you said, my ability may be less than a worm in your eyes. But people can also die from a worm’s bite at times. If I’m lucky, I might save even one more person.”

Heaven Killer’s eyes narrowed.

“Is your life worth only that much?”

“Only that much...?”

Un Geom bared his teeth and laughed. His expression was endlessly bright, but the blood-soaked figure evoked a strange eeriness.

“Where else is there a matter more valuable than that?”

Heaven Killer silently watched Un Geom for a moment, seemingly intrigued.

Just by looking, one could tell that this was all Un Geom could do now. Yet he never backed down. He was literally enduring while chipping away at his soul.

Heaven Killer slowly nodded.

“You have not inherited only the name I see.”

Heaven Killer took his gaze off Un Geom and looked at the distant sky, as if recalling old memories.

“Even after a hundred years, even if its power is lost, Mount Hua is still Mount Hua...”

Heaven Killer’s expression softened momentarily. At the same time, the intense killing intent that seemed ready to slash everything around vanished as if washed away.

“I got a little excited.”

Everyone looked at Heaven Killer with suspicious faces. Heaven Killer slowly shook his head and said.

“No need to look at me like that. I will not harm you here today.”

“...”
