“Of course, don’t misunderstand my actions. The cult shows no mercy to lowly unbelievers. Especially those who use the name Mount Hua with theirs—I honestly don’t want to spare even an ant. But...”

Heaven Killer glanced elsewhere. There lay the scattered ashes of Dan Ja-Kang.

“Everyone has their positions assigned.”

“...”

“Erasing Mount Hua’s traces from the world is an unavoidable duty given to me, but inheriting what the apostate intended is something a Cultist must avoid. So you will not die. At least not here today.”

“Heh heh heh heh.”

At that moment, a low laugh burst out.

Heaven Killer’s gaze naturally turned toward the source of the laughter. A man dressed outrageously was covering his mouth as if struggling to hold back laughter.

Heaven Killer silently stared at him, and Jang Ilso waved his hand.

“Ah, sorry. You were speaking seriously.”

“...”

“But I’m a bit twisted. When someone acts like they hold my life by the throat, it’s hard to hold back laughter.”

Heaven Killer narrowed his eyes at Jang Il-so and shook his head.

“The Central Plains have changed a lot.”

“...”

“Yes, enough time has passed. A hundred years have gone by, enough to forget us.”

Heaven Killer slowly looked around.

A land of unparalleled devastation. Almost nothing remained, but he could clearly feel the aura of death that had engulfed this place.

A city had vanished. All lives within it were gone. But...

“Don’t think this is all there is. What you have seen is only a fragment of the Cult’s power.”

“...”

“You will know. Even if you don’t want to remember the past, you will recall it. Unbelievers of the Central Plains.”

At that moment, an indescribable solemnity blossomed on Heaven Killer’s face.

“Soon, HE will return.”

Everyone’s hearts dropped.

Who here would not understand who “HE” referred to from those words? Among those in the Demonic Cult, only one being could make even a High priest so reverent.

“Then, you will face it. The predetermined outcome. An unavoidable fate.”

Heaven Killer turned around.

“Until then... enjoy the remaining time given to you. Though it may not be much.”

Heaven Killer moved forward indifferently. Or rather, tried to.

But what grabbed his ankle was a flat voice that pierced his ear.

“That’s not it, is it?”

Heaven Killer stopped and glanced back without moving his body. There stood a young Mount Hua swordsman, still bearing a youthful innocence.

Chung Myung spoke softly.

“It’s not that you don’t kill, but that you can’t kill. Isn’t that right?”

“...”

“What should we do about this?”

The teasing voice seemed to pierce Heaven Killer’s heart.

“You never know. Among the people killed here by those who ran wild because you couldn’t control them... there might have been that proud Heavenly Demon brat? Isn’t that right?”

“You...”

Grinding teeth!

Heaven Killer gritted his teeth. Suddenly, his eyes bloodshot—not because of martial energy, but purely from overwhelming rage causing the veins in his eyes to burst.

“Hahahahaha!”

Seeing that, Chung Myung laughed like a madman.

“Are you angry?”

“...You, bastard...”

“Then what will you do to me? Try to kill me?”

Everyone could clearly feel the tremendous anger Heaven Killer emitted. But unlike before, his anger was not directed as hostility toward them.

“No. You can’t.”

Chung Myung sneered crookedly.

“I am.”

“Eee...”

“I might be the Heavenly Demon for all, you know? Isn’t that right?”

“BRATTTTTTT!”

A scream filled with deep resentment, intense malice, and foul-smelling grudge erupted. Red blood trickled down from the corner of Heaven Killer’s eye.

Chapter 1066: I Said Try To Kill Me? (1)

It was like witnessing a glass mask shatter with a sharp crack.

The calmness, disguised as gentleness, crumbled, revealing fierce hatred. Seeing Heaven Killer’s face change in an instant, Chung Myung burst out laughing gleefully.

The reason Chung Myung was never fooled by the lie—so convincingly wrapped that it almost deceived him for a moment—was very simple.

‘He’s not that kind of guy.’

Because he knew what kind of person Heaven Killer really was.

The Heaven Killer Chung Myung remembered was someone who showed no mercy not only to enemies but also to allies. Literally, Heaven Killer (Heavenly Murders) was a being filled with murderous intent and hostility toward all that exists in and around them.

Even after a hundred years, his essence would not have changed. Yet, would such a Heaven Killer simply let everyone here go and retreat for such a trivial reason?

‘That’s ridiculous.’

Then there was only one reason: he had a reason to spare them while uttering empty words he didn’t mean.

And considering that, everything became clear.

“Why? Was I wrong?”

Chung Myung smirked. It was a clear provocation, but Heaven Killer seemed to regain his composure quickly. It seemed the hundred years had not been wasted.

But Chung Myung had no reason to regret it. He had already confirmed everything he needed to.

The answer to the question that had troubled Chung Myung until now.

How exactly does the Heavenly Demon return to the world?

‘It’s no different from me.’

Something he could guess but not be sure of. The answer to that question lay in Heaven Killer’s reaction now.

“Returns?”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“I’m curious whether that’s really words spoken with knowledge, or...”

Chung Myung’s meaningful smile pierced Heaven Killer without mercy.

“Or just an unfounded belief.”

Heaven Killer’s expression vanished. But Chung Myung could clearly read the emptiness of a fanatic who had lost his god.

Chung Myung himself had also returned from death.

What was noteworthy in that process was that before Chung Myung became aware of himself, he definitely lived in this world under the name Cho Sam.

At some point, Cho Sam became Chung Myung.

This was the crucial point.

What if the Heavenly Demon, that very Heavenly Demon, underwent the same process as Chung Myung?

‘It means the Heavenly Demon... might be living in this world as a nameless villager.’

What if, in such a state, he was attacked by the Demonic Cult? What if the cultists, who could not recognize him as the Heavenly Demon, attacked him? What would happen?

Would the Heavenly Demon in crisis regain himself and awaken like Chung Myung did? Or would he remain unawakened and simply die as a nameless commoner in their hands?

It’s unknown. The only certainty is that the latter possibility cannot be ruled out.

And judging by Heaven Killer’s reaction, they had no way to find the Heavenly Demon until he awakened.

Unlike the Podalap Palace cultivating the position of Banseon lama to find the reincarnation of the Dalai Lama, they had no choice but to wait.

‘Therefore, the Demonic Cult can never attack the Central Plains.’

Cultists who cannot confirm the existence of the Heavenly Demon cannot kill people. The only ones they can kill are those who were alive before the Heavenly Demon died.

But how many such people remain in the world now?

As time passes, cultists have no choice but to restrain themselves from killing people. They might inadvertently kill the Heavenly Demon who will be resurrected by their own hands—a terrible situation that must never happen from the cult’s perspective.

For those who worship the Heavenly Demon, that is an unforgivable sin a hundred or even a thousand times over. That is why none other than Heaven Killer himself hurried all the way here.

To stop the massacre Dan Ja-Kang was committing.

The weight of the name Heaven Killer was too heavy to come all the way here just to stop a young bishop named Dan Ja-Kang. What Heaven Killer tried to stop was not Dan Ja-Kang’s deviation but the act of massacre itself.

“Heh heh heh heh.”

Chung Myung laughed as if incredulous.

“That must have been hard.”

“...”

“To say you must kill everything alive in the Central Plains, yet make it so you can never kill them. Is there anything more ridiculous than this?”

Heaven Killer’s face twisted horribly.

“Yes. It must have been hard to say. To say the Heavenly Demon might be living as some commoner denies the Heavenly Demon’s perfection. I damn well understand the hardship of having to worship the imperfect as if perfect.”

“Shut up...”

“How pitiful. I thought your ordeal was just endless waiting...”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“To think you live a pitiful life unable to lift a finger even before the enemies who killed your god.”

“You BRATTTT!”

Heaven Killer shouted with a face twisted like a demonic fiend, but Chung Myung didn’t even blink and calmly mocked him.

Even if no one else knows, Chung Myung knows what the Heavenly Demon means to the cultists.

Even if killing Chung Myung would harm the Heavenly Demon with a probability less than one in ten million, like picking a single grain of sand from a vast desert, they dare not act on it.

A faith beyond reason. That is the essence of the Demonic Cult.

“Are you angry?”

That is why Chung Myung sneered at those pitiful fanatics who knew the Heavenly Demon well but failed to see his true nature.

Step.

Chung Myung approached Heaven Killer, passing Baek Cheon and Un Geom without hesitation.

The faces of Mount Hua disciples who saw the scene began to turn pale.

‘Th-that...’

‘That crazy bastard!’

But no one dared to stop him. To intervene in the confrontation between Chung Myung and Heaven Killer was impossible for them now.

Unaware or uncaring of those watching from behind, Chung Myung walked calmly.

Step.

Finally, he stood before Heaven Killer. No, just ‘before’ was insufficient. At a distance almost close to touching, Chung Myung faced Heaven Killer nose to nose, curling his lips into a smirk.

“Then try to kill me.”

“Ugh...”

“I said, try to kill me.”

Heaven Killer’s body began to tremble. He could not control the extreme anger rising within at the taunts.

He had probably crushed Chung Myung’s head hundreds of times in his mind, but Heaven Killer’s hands never moved.

This was Heaven Killer.

Born with the fate of the Heaven Killer nature that instinctively craves blood and death. For him, restraining from killing was like enduring a burning thirst.

Heaven Killer had endured that long thirst for over a hundred years without a drop of water.

A fierce faith. No, at this point, it was more fitting to call it a miraculous faith.

“What?”

But to Chung Myung, that faith meant nothing.

Chung Myung whispered softly into Heaven Killer’s ear, like the devil tempting a meditating Buddhist.

“I said, try to kill me?”

“...”

“Heh heh heh heh.”

Heaven Killer could not move, fearing that the already battered Chung Myung might kill him. He couldn’t even freely emit his killing intent. Chung Myung mocked him fiercely.

“Why? Can’t do it?”

Heaven Killer’s hands twitched. His pitifully trembling hands moved violently for a moment, then returned to their place repeatedly. Bloody tears flowed from his reddened eyes.

“You filthy... filthy unbeliever...”

“Yeah. I’m a filthy unbeliever, you know?”

Chung Myung bared his teeth and laughed.

“And you’re an idiot who can’t even kill an unbeliever worse than a fly.”

Chung Myung’s sneer was etched into Heaven Killer’s eyes.

“Isn’t that so?”

Heaven Killer glared at Chung Myung with a face twisted like a demon. Red blood oozed from his swollen lips. It wasn’t from biting his lips but from severe internal damage caused by the overwhelming qi.

At that moment, Chung Myung reached out and grabbed Heaven Killer by the collar.

“Listen well, you idiot bastard.”

Chung Myung pulled Heaven Killer’s head sharply forward, growling as he locked eyes with him.

“Don’t open your mouth like you’re something great when you can’t even lift a finger. Get lost. Just breathing the same air as your cult bastards makes me want to vomit.”

“Ugh...”

After finishing, Chung Myung pushed Heaven Killer away forcefully.

Heaven Killer couldn’t even resist and stepped back. His face was a mix of rage, hatred, and humiliation. Gritting his teeth, he spoke with difficulty.

“You... you will definitely...”

A fierce curse spilled out.

“You will surely regret this act you pulled. On the day HE descends, I will personally tear you apart without leaving a single piece of flesh. No! I will make you beg to be killed by my hands! Surely! Surely! I will stake everything to bring you and Mount Hua unbearable pain! Surely!”

“...”

“No matter where you run in this world, I will find you and make you neither live nor die! Remember, disciple of Mount Hua! You will deeply know the depth and intensity of the cult’s wrath!”

The venomous hatred seemed alive and vivid to Chung Myung. Yet, he only coldly laughed at the hellish curse.

“Ah, really?”

A smile lingered in his eyes as he looked at Heaven Killer.

“That’s your wish. But you misunderstand one thing.”

“...”

“You don’t have to bother looking hard. If the Heavenly Demon bastard returns and you set foot on the Central Plains again,”

A cold declaration flowed from Chung Myung’s lips.

“You will face me and Mount Hua first.”

Heaven Killer’s eyes filled with hatred and Chung Myung’s cold gaze clashed fiercely in the air.

“So get lost now, you stupid bastard. Tremble in the cold land where no trace of humans remains, waiting for the Heavenly Demon who may never return. That will be the only way to prove your faith.”

With those words, Chung Myung turned without waiting for a reply, without hesitation.

Even as Chung Myung showed his back, Heaven Killer could not strike him once. He just trembled as if about to explode.

“...Your name?”

Amid the intense hatred that seemed to burst his brain, Heaven Killer barely managed to ask. Chung Myung didn’t even turn his head, letting out a strange smile.

“Chung Myung.”

“...Chung Myung?”

“Why? Sounds strange?”

“...”

“It’s good for a fool like you. You’ll never forget it.”

Heaven Killer looked at Chung Myung’s back with cold eyes and nodded.

“The Cult... will remember you.”

The image of Heaven Killer glaring at Chung Myung with hellish demon eyes soon vanished as if it were an illusion.

Mount Hua disciples, sensing Heaven Killer’s presence completely gone, collapsed weakly on the spot.

Thud.

Baek Cheon, who had completely given up dignity and sat down, murmured with a stunned face as Chung Myung calmly walked toward them. It was a phrase representing everyone’s feelings.

“That... that crazy lunatic bastard...”

No one dared to refute those words.

Chapter 1067: I Said Try To Kill Me? (2)

Jo Gul stared blankly at Chung Myung, unable even to think about wiping the cold sweat streaming down his face.

“...No.”

Of course, he knew he was crazy. Which disciple of Mount Hua wouldn’t know that bastard was out of his mind?

But even madness has its limits. He never imagined it could reach that extent.

‘No, I still thought...’

Until now, whenever that bastard did something crazy, Jo Gul believed there was some calculation behind it. He never imagined Chung Myung could be so reckless.

“...Crazy bastard.”

It was Yoon Jong who said it. When those words come from him, everything is said. How terrible the situation was is proven by Baek Cheon, who had collapsed, and Yu Yiseol, who lay exhausted on the ground like a cat.

“It would be more sensible to shout ‘Put your head in the tiger’s mouth and try to get eaten!’”

At that moment, Tang Soso muttered in a dazed voice.

“That lunatic... if he’s going to die, he should die alone. Why drag us down too...”

Jo Gul flinched and turned around. Even though Mount Hua has no hierarchy, calling your sahyung a lunatic was a bit much. Since Yoon Jong heard it, he might get angry.

“Exactly!”

Nope. Looks like the senior brother is really angry.

And it seemed that not only Mount Hua disciples were exhausted from Chung Myung’s madness.

“I...”

All the exhausted ones turned their gaze simultaneously to where the voice came from. If it had been someone else, they wouldn’t have had the strength to turn their heads, but even in this situation, Jang Ilso’s reaction could not be ignored.

Unable to collapse like the Mount Hua disciples, Jang Ilso stood upright with a look of disbelief on his face. His gaze was fixed on Chung Myung walking toward him.

“I thought I was the craziest man in this murim world...”

“...”

“There seems to be someone even crazier.”

The Mount Hua disciples who heard that looked at each other with faces that could neither laugh nor cry. They never thought the moment would come when they would agree with Jang Ilso’s words.

Jang Ilso’s face, paler than usual, seemed to show that his earlier words were not just a joke.

In contrast, Chung Myung’s face approaching was calm as if everything was someone else’s problem. He even clicked his tongue at Baek Cheon and his group.

“Honestly, anyone would think you did something great. What are you sitting down for!”

“Hey, you...”

“Taoist... Taoist, please just go far away and die. Please...”

The Mount Hua disciples, with no strength left to shout, only cursed weakly.

Still, Baek Cheon, claiming to be a sasuk to them, was the first to stagger to his feet and ask.

“What on earth...”

“Huh?”

“What happened? Why did that guy leave so obediently?”

They had overheard the conversation but without prior knowledge, they couldn’t understand anything at all.

“It’s nothing special...”

Chung Myung’s gaze briefly met Jang Ilso’s.

“I’ll tell you later.”

Baek Cheon nodded understandingly, but Jang Ilso spoke with a hint of disappointment.

“I thought we were comrades who fought together. Won’t it hurt my fragile heart to be so blatantly left out?”

His voice was soft, almost friendly, but Chung Myung’s response was icy cold.

“The moment I use the word comrade with a bastard like you will never come.”

“I’d call that a harsh answer... but watching what you do, I think it’s better for me too.”

Jang Ilso gave a slightly strange look and spoke. Voices of agreement came from here and there.

“That’s right.”

“...Honestly, we’re sorry.”

“Sorry.”

“What, you bastards?”

Chung Myung glared at the Mount Hua disciples who agreed with Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso spoke.

“The ‘Heavenly Demon’ those guys are waiting for is...”

The mention of ‘Heavenly Demon’ changed everyone’s mood instantly. Jang Ilso kept his gaze fixed on Chung Myung and spoke gravely.

“It seems he doesn’t just fall from the sky but is resurrected from someone’s body?”

Chung Myung glared coldly at Jang Ilso, but Jang Ilso just lifted the corner of his red lips and met that gaze.

“And the Demonic Cult has no way to confirm the resurrected one is the Heavenly Demon until he realizes it himself. So... no one dares to kill people?”

Chung Myung didn’t bother to answer. But in this situation, what clearer answer is there than silence? Jang Ilso’s eyes gleamed strangely.

“That means... that Heavenly Demon might have already resurrected and is living here in the Central Plains without even knowing he’s the Heavenly Demon?”

Chung Myung’s gaze grew fiercer. Then Jang Ilso chuckled softly.

“Looking at me with those eyes only makes me more certain of those words.”

Chung Myung, grinding his teeth as if disgusted at Jang Ilso, sighed. Since it was Jang Ilso who saw everything with his own eyes, he wouldn’t be swayed by any words.

“There’s nothing certain.”

Chung Myung said in a low voice.

“Whether he has already resurrected, or not yet born, or if he knows he’s the Heavenly Demon but hasn’t revealed himself to the world.”

“...”

“That’s the problem—no one knows anything about it.”

Jang Il-so nodded as if he roughly understood.

“Resurrection...”

He murmured for a moment, then sighed lightly.

“You really believe such absurd stories. Those crazy fanatics, and you who are crazier than them.”

Chung Myung didn’t bother to answer.

There was no reason or confidence to explain. If he hadn’t experienced the process of rebirth himself, he would have dismissed all this as nonsense like Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso’s gaze swept over the place where Heaven Killer had existed and the land marked by Dan Ja-Kang’s traces.

“But... to dismiss those words as mere lunatic nonsense... those crazy bastards aren’t ordinary lunatics.”

Jang Ilso clearly felt Heaven Killer’s power as well.

Dan Ja-Kang alone was a strong man he had never encountered before. Yet Heaven Killer possessed power that crushed even Dan Ja-Kang in an instant. Clearly, no one in the murim world could rival Heaven Killer at this moment.

“If that power combined with the Demonic Cult’s power I saw?”

Conquering the Central Plains wouldn’t seem so difficult. At least, it was a much more realistic story than the dream Jang Ilso was having.

But neither Heaven Killer nor the Demonic Cult moves.

Someone who could grasp everything if they wished was wasting their life in some unknown borderland, all for the sake of faith.

Because it was Jang Ilso, and no one else, he understood how absurd this situation was.

The strong are bundles of self-awareness. No matter how talented, it takes great effort to embody that talent into real power.

Therefore, those who gain overwhelming power after a long process become embodiments of self-love that ordinary people find hard to understand.

A strong man like Heaven Killer would have his immense self-awareness overflow across the Central Plains. Yet, instead of trying to overcome the Heavenly Demon with that power, he has thrown away everything easily attainable and is just waiting for the Heavenly Demon’s reincarnation.

Like a loyal dog guarding an ownerless house.

‘Is this really possible?’

Impossible. No, it must not be possible. There is only one situation where it could be.

Jang Il-so looked around everyone with a sinister gaze.

“So that Heavenly Demon...”

Everyone held their breath at the ominous voice.

“Is someone who can even turn that monster into a dog guarding an ownerless house.”

Anyone living in the murim world would have heard the two words ‘Heavenly Demon.’ That name is a symbol of power and the embodiment of fear.

But no expression about the Heavenly Demon was more intense than the sentence Jang Ilso uttered.

“Beyond loyalty, submission... no, beyond submission to the point of offering oneself as a sacrifice.”

Jang Ilso let out a bitter laugh.

Even the great Jang Ilso has never completely subjugated anyone like that. Just because someone says they’ll resurrect after death, would his people wait for them for a hundred years?

Would those murder-crazed bastards endure a long life of patience in the borderlands like monks devoted to Buddhism?

“No way.”

Then what on earth must be done to make someone like Heaven Killer act like that, not even basic warriors?

No one dared to speak.

The one who fought them was Dan Ja-gang, and what they faced was Heaven Killer. But at this moment, the huge shadow of the Heavenly Demon, whom none had ever met, seemed to press down on everyone here.

Jang Ilso’s eyes traced a strange arc.

“Such a guy might be living somewhere here without even knowing who he is...”

Jang Ilso slowly shook his head.

“That’s an excessive story even for a joke.”

They solved everything. They drove out the Demonic Cult that invaded Hangzhou and even caused the death of one of their top forces, the bishop. Considering the achievements, nothing more could be hoped for.

But on the faces of those left here, there was no joy for the achievement. Those who realized the demonic sect’s power and guessed the Heavenly Demon’s strength were left only with an indescribable pressure.

“Well, it doesn’t matter. That’s not an immediate problem for us.”

Jang Ilso sighed lightly and smiled faintly.

“Rather than the uncertain future...”

Though it was a bright smile, the moment the Mount Hua disciples saw his eyes, they suddenly felt their hair stand on end.

“Shouldn’t we solve our own problems first?”

Jang Ilso’s voice changed. The strange friendliness till then had disappeared, replaced by a killing intent. Baek Cheon and his group instantly tensed.

‘No way...?’

‘At a moment like this?’

Everyone tensed up.

They thought Jang Ilso might reveal his true nature at any time. The reason they came to the south with only a few was because they couldn’t trust him, wasn’t it?

But still, this was too fast.

As soon as Jang Ilso finished speaking, Red Dogs quietly but swiftly gathered around him. At the same time, Mount Hua disciples also confronted Jang Ilso with Chung Myung at the center.

Jang Ilso’s sly gaze and Chung Myung’s cold eyes intertwined in the air.

Chung Myung and Jang Ilso couldn’t properly fight anyway. So this was a fight between the Red Dogs and Mount Hua. Though the Red Dogs were very strong, Mount Hua’s forces were no pushover.

‘At least retreating...’

Just as Baek Cheon tried to exchange a glance with Un Geom, Jang Ilso shook his head as if he saw through his intention.

“Oh dear, dear. Was your name Baek Cheon?”

“...You.”

“If you’re that slow at calculating... you might survive in the north, but not in the south.”

“What nonsense...”

Jo Gul, about to raise his voice for Baek Cheon, suddenly shut his mouth as if someone had covered it. His pupils trembled greatly.

He had seen them. Those who had appeared all around.

A groan escaped from Yoon Jong’s mouth.

“Huh... Black Ghost Fortress...”

The elite forces of Black Ghost Fortress, left at the entrance of Hangzhou, had already surrounded them, tightening the encirclement. They wouldn’t let a single person escape.

If it were only the Red Dogs, they might manage, but if Black Ghost Fortress joined?

“Damn it...”

A momentary gloom crossed the faces of the Mount Hua disciples. Jang Ilso, watching their expressions with amusement, laughed aloud.

“Aren’t you curious?”

A brilliant smile spread across Jang Ilso’s face. A cruel light flickered in his pale eyes.

“Which is more terrifying: fighting a tiger the size of a house, or being surrounded by a pack of starving hyenas?”

“This bastard...”

Jo Gul’s eyes began to blaze with fury.

Chapter 1068: I Said Try To Kill Me? (3)

Trust is truly a word with a pleasant resonance. Nothing gives people more strength than the trust formed between individuals.

However, in some relationships, trust is nothing more than a castle built on sand.

‘I knew it...’

Baek Cheon bit his lip tightly.

Who could dare argue that Jang Ilso is the one you must never trust in this murim world? The one you should never rely on?

Yet, the reason Baek Cheon momentarily let down his guard was because of the fierce battle and the image Jang Ilso had shown. The feeling of comrades-in-arms often overcomes all conflicts, even if just for a moment.

But Jang Ilso is still Jang Ilso. That sinister snake was someone who should never be trusted until the very end.

“Sasuk...”

“...Yes.”

Baek Cheon drew his sword heavily.

The forces of the Black Ghost Fortress closing in seemed to number over two hundred. If the Red Dogs, who guarded Jang Ilso, joined them, their numbers would increase even more.

Considering these were elite fighters carefully selected and trained by Black Ghost Fort and the Ten Thousand People Clan, the roughly ten disciples of Mount Hua were no match. But...

“We have to fight.”

Baek Cheon’s eyes burned coldly. A desperate situation was nothing new. Had there ever been a battlefield they passed through that wasn’t desperate?

‘We just need to open a path.’

There was no need to fight them head-on. They just had to break through this encirclement and cross the Yangtze River. Though the distance from Hangzhou to the Yangtze was brutally long, that was a problem for later. First, they had to escape this place.

Baek Cheon’s burning gaze shot toward Jang Ilso. Seeing him glare so fiercely, Jang Ilso chuckled softly.

“You’re a naughty kid, shooting someone with eyes like that.”

In truth, Baek Cheon was glaring at Jang Ilso to provoke him, but his attention was entirely on Chung Myung.

Mount Hua’s strength was almost intact. Namgung Do-Wei, who had broken the path at the front, was nearly exhausted, and Hae Yeon had also greatly depleted his internal qi, but they could still keep up the pursuit.

The biggest problem now was none other than Chung Myung. Baek Cheon glanced at his back.

‘Can he even take a step?’

Objectively, it was impossible. Even the visible wounds were so severe that survival was miraculous. Considering Chung Myung’s nature of not groaning even when near death, the internal injuries were likely far worse than the external ones.

But Baek Cheon was certain.

The moment the battle began, Chung Myung would charge at Jang Ilso without looking back. That was Chung Myung’s way.

But this time, no matter what, they had to stop Chung Myung from rampaging. So that only they would survive and not wail over Chung Myung’s corpse.

Then, the only one who could take Chung Myung’s role here was Baek Cheon.

At the very moment Baek Cheon’s eyes grew cold with killing intent, Jang Ilso, who was watching the elite Black Ghost Fort forces closing in with a strange gaze, slowly spoke.

“A very... appropriate encirclement. I almost want to compliment you.”

With a crooked smile, he fixed his gaze on one spot.

“But... I don’t think I ever gave such an order. Hmm? Desolate Gold Elder.”

At those words, Baek Cheon instinctively turned his gaze. Though he knew he must never take his eyes off Jang Ilso, his eyes instinctively followed Desolate Gold Elder.

Among the elite Black Ghost Fortress, Desolate Gold Elder walked forward with one sleeve torn and fluttering. Unlike the battered Chung Myung and Jang Ilso, he maintained a clean appearance without a single wound.

Receiving Jang Ilso’s gaze, Desolate Gold Elder spoke without changing his expression, his voice utterly emotionless.

“Generally... a good subordinate anticipates and acts according to their superior’s will.”

“Ha ha.”

Jang Ilso let out an ambiguous laugh. It was hard to tell if it was from approval or displeasure.

“Those guys are dangerous.”

Desolate Gold Elder’s gaze left Jang Ilso and fixed on the Mount Hua disciples. Faced with his emotionless stare, the disciples bit their lips unconsciously.

“Alliance Lord must have felt it too, right? Those guys are different from the fools in the Justice faction who are drunk on their power and just occupy positions. Someday, they will surely hold you back.”

“...”

“Before it’s too late, they must be eliminated here and now. A tiger cub must be caught and killed while it’s young.”

Hearing that, Jang Ilso slowly scanned the Mount Hua disciples. His gaze finally landed on Chung Myung.

“That’s certainly a reasonable statement from you.”

“I...”

Baek Cheon clenched his teeth tightly. As if savoring the words, Jang Ilso slowly nodded.

“A tiger must be caught and killed while it’s young... Yes, that’s true. But...”

A sinister aura began to cloud Jang Ilso’s face as he looked at Desolate Gold Elder.

“Don’t you think it’s a bit late to be saying that now?”

Baek Cheon sensed something strange at that moment.

Neither Jang Ilso’s gaze at Desolate Gold Elder nor Desolate Gold Elder’s gaze back showed even a shred of goodwill typical of allies.

It was more like the eyes of beasts staring down an intruder in their territory.

‘Could it be?’

At that moment, Jang Ilso spoke again. His words gave Baek Cheon certainty.

“There’s no way such a grand encirclement was set up just to catch these guys. So... Desolate Gold Elder.”

A wicked and deadly light poured from Jang Ilso’s eyes. Just looking at them chilled the soul.

“Which side are you truly hunting here though?”

“...”

“These guys? No, no. That can’t be.”

Jang Ilso bared his teeth and laughed.

“It’s me, isn’t it?”

“...”

“Or maybe not?”

Desolate Gold Elder silently stared at Jang Ilso with indifferent eyes. Receiving that gaze, Jang Ilso spoke as if amused.

“Come to think of it, it’s a good opportunity. Yes, a really good opportunity. I’m completely exhausted leading this small group.”

“...”

“But... I never thought the great Desolate Gold Elder would do something so foolish. Did your much-boasted abacus get completely smashed by the hands of the demonic cult? Do you truly think someone like you can take my place if I disappear? Seriously?”

“Place?”

Desolate Gold Elder, who had been quietly listening, retorted coldly.

“That’s unworthy of the Hegemony Lord.”

“...”

“I don’t care about places. The Four Sects Alliance was created out of necessity. If you disappear, Southern Lands will just return to its original state.”

Those words were a perfect wedge, making everyone here fully understand the situation they were in.

The encirclement prepared by Desolate Gold Elder was not to capture the Mount Hua disciples. His true target was none other than Jang Ilso.

“You’re talking nonsense.”

Jang Ilso looked coldly at Desolate Gold Elder and spoke slowly.

“Do you think the Justice Faction will leave Gangnam alone after the Four Sects Alliance disappears?”

“You’re the one mistaken, Jang Ilso.”

Desolate Gold Elder no longer called Jang Ilso ‘Alliance Lord’.

“Mistaken?”

“What they hate is not the Desolate Gold Elder, but you and the Ten Thousand People Clan.”

“...”

“Even now, those obsessed with power have no reason to waste effort on Southern Lands without you. It’s not because of you that they don’t attack the Southern lands; it’s because of you that they might.”

It was a painful point. Even Jang Ilso couldn’t refute it and let out a bitter laugh.

But Desolate Gold Elder’s words were not finished.

“And... why should I care about that in the first place?”

“Hmm?”

A cold smile appeared on Desolate Gold Elder’s face.

“Even if the day comes when the Four Sects Alliance you belong to rules the world, I doubt there will be a place for me there.”

“...”

“Isn’t that so?”

Listening to Desolate Gold Elder’s speculation go on, Jang Ilso gave a faintly strange smile. Desolate Gold Elder met his gaze with an equally chilling aura.

“When the hunt is over, the hunting dog goes into the pot. There is only one way for the hunting dog to survive.”

Desolate Gold Elder’s voice spread coldly over the desolate land.

“Either bite the hand and run before the master suspects, or bite the master’s neck first.”

“...”

“What do you think, Jang Ilso?”

This time, everyone’s gaze turned to Jang Ilso. He scratched the back of his head in a somewhat exaggerated manner and clicked his tongue shortly.

“Tsk.”

Then his long eyes traced a curve.

“I thought you’d at least wait until the Shaolin retreated...”

“You never underestimate your enemies. But... not considering me an enemy anymore was your mistake.”

Desolate Gold Elder glared at Jang Ilso with murderous eyes.

“If you wanted to tear me apart, you should have at least properly leashed me. Did you really think I was like the dogs I’ve trained all this time?”

“That’s a painful truth.”

“In the murim world, regret is never too late. If you misjudge something, you must pay the price. That’s the law of the Evil Sects.”

Desolate Gold Elder nodded toward Jang Ilso, Red Dogs, and Mount Hua.

“Leave no one alive. Kill them all.”

“Alliance Lord...”

“No need to capture alive. Kill them. Leave no aftermath.”

“Understood!”

With intense killing intent, the elite Black Ghost Fortress slowly tightened the encirclement at the same steady pace as before. Their cautious approach, without a hint of carelessness, only increased the pressure.

“Tsk.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue shortly and glanced at Chung Myung.

“Since the situation has come to this, we have no choice but to join forces, huh?”

Then Chung Myung glanced at him with indifferent eyes.

“You talk so big, yet you can’t even watch over one subordinate. Stupid bastard.”

“When someone falls into a pit, shouldn’t you naturally help? Not spit on them and stomp for fun. Seriously, you have a terrible personality, Taoist.”

Hearing that he had a bad personality from an Evil sect member, Chung Myung shook his head and gripped his sword.

Though he was bluffing, this was a real crisis. Both he and Jang Ilso could barely stand now.

However, Chung Myung’s gaze turned to the backs of the Mount Hua disciples. They guarded him tightly, surrounding him without a gap.

Just like Dang Bo did in the past, they were protecting him here now. Or perhaps, every time he fought.

Looking at the back that had grown much larger than before, Chung Myung turned his gaze to Desolate Gold Elder. Forcing down the surge of anger in his chest, he spoke coldly.

“As expected from an Evil Sect bastard, you still don’t know anything. Then I’ll have to teach you.”

Chung Myung bared his teeth fiercely.

“No one who turned Mount Hua into an enemy ever survived.”

At that moment, the swords held by the Mount Hua disciples simultaneously surged with sword qi.

Chapter 1069: I Said Try To Kill Me? (4)

Desolate Gold Elder watched coldly as his subordinates tightened the encirclement. His gaze slowly swept over them before settling on the Red Dogs and the disciples of Mount Hua, who were sharpening their blades. The last place his eyes landed was, as expected, on Jang Ilso.

‘Jang Ilso, surely you won’t call me a coward.’

Exploiting an opponent’s weakness was the nature of the Evil Sect. It made no difference whether the target was enemy or ally. It wasn’t the one who stabbed in the back who was bad, but the one who left an opening who was foolish. That was the Evil Sect’s way.

Jang Ilso knew this better than anyone and had used it to come this far. Even if he died here and became a restless spirit, he would have no right to blame Desolate Gold Elder.

Desolate Gold Elder’s gaze shifted slightly downward. Seeing the empty, fluttering sleeve, his eyes darkened with gloom.

If he had not lost this arm, he might have made a different choice at this moment. Though it was unpleasant to grovel under Jang Ilso, if there was something to gain, there was no reason to refuse.

Pride was not very important to him. He did not intend to sell himself cheaply, but if given a proper price, he would not hesitate to sell.

However, the moment he lost this arm, everything changed. For him, who wielded an excellent sword, losing his arm meant he could no longer live as he had in the past.

If he were in Jang Ilso’s position, how would he have treated the weakened Desolate Gold Elder? Having lost most of his power and holding only the title of guardian of the Black Ghost Fortress, which he could not even protect, how would he have handled him?

‘There is no need to think twice.’

He would have approached like an old snake, whispering that if Desolate Gold Elder followed him alone, he could protect everything he had now. From the moment Desolate Gold Elder, having no other choice, pledged loyalty, he would have slowly and leisurely taken everything he had.

And later, when Desolate Gold Elder was no longer useful, he would simply dispose of him.

The moment Jang Ilso left here, there was no other option for Desolate Gold Elder. He had to choose either to die immediately or surrender to Jang Ilso, lose everything, and wait for a miserable death.

Desolate Gold Elder had no intention of agonizing over these choices. Worrying over two losing options was not his way.

He chose a third path: to eliminate Jang Ilso here and overturn the entire situation in his favor.

Jang Ilso’s death would plunge the barely unified Southern situation into chaos, and the Northern alliance slowly forming against him would also be thrown into confusion.

Is there any other way for the weaker party to survive than to plunge the world into turmoil?

‘This is a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity.’

It was a chance close to divine luck.

If the cultists they faced had not stopped fighting and retreated upon Dan Ja-Kang’s appearance, Desolate Gold Elder would not have had the strength left to face Red Dogs and Mount Hua head on.

Naturally, Desolate Gold Elder had no intention of missing such a god-given opportunity.

“You have one misjudgment.”

His cold gaze turned toward Mount Hua and Red Dogs, who were sharpening their blades against the approaching Black Ghost Fortress.

“You have underestimated me too easily, Jang Ilso.”

And his misjudgment would determine the fate of the tempestuous Jang Ilso.

The encirclement tightened more and more.

‘This is not good.’

Baek Cheon’s eyes darkened.

It would be better if all those troops charged at once. Because in the chaos of battle, there would definitely be openings.

But except for the ten at the front, the others did not seem to intend to clash blades with them at all.

It was clearly felt. That was not a formation to kill. It was a formation to prevent escape.

There was a will to not let even a single rat escape from here, even if it meant suffering more losses by not committing all forces to the ten at the front.

It was excessive treatment for the nearly fallen Jang Ilso and Chung Myung, but Desolate Gold Elder was a man who did not tolerate even the slightest variable in his command.

‘Or perhaps their presence made Desolate Gold Elder so cautious.’

Whatever the cause, the result was the same.

Baek Cheon slightly lowered one shoulder and bent his knees, as if ready to dart off to one side at any moment. But as soon as he lowered his shoulder, the elite Black Ghost Fortress troops filling the rear tightened toward the direction of his shoulder.

‘...Like soldiers.’

It was clearly not a skill honed by just one or two encounters. They must have been trained professionally in such encirclements, like hunting practice.

Baek Cheon instinctively licked his lips slightly. There was no way to deny that this situation was a crisis for them. If viewed very coldly, everyone here was halfway at death’s door.

But strangely, there was no fear.

“Sasuk, shall I go first?”

And it seemed the disciples felt the same. Glancing at Jo Gul, who was revving up as if ready to charge forward, Baek Cheon calmly asked.

“Aren’t you scared?”

“Scared?”

Jo Gul snorted as if he was hearing nonsense.

“Those bastards are only strong, but not like the high priest from earlier.”

“...”

“I’ve seen with my own eyes people who glare and charge at even those so-called high priests, so if I get scared of those guys, I’d be pathetic.”

Baek Cheon grinned.

“Yeah. I agree with that.”

Other Mount Hua disciples also raised their swords slightly, as if agreeing. Then Baek Cheon spoke sharply in a cold voice.

“But those two raging are enough. Don’t lose your reason. Our job is not to fight those guys but to break through the path.”

“Yes.”

Everyone’s expression hardened.

‘Individually, there’s no particular problem...’

The problem was their numbers and formation. Though it looked like a rough encirclement, their strange and slow movements clearly indicated a kind of formation. If they fought thoughtlessly, they might suffer great harm.

But...

‘We can’t afford to hesitate in this situation!’

Baek Cheon steeled his resolve, gripping his sword tightly and twisting it to charge.

‘Alright! I’ll charge first and open the way!’

Just as Baek Cheon infused qi into his legs and prepared to dash forward.

“Oh?”

“Gasp!”

Startled by the sudden excited voice behind him, Baek Cheon turned around.

“Wow, this is interesting.”

“Yes?”

Im So-Byeong was holding a fully opened fan, fluttering it gently. He seemed completely unconcerned whether Baek Cheon looked at him in disbelief or not.

“Wait, wait... just a moment...”

Muttering to himself, he began to scrutinize those who had formed the encirclement. His eyes were like those of a bird of prey.

“The Eight Trigrams, contradictions. Essence and... no, is the three talents (refers to heaven, earth and man) mixed in as well...?”

Baek Cheon blinked blankly.

At that moment, Im So-Byeong suddenly folded his fan and struck it down onto his hand.

“Tsk tsk tsk. Such an elegant formation, yet it’s from an Evil sect scum.”

“Yes?”

“Now, now. Look, the seal.”

Im So-Byeong nodded toward the Black Ghost Fortress.

“The key is not the ones at the front, but those lurking behind. If you break through in a straight line, the front will tighten defense and retreat positions, while those behind will surround the intruder from both sides. At least, attacks will come from five directions. Then what happens?”

“...Yes?”

Baek Cheon asked blankly, and Im So-Byeong frowned. With a look that said, “How can a Mount Hua disciple not understand something this simple?” Baek Cheon hung his head deeply in embarrassment.

‘Well, someone has to teach me for me to...’

All he learned at Mount Hua was “Cut off the head first in battle” and “People don’t die so easily, so just charge in,” so what else could he do?

“If you charge, you’ll be caught in a pincer attack and die.”

“Ah.”

He should have said it that clearly from the start. Baek Cheon nodded with a clean expression.

“It seems Desolate Gold Elder has studied Mount Hua and Ten Thousand People Clan quite a bit. Both Mount Hua and Ten Thousand People Clan are like wild boar sects representing the Justice and Evil factions, so when a fight breaks out, they would just charge without thinking, right?”

“...Well, that’s a bit...”

It’s not entirely wrong, but hearing that from a bandit leader’s mouth, they should consider their position too...

“It looks like a hasty encirclement, but in reality, they are prepared to respond appropriately to both of you. If we rush in impatiently, they will surround and swiftly cut our necks and stab our bellies repeatedly. Heh heh heh heh. Of course, there might be some fool who charges blindly even after seeing this...”

Just before charging, a man’s shoulder tensed as he stopped by chance. He felt a sudden chill in his neck and a stabbing pain in his stomach.

“Indeed, Desolate Gold Elder is different from some Black Dragon King. He truly understands the art of war. He might be the worst enemy to Mount Hua and the Ten Thousand People Clan. Heh heh heh heh.”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes sparkled as he said those words. Somehow, he looked like someone excited to finally meet his specialty in a situation like this.

‘This guy really isn’t sane either.’

Why do only these kinds of people gather around Mount Hua?

“But!”

Smack!

At that moment, Im So-Byeong struck his palm again with his fan.

“That’s a story for when I’m not here.”

He smiled meaningfully and gently pushed Baek Cheon forward.

“If they attack from all sides, hold your ground. Even if they show an opening, never rush out. The moment anyone sticks out, they will surround the exposed one even if it means showing their backs. If others rush out to save them one by one, they will all be annihilated.”

Hearing this, Baek Cheon shouted urgently.

“Yoon Jong!”

“Yes, Sasuk!”

“Put a leash on that Jo Gul bastard!”

“Yes!”

“No, why me...?”

“Shut up and stick by my side!”

“...Yes.”

Baek Cheon glanced back at Im So-Byeong and said.

“...But will holding out improve the situation? The longer we delay, the more disadvantageous it is for our smaller numbers.”

“Of course. But...”

Im So-Byeong glanced somewhere and smiled.

“I’ll tell you then, when it happens.”

“...”

“Here they come. More talk later! For now, hold your ground as much as possible. You can do that, right? You’re from Mount Hua?”

At that moment, the elite Black Ghost Fortress troops tightening the front lowered their stances and began charging toward Mount Hua.

“What the...”

A big grin spread across Baek Cheon’s face.

“Of course!”

Whoosh!

As Baek Cheon swung his sword, the Mount Hua disciples guarding him simultaneously shook their swords. The crimson plum blossoms they ignited bloomed brilliantly before the charging Black Ghost Fortress elites.

Chapter 1070: I Said Try To Kill Me? (5)

Pararararak!

Red petals fluttered wildly in all directions.

To the eye, it might have seemed nothing more than a beautiful sight. The red petals soaring high into the sky and drifting on the wind looked almost as warm as a peach blossom garden in the celestial realm.

But to those who knew the truth, there was no sight more threatening than this.

Anyone with even a little understanding of martial arts would be horrified to realize that each of those countless fluttering petals was actually sword qi imbued with killing intent.

Those who understood martial arts more deeply would surely focus on a different aspect.

The gaze of Desolate Gold Elder grew cold and settled.

‘Mount Hua...’

Scattering that much sword qi is no ordinary feat. Even more difficult is controlling that sword qi precisely as one desires.

Think about it.

Right now, the number of people scattering sword qi reaches as many as five.

All five are wildly scattering unpredictable, bizarre sword qi, embroidering the air chaotically, yet none collided or interfered with each other’s.

Logically, the sword qi emitted should collide and cause clashes. But the sword qi of Mount Hua disciples harmonizes perfectly as if launched by a single person.

It was a perfect display of how relentlessly they had synchronized their breathing and movements.

But those facing the sword qi were no less formidable.

“Hyaaah!”

The elite warriors of the Black Ghost Fortress shouted at the top of their lungs and threw themselves without hesitation into the wave of petals rushing toward them.

Kakagakang!

Screech! Screech!

The sounds of sword qi clashing and flesh being chillingly sliced echoed simultaneously. Judging it impossible to block all sword qi, they protected their heads and torsos as much as possible while ignoring other parts.

It was a ruthless decision to sacrifice their own flesh to approach the enemy.

In a normal battlefield, this would be an utterly foolish act to prefer. But when holding overwhelming superiority and able to end everything in one battle, that foolish decision transforms into a perfect one.

“Grrrgh!”

They gritted their teeth against the pain inflicted by sword qi piercing their limbs.

“Tahhat!”

And ultimately, with that simple tactic alone, they succeeded in breaking through the pouring sword qi. Mount Hua’s swordsmanship is flashy and easily deceiving to human eyes, but its concentrated power is weak to cause damage. They precisely targeted this weakness.

Baek Cheon’s face hardened.

‘Not even hesitation?’

It’s not strange if one or two people make such a choice. There might be one or two who can instantly grasp the opponent’s swordsmanship weakness by instinct or reason.

But the fact that all those rushing into the battlefield without even exchanging glances respond the same way means only one thing.

They have clearly already studied and prepared to counter Mount Hua’s swordsmanship.

This must be a first experience for Baek Cheon and the disciples of Mount Hua.

Mount Hua’s swordsmanship differs from the typical Justice faction sect’s swordsmanship. It maximizes the use of illusionary sword principles, which are rarely used in the Justice faction for being considered tricky. Because of this, those encountering Mount Hua’s sword qi for the first time always feel great bewilderment.

But at this moment, Mount Hua’s disciples clearly understood.

In the martial world, Mount Hua is no longer a strange or novel sect. Rather, it is a sect that everyone watches and keeps their guards against.

Everyone in Mount Hua has worked together to dramatically raise their standing. And as with all things in the world, a higher position naturally brings greater risks. Now, sects wary of Mount Hua have begun actively analyzing their swordsmanship and seeking ways to counter it.

This means Mount Hua is recognized as a ‘strong force’ in the murim world, and also signals that the challenges they will face ahead will be even harsher.

“Kyaaaah!”

The elite Black Ghost Fortress who pierced through the forest of sword qi charged at Mount Hua’s disciples with bloodshot eyes. Though their bodies were slashed and bleeding, their momentum did not wane.

“DIEEEEEE!”

Kwaaaaaa!

Despite their high spirits, the sword strikes they wielded were not so much powerful as swift.

‘Kuk!’

Kakang!

Baek Cheon, at the forefront, quickly raised his sword to block the incoming blade.

“Fast!”

It was not to overpower by force, but a swift strike to maintain offense and defense and hold the opponent. It was clearly aimed at not giving Mount Hua’s flashy sword techniques any chance to threaten them.

Each attack they displayed clearly showed they had thoroughly studied Mount Hua.

Enduring the heavy pressure of the blade, Baek Cheon bit his lip tightly.

‘We were complacent.’

Mount Hua is the greatest enemy of the Four Sects Alliance.

Baek Cheon was fully aware of this and sometimes even took pride in it. But he never imagined this clear fact would manifest in such an incident at this moment.

But now that he knew, it was enough.

“Taat!”

Baek Cheon swung his sword strongly, deflecting the blade.

At the same time, a fist filled with grand fist qi brushed past right beside his ear.

Kwang!

Hae Yeon’s fist pierced the open chest of the enemy. As the enemy coughed up blood and fell back, Baek Cheon, about to step forward in pursuit, stopped instantly and firmly stomped the ground.

‘Hold on!’

What they must do now is not to completely finish off the enemy as usual. Even if it means letting the enemy go unharmed, they must endure.

“Hyaaap!”

Wave after wave, the second and third waves surged in.

Their fervent charge showed not joy in driving the enemy back, but rather an urgency not to give any distance to them. It was a desperate resolve not to allow Mount Hua’s swordsmen any chance to scatter their unique sword qi properly.

Blades fiercely descended toward Baek Cheon’s head. At the same time, sword qi emitted from somewhere flew toward his legs.

The meaning of this simultaneous attack was clear. It was not to split Baek Cheon in one blow, but to accumulate even the small wounds on his lower body.

Even if it meant injuring themselves in the process, they intended to accumulate wounds on Baek Cheon and slowly wear him down.

This was exactly how Mount Hua’s disciples charged at an absolute strong opponent.

Gritting his teeth, Baek Cheon swiftly swung his sword and struck three consecutive sword slashes at the attackers.

Paaaah!

Before the blade aimed at his head could reach, before the sword qi flying at his legs could extend, Baek Cheon’s sharp sword qi pierced the charging enemy.

Kwaddeudeuk!

But then, a blade that had not yet lost all its power slashed long across Baek Cheon’s cheek. Feeling the sudden burning pain on his face, Baek Cheon shouted.

“Get your wits right and be collected! These are different from anyone we’ve faced before!”

Other Mount Hua disciples also clearly sensed this and responded loudly with tense answers.

It was the first time.

Not standing as the challenger in weakness, but as the challenged strong one. Not being crushed by greater numbers and power, but facing desperate charges.

As the battle began to turn completely differently than expected, Mount Hua’s disciples gradually started to be pushed back.

“Mount Hua...”

Desolate Gold Elder, watching the battle coldly, muttered lowly.

‘As expected, those guys are too dangerous.’

They differ from any Justice faction sect fellows he knew.

Young martial artists whose baby faces have yet to shed their baby fat possess excessively high martial prowess for their age and fear no real combat.

It is the same now.

The way the elite Black Ghost Fortress attacked was based on information he analyzed from observing Mount Hua and their past deeds. Moreover, it was a method to counter Mount Hua’s martial arts considering their recent performance at Plum Blossom Island.

Originally, the plan assumed surrounding the entire Mount Hua with a larger force, but it was clearly a tactic effective even against a small number like now.

To the unknowing, his strategy might seem perfectly executed to hold Mount Hua back, but in reality, Mount Hua’s response was more agile than he expected.

They instantly understood the enemy’s intent and adjusted their methods accordingly. Those young ones.

“Those other Justice faction sect flowers raised in warm greenhouses that wither at the slightest cold breeze could never dream of this.”

This is not just Desolate Gold Elder’s personal view or opinion to be dismissed. Mount Hua has proven itself countless times. Their performance at Plum Blossom Island alone needs no further explanation.

If they continue to grow without hindrance like this?

‘No one in the world will be able to stop the sect called Mount Hua.’

Therefore, they must be killed. This place is not only to remove the blade called Jang Il-so that is at his throat now, but also to break the arrow called Mount Hua that will someday pierce his heart.

‘But... no matter how I look at them, I just can’t understand.’

How on earth did such guys appear?

Mature and refined swordsmanship and rich real combat experience are actually incompatible in a being.

To gain as much experience as possible, one must face life-risking real combat early, but such people often die before honing their skills. Conversely, if one avoids real combat until martial arts fully mature, they become fools strong only in theory but ignorant of real life combat.

The murim world has always chosen one of two paths. The Evil Sects push people into real combat to raise survivors, while the Justice faction sects avoid real combat and wait for the swordsmanship to mature.

Thus, the Justice faction sect has a thick layer of strong warriors who have long mastered martial arts, while the Evil sect’s low-level warriors, mere expendables, are stronger than the Justice sect’s.

But those Mount Hua guys are neither.

They possess both the refined swordsmanship of the righteous sect’s elite and the sharp real combat edge of the Evil Sect’s low-level warriors.

The term “between justice and evil” originally refers to those who belong to neither justice nor evil sects, but looking at those guys, the meaning of that term might need to be rewritten.

‘How on earth did Mount Hua produce such guys?’

It’s as if an absolute martial god, mastering both justice and evil martial arts, trained disciples in a way never seen before.

But there’s no way such an absolute being exists, nor that such a being would be in the ruined Mount Hua.

‘No, there’s no reason to think about it.’

Because those guys will die here today anyway.

Desolate Gold Elder shot a cold glance at Jang Ilso surrounded by the Red Dogs.

‘You don’t raise a tiger cub, Jang Ilso.’

Ultimately, this was another mistake of Jang Ilso. He thought he could handle what he could not. Like a foolish person who picks up a tiger cub, believing he alone won’t be bitten by it, even after seeing others die from the tiger’s bite.

“Don’t rush, tell them to tighten slowly. No matter how great the damage, it’s fine. But not a single one of them must be left alive.”

“Yes!”

Desolate Gold Elder slightly frowned and slowly stepped closer toward the battlefield.

He briefly raised his unfamiliar left hand to the hilt of the sword tied on his right side, but soon let it drop again.

Chapter 1071: Who Made The Mistake? (1)

Kagaang!

Yu Yiseol’s brow furrowed slightly.

The moment she blocked one sword strike, another came flying in, pressing down on her sword. Before she could respond, yet another sword strike followed.

Kaaang!

The preemptive sword strike collided swiftly, restricting the movement of her blade, and the heavy sword strike that followed seemed to bind her so she couldn’t move.

Within this sequence, it was impossible to display the flashy sword techniques unique to Mount Hua.

If the numerical difference were two or three times, there might be room for a counterattack, but a difference of over ten times is impossible to overcome for anyone. Moreover, they skillfully exploited this numerical advantage against Mount Hua.

But!

‘So slow.’

Paaaah!

Her sword was pulled back like a flash of lightning. Leaving a long trail in the air, Yu Yiseol drew her sword back and then thrust it forward at twice the speed.

The elite of the Black Ghost Fortress widened his eyes at the lightning-fast sword movement, but before a scream could escape, Yu Yiseol’s sword mercilessly pierced his neck.

Kwa-deuk!

Yu Yiseol, habitually pressing down on the sword stuck in his neck to lift herself with the recoil, suddenly hesitated.

They were all just waiting for her to jump inside.

It wasn’t just her who would become endangered by that action. The moment she was surrounded by enemies, her sahyungs and sajaes would rush to save her without caring for their own lives.

A sudden burden overwhelmed her—the fear that her momentary misjudgment might lead to the annihilation of her sahyung-sajaes.

The elite of the Black Ghost Fortress ignored their comrade’s death and swung their swords again toward Yu Yiseol’s exposed side.

Swaaaek!

Seeing the swords flying toward her sides, she swiftly withdrew the sword embedded in the enemy’s neck and swung it widely to the left and right.

‘No need to panic.’

Kaaang!

The incoming sword clashed with her blade and quickly bounced off.

Mount Hua’s swordsmanship is certainly centered on illusion and change. It creates countless variations, cleverly mixing real and fake sword qi to confuse opponents—this is the essence of Mount Hua swordsmanship.

But that is not all of Mount Hua.

Paaaah!

Yu Yiseol’s sword swiftly thrust again, piercing the retreating enemy’s shoulder in one strike.

Kwa-deuk!

Their strategy was certainly quite good. This was the first time Yu Yiseol and other Mount Hua disciples had encountered enemies who could counter their swordsmanship.

It was true they felt bewildered and momentarily recoiled. They couldn’t use their specialty footwork, nor could they freely draw the sword paths of the Plum Blossom sword technique they had swung countless times. The situation was precarious beyond simply being disadvantageous.

But Yu Yiseol’s gaze was steady as ever, without the slightest wavering.

Paat!

She stamped her foot in place for balance and swung her sword like a ray of light, cutting through the air. It was an extremely efficient sword, utterly free of waste or excess.

‘My body already knows.’

Certainly, this was the first time they had met enemies who could counter Mount Hua’s swordsmanship. Yes, it was the first time for them.

But Mount Hua disciples had fought countless fierce battles against those who easily countered their swordsmanship. They had been broken and shattered by the opponent but continuously stood up and fought again and again.

Therefore, how to face such enemies was engraved not in their minds but in their bodies.

‘Sajil!’

Originally, Mount Hua’s sword is extremely strong on battlefields where groups clash, but in extreme melees where swords entangle in seconds, it cannot display its full power.

But these were different.

The basics they obsessively emphasized beyond stubbornness. And continuous realistic sparring that ordinary prestigious Justice factions would never allow.

All of that remained fully within this body.

Like hammering steel to make it harder, the constantly tempered body moved before the mind to slay the enemy.

Kaaaang!

The incoming sword collided with the thin blade and absurdly bounced off easily.

‘Strength and internal qi aren’t everything.’

She knew all this intellectually. Strength doesn’t come just from exerting force. The important thing is to apply internal qi precisely when needed. Knowing the time and place can create multiple times the impact with the same strength.

And that proper time and place was remembered by her body.

All the almost forcibly instilled techniques were embodied and released from her body.

Swoosh!

She cleanly severed the carotid artery of the retreating enemy in panic. Then she calmly and unwaveringly stared at another enemy rushing toward her with cold eyes.

‘Look at this?’

Im So-Byeong’s eyes slightly widened.

The numerical difference was clear to the eyes. The difference in strength was also obvious. Therefore, he prepared various tactics, even ones close to luck, to overcome this huge gap that they had to cover.

But everything he prepared was becoming useless at this moment.

‘They get stronger here too?’

That made no sense. They were already thoroughly trained. They were not beginners, so experiencing one real fight wouldn’t suddenly improve their skills drastically.

This was not getting stronger but adapting, or rather changing. They adjusted everything from footwork to sword techniques and breathing according to the opponent.

It was truly a shocking sight.

It was a framework called martial learning. It meant standardization. That’s why beginners start by drawing precise sword paths.

Endless repetitive training solidifies one’s framework. The longer one trains martial arts, the firmer the framework becomes. Therefore, freely changing a once-made framework is practically impossible.

But they were now accomplishing the impossible. Their seemingly rigid frameworks were softly twisting and changing into forms suitable for the enemy.

‘Who in the world has ever seen this?’

The existence of the heretics and the high priest’s power was a huge shock, but this scene was shocking in another way. Those who understood what this change entailed would be even more shocked.

‘I thought they would have gotten stronger over three years.’

That monstrous Chung Myung trained them, so naturally they should have gotten stronger. Such a master wouldn’t bother teaching these rookies one by one in the murim world.

But what they showed went beyond Im So-Byeong’s common sense.

‘What on earth did you make, taoist...?’

Goosebumps ran down his spine at the sight. A sword that could respond to any battlefield and situation—that meant a sword without weaknesses, didn’t it?

Of course, having no weaknesses didn’t mean perfection...

‘At least there will never be a case of them dying foolishly without showing their true ability.’

Watching the scene before him, Im So-Byeong finally chuckled.

“...What a relentless person.”

He felt it from that sword. What the one who taught them truly wanted.

If they had only focused on strengthening Mount Hua’s sword, the moment their sword was first countered, surely someone would have died or been seriously injured.

Because they mastered a sword that could respond to any situation, they could block and endure. Their nature focused on overwhelming and defeating opponents, but their sword was specialized in protecting their own lives.

It’s easier said than done. To master such a sword, one can’t even imagine what process they must have gone through.

One could feel a tremendous obsession bordering on terror.

Both the teacher and the learner must have crossed the brink countless times. The direction of that obsession was clearly visible to Im So-Byeong standing behind.

“Was your greed too excessive...?”

Im So-Byeong shook his head in disbelief but his eyes gleamed again. In any case, from his perspective, his cards had increased.

‘Let’s see.’

Centered on Chung Myung in the middle, Baek Cheon and the Five Swords surrounded all sides. Between Chung Myung and the Five Swords were Un Geom, Hae Yeon, Tang So-So, Namgung Do-Wei, and Im So-Byeong, supporting those guarding the front.

The skill levels of those inside varied, but it didn’t matter. They were effectively holding an impenetrable shield now.

“Then shall we press a little more?”

Whoeeek!

He lightly swung his fan. The chaotic qi emitted from the fan’s tip passed over Yoon Jong’s back and momentarily pushed back those charging from the front.

“Let’s widen the gap a bit more to create space to fight!”

No sooner had he spoken than the Five Swords stepped forward one step.

Thanks to this, the number of enemies they had to face at once increased, but their feet also became a bit freer.

“Dieeee!”

Jo Gul unleashed his specialty, a storm-like flurry of strikes, toward the charging elite of the Black Ghost Fortress. Dozens of sword qi burst forth, piercing the attackers one after another.

The space ahead momentarily emptied, but Jo Gul looked around instead of rushing forward as usual.

Paaaah!

Then, without hesitation, he swung his sword at an enemy rushing toward Yoon Jong.

The elite of the Black Ghost Fortress, startled by the sword flying from the side, quickly moved and turned his head, but Jo Gul’s sword was twice as fast as his reaction.

Pook!

“Kkeuruk...”

The sword pierced through the side of the neck and came out the other side. The elite of the Black Ghost Fortress, foaming at the mouth, collapsed on the spot.

“No need to thank me.”

“Just do your job, you idiot!”

“...Even if I help, I get cursed.”

The brief opening was used not to kill enemies but to help others. Uncharacteristic of Jo Gul.

Im So-Byeong’s gaze grew sharper.

Perhaps Plum Blossom Island was too narrow a battlefield for them to fully display their skills. After all, how could mere numbers draw out all their abilities?

“You’ve created real monsters.”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes scanned the battlefield. The situation was still far from good. Even if a few died, it was just a drop in the bucket against the ant-like enemies.

Im So-Byeong’s gaze turned toward Desolate Gold Elder beyond.

‘You created a hell where those who rush in nervously die?’

Im So-Byeong gripped his fan tightly.

“Isn’t that the same for you?”

The one who moves first loses. He thought this was more a battle of guts than strategy. Im So-Byeong glanced back.

As expected.

‘That’s how it should be.’

Chung Myung had his eyes closed. At first glance, he seemed out of place on the battlefield, a peaceful look. But in fact, he was not resting but entering a meditative state.

Standing, he was circulating qi to prepare his body to fight again as quickly as possible.

In a battlefield where qi attacks and secret assassin techniques fly, to circulate qi standing? No sane person could do that.

It was impossible without absolute confidence that those surrounding him would block any attack.

“He’s crazy.”

Im So-Byeong smiled quietly and looked again toward Desolate Gold Elder.

Desolate Gold Elder was probably watching this scene too. Just knowing Chung Myung was circulating qi would cause unbearable mental pressure.

“Now, let’s see who has more guts.”

Im So-Byeong, with a steady smile, watched Desolate Gold Elder with a confident face.

Chapter 1072: Who Made The Mistake? (2)

The furrow between Desolate Gold Elder’s brows deepened noticeably.

“What is this?”

The situation was unfolding in a way he hadn’t anticipated.

Before setting this stage, he had thoroughly analyzed the sect called Mount Hua. Any sect with even a slight understanding of the murim world would recognize how significant a variable Mount Hua represented.

Because his analysis was so thorough, he believed he had set a perfect trap. In fact, they had fallen right into it. Moreover, they had preserved the elite forces of the Black Ghost Fortress and succeeded in encirclement, so he thought no other variables existed.

But now, Mount Hua’s response was completely deviating from what he had expected.

“Is this even possible?”

No way.

Every human has an unchanging disposition. The chooser believes with certainty that they have reached the right conclusion after deep thought, but in reality, most are led not by logic but by their inherent disposition.

The same applies to sects. The disposition of a sect does not change easily, just like that of a person. In fact, because many people gather to reach a single conclusion, the sect’s disposition is even more steadfast and difficult to change.

Yet now, Mount Hua was making choices completely opposite to the disposition they had shown so far.

Mount Hua was a sect that, when adversity struck, charged headlong into it. This had not changed at all, whether in the past Black Dragon Pirates or this time at Plum Blossom Island.

But suddenly, they planted their feet firmly and started to hold their ground?

“This is impossible!”

But isn’t that impossible thing happening right now? Then there must be a cause.

Desolate Gold Elder soon found the cause without much difficulty.

“The Green Forest King.”

Im So-Byeong was fluttering his fan and looking his way. Even from quite a distance, Desolate Gold Elder could clearly feel the unmistakable sneer in his eyes.

Gnashing his teeth harshly, he clenched his jaw.

Even amid the chaotic situation, he calmly grasped the opponent’s intentions and instantly found a perfect countermeasure. If anyone could do that there now, it would surely be Im So-Byeong.

Im So-Byeong was the one who, even against Jang Ilso’s Ten Thousand People Clan, concealed his actions as much as possible and prolonged the standoff. His personal martial arts skill might be insufficient as a variable, but even Desolate Gold Elder had to acknowledge his military strategy.

Then is the cause of this situation on Desolate Gold Elder, who knew Im So-Byeong was there but failed to prepare a countermeasure?

No one would dare say that.

Think about it. The Justice faction is a place tightly bound by pride in upholding agreements. Therefore, they always find even mentioning the Evil Sect disgusting.

Mount Hua is also worthy of the name of a prestigious sect. For such people to take orders from not just any Evil Sect member but one of the Evil Sect’s leaders, the Green Forest King? That is impossible.

From the start, inserting the Green Forest King, an Evil Sect origin, into a small elite group crossing into the south is incomprehensible to any sane person. Who could have guessed that Mount Hua’s disciples would follow Im So-Byeong’s orders?

“Damn it.”

Desolate Gold Elder bit his lip tightly.

“But now, it’s meaningless to argue logic.”

What was certain was that this absurd event was actually happening before his eyes.

“But it’s still okay.”

He still held the advantage. It’s true things had gotten complicated, but they likely lacked the capacity to break through this encirclement.

Even if a slow war of attrition continued, they would eventually fail and wither away.

However, one more variable was now looming over Desolate Gold Elder. He looked at that variable with a stern face.

“In this situation, to be able to...”

It was Chung Myung, standing in the middle of Mount Hua’s formation with his eyes closed.

Cultivating qi is fundamentally dangerous, as even minor shocks can cause possession by demons. Therefore, it must be done in a safe place.

But that madman was calmly cultivating qi right in the heart of a life-and-death battlefield. Even with nerves tougher than a whale’s tendon, no one could attempt that. Such people could only be called lunatics.

The problem was that this lunatic’s actions were tightening the noose around Desolate Gold Elder’s neck.

“...How long will it take?”

Complete recovery was impossible. Even the one cultivating qi wouldn’t expect that. But how long until he recovered enough to wield a sword and fight again?

“An hour? Or two?”

He couldn’t calculate it.

Desolate Gold Elder, glaring at Chung Myung with murderous eyes, flinched and opened his hand. His palm was already damp with sweat.

“...Am I being cornered? In a situation like this, no less?”

Desolate Gold Elder’s face began to contort more and more.

Chung Myung’s power was certainly threatening.

Even assuming full recovery was difficult, he was the one who defeated Dan Ja-Kang. Desolate Gold Elder knew well how monstrous Dan Ja-Kang was; his lost arm still ached at the thought of it.

If Chung Myung joined the battlefield with even partial strength restored, he could not guarantee his people surviving this encirclement.

“I must finish it before then.”

Desolate Gold Elder unknowingly stepped forward. It was an unconscious act, even though he clearly knew he should observe the situation from further back.

About to order a full offensive, Desolate Gold Elder forced back his words and bit his lip tightly.

“Press harder. Give them no breathing room!”

“Yes!”

Desolate Gold Elder absentmindedly tapped his thigh with his limp fingers. The movement looked like he was flicking abacus beads.

Across the fiercely contested battlefield, Im So-Byeong watched Desolate Gold Elder’s movements without missing a single one.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso’s gaze, fixed on Desolate Gold Elder, slowly shifted to Im So-Byeong.

Im So-Byeong, with the fan open, looked around with eyes chillingly cold and calm.

“The Green Forest King.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes briefly grew eerie.

Before the name Four Sects Alliance existed, the one Jang Ilso most feared was not the Black Dragon King, Desolate Gold Elder, or Thousand Faced Scholar, but the Green Forest King, Im So-Byeong.

Was it because the Green Forest was strong? Because Im So-Byeong was outstanding?

Not at all.

Because devouring them was the most urgent task.

To him, the Green Forest King was like a chess player playing without chariots or cannons. The Green Forest bandits were numerous but a half-baked sect lacking skilled warriors.

Moreover, the Green Forest couldn’t even properly utilize those numbers. Gathering the Green Forest disciples scattered across the mountains, supplying their consumption, and maintaining formation was impossible.

A place large in size but unable to concentrate power properly, lacking a main force. What more appetizing prey could there be?

But Im So-Byeong led that Green Forest and withstood the Ten Thousand People Clan’s assault.

Maintaining a long front line while avoiding direct clashes as much as possible, he used the lack of a main force as an advantage, hiding his and the Green Forest’s traces to prevent giving targets.

Im So-Byeong endlessly repeated this strategy-less tactic, minimizing Green Forest’s losses.

The more so, the firmer Jang Ilso’s resolve became: to eliminate the Green Forest as soon as possible, before Im So-Byeong gained a usable trump card.

But...

“I never imagined he would get that card from somewhere other than the Evil Sect.”

Though it may seem out of place, Im So-Byeong looking excited was not just Jang Ilso’s illusion. He, who always played to maximize numerical advantage, could now do what he wanted from the opposite position for the first time.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso looked at Im So-Byeong with a strange gaze.

“Moreover...”

Having taken in Im So-Byeong’s response and Mount Hua’s formation at a glance, Jang Ilso twisted a smile. The revealed one was as threatening as a beast’s fangs.

“You read people well. Still, as always.”

Jang Ilso murmured softly and spoke to Ho Ga-Myung standing beside him.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance lord.”

“We need to change the plan.”

Ho Ga-Myung looked back at Jang Ilso with questioning eyes.

“We need to coordinate the rhythm a bit.”

Seeing the sly smile on Jang Ilso’s face, Ho Ga-Myung’s gaze momentarily turned toward Mount Hua.

“Well... that can’t be imitated.”

Those biting the enemies. If they stayed put like Mount Hua’s disciples now, they couldn’t exert their strength. That’s only possible for Mount Hua. But even in this situation, there was a way to fully unleash the Red Dog’s power.

“We need to change the biters a bit. Deal with those targeting Mount Hua first.”

“Then Alliance lord will be in danger.”

“Tsk.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue in displeasure. This was why, despite trusting Ho Ga-Myung greatly, he could not entrust him with full authority.

“To catch a big fish like Desolate Gold Elder, I must offer my life as bait, shouldn’t I?”

“But...”

Ho Ga-Myung glanced at Jang Ilso’s expression and finally nodded.

“Understood, Alliance Lord.”

As soon as Ho Ga-Myung gave the order, the Red Dog’s gaze turned simultaneously. The reaction was chillingly fast and perfect.

Jang Ilso’s eyes traced a strange arc.

“Now, Desolate Gold Elder...”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

“Did you calculate this situation?”

Can you flick those proud abacus beads and dominate here?

One who sees people only as numbers.
